*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双无人机空中对接平台，采用主从编队和被动磁锁紧机构，实现了小型四旋翼无人机在推力有限条件下的可靠空中对接，并提供了完整的软硬件栈和定量评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机之间的可靠空中对接是实现模块化空中协作与操作的关键，但现有方法在推力、载荷受限条件下难以同时满足精确相对位姿控制和可重复对接的需求。

Method: 采用主从编队控制，设计轻量化模块化机架配合被动磁锁紧机构；提出进度感知任务管理器管理四个阶段（接近、对准、捕获、稳定）；基于ROS 2集成Crazyflie/PX4接口，实现同步日志记录用于基准评估。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，通过编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接时间及故障模式统计等指标对平台进行了定量评估。

Conclusion: 该平台为对接监督与同步策略提供了统计意义上的比较基础，为模块化空中协作和可重复空中操作提供了实用测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [49] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA是一个即插即用的框架，通过多视图一致性约束、实例估计模块和掩码自监督3D编码，为预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型注入3D场景理解能力，显著提升了机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型缺乏3D场景理解能力，表现为三个相互关联的挑战：无法强制执行多视图一致性的3D空间位置提取薄弱、3D实例理解不足、以及遮挡下的推理脆弱。虽然存在成熟的3D感知方法，但直接集成到VLA管线中受到架构不兼容和对昂贵实例级标注的严重依赖的阻碍。

Method: (1) 跨模态的显式多视图一致性约束3D特征编码与空间条件几何聚合方法；(2) 使用高层级实例令牌的实例估计模块实现3D实例感知；(3) 保留预测器进行视觉令牌补全的掩码自监督3D编码分支以处理遮挡。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上，3DVLA集成多个VLA基线后，在操作性能上取得了一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 3DVLA是一个即插即用、无需额外人工标注且不丢弃VLM先验的框架，能够有效增强预训练VLA的3D推理能力，验证了其有效性和兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [50] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA是一种即插即用的相位自适应推理框架，通过动态计算调度（视觉编码器、LLM、动作头联合分配）加速VLA模型，在不修改基础模型的前提下实现最高3.77倍加速，并将控制频率从13.8 Hz提升至26.3 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率低，难以满足实时机器人操作需求；已有的加速方法通常优化单个组件或依赖固定加速规则，忽略了序列化具身控制中不同控制步骤的非均匀推理需求。

Method: 提出ElegantVLA框架，包含一个轻量级调度器，通过观测时序表示相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进度，联合分配视觉编码器、LLM和动作头的计算资源。视觉-语言推理部分采用五级计算模式（从完全重计算到多步时序复用），动作生成部分采用三级去噪模式（稳定运动时复用中间去噪状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整精化）。

Result: 在GR00T和CogACT上分别实现最高2.55倍和3.77倍加速；在6个真实世界GR00T任务中，计算量减少2.18倍，控制频率从13.8 Hz提升至26.3 Hz。

Conclusion: 受人类运动控制启发，VLA模型应学习何时投入完整计算、何时复用先前的计算结果。ElegantVLA作为一个通用加速框架，无需修改或重新训练基础模型即可显著提升VLA管线的推理效率和控制频率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [51] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种人在回路中的强化学习框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏方法，将有视觉的教师策略知识迁移到仅依赖本体感知（位姿、速度、力矩）的无视觉学生策略，在真实世界的NIST装配基准任务上实现了95%的成功率，并展现出对未见任务变体的鲁棒泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉信息能加速接触丰富的机器人操作学习，但视觉策略容易过拟合训练时的视觉条件，导致鲁棒性和泛化性受限。现有方案依赖域随机化或数据增强，而本文希望在不使用这些技术的情况下，在真实世界中训练出鲁棒且泛化的策略。

Method: 采用人在回路中的强化学习框架，结合教师-学生蒸馏：先训练一个基于视觉的教师策略，再将其知识蒸馏到仅依赖本体感知（位姿、角速度、力矩）的学生策略中。整个过程在真实世界中进行，无需域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在真实NIST装配基准板上，3个代表性任务经过约50分钟训练后达到95%总体成功率，并能鲁棒泛化到8个未见任务变体。通过蒸馏微调，在最具挑战性的任务上实现了完全成功。

Conclusion: 提出的方法在鲁棒性和适应性上均优于基线，证明了通过蒸馏将视觉知识压缩到本体感知策略中，可以在保持快速训练的同时获得强大的任务泛化能力。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [52] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解释的计算框架，利用多感官触觉数据建模人类材料感知与识别。该框架包含三个互连模型：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性，模型2基于感知表征进行材料分类，模型3直接从触觉特征分类材料。研究发现整合按压、静态接触和滑动交互信息可提高预测准确性，热觉线索对感知建模和材料分类尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对材料的触觉感知依赖复杂的多感官触觉线索，但低层次触觉信号与感知表征之间的关系尚不明确，这一知识鸿沟阻碍了触觉在数字环境中的集成以及具备类人触觉感知的机器人开发。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个互连模型的可解释计算框架：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性；模型2基于感知表征对材料进行分类；模型3直接从触觉特征进行材料分类。

Result: 整合按压、静态接触和滑动三种交互模式的信息能提升预测准确性；热觉线索在感知建模和材料分类中信息量最大。

Conclusion: 热觉和顺应性（compliance）线索在当前的机器人手指和触觉显示器中代表性不足，融入这些线索可提升人工系统逼近人类材料感知的能力，并为设计更具感知基础的人机触觉界面提供指导。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [53] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-Seg通过风格扩展和纹理正则化，解决了越野环境下语义分割因分布偏移（域差异和传感器损坏）导致预测不准确的问题，在多种分布偏移目标域上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境中的语义分割面临源-目标域差异和传感器因粗糙地形损坏导致的分布偏移，使得模型在训练条件之外的场景下预测不准确，影响导航任务。

Method: 提出ST-Seg框架，包含风格扩展（Style Expansion, SE）生成多样化真实风格以扩宽域覆盖，以及纹理正则化（Texture Regularization, TR）通过深度纹理流形稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表示。

Result: 在多种分布偏移的目标域上，ST-Seg相比现有方法取得显著性能提升，证明了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ST-Seg增强了语义分割在越野导航中的实际应用能力，有效应对分布偏移问题。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [54] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAConf，一种基于单类判别式置信度框架，利用冻结的VLA内部表示直接估计逐步异常分数，无需重复采样，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人实验中显著提升了置信度信号质量和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型置信度估计方法存在计算效率低（需要重复采样）和跨架构泛化性差（仅适用于离散动作空间）的问题，难以在开放世界机器人操作任务中高效、通用地提供风险敏感决策所需的置信度信号。

Method: 提出VLAConf，一种单类判别式置信度框架：1）利用冻结的预训练VLA内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步异常分数；2）采用步条件建模编码轨迹中的 rollout 阶段信息，避免穷举重采样带来的计算开销。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，VLAConf构建的后验校准置信度信号质量显著优于现有基线，推理效率大幅提升；真实机器人实验进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf通过单次前向传播和轻量级置信度头，高效解决了VLA模型置信度估计中的计算效率和泛化性问题，为开放世界机器人操作中的风险敏感决策提供了可靠支持。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [55] [EXACT-MPPI: Exact Signed-Distance Navigation for Arbitrary-Footprint Robots from Point Clouds via Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29663)
*Chen Peng,Zhikang Ge,Wenwu Lu,Haiming Gao,Stavros Vougioukas,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EXACT-MPPI是一个无需训练的局部导航框架，通过将精确符号距离评估器嵌入MPPI控制器，直接利用点云观测和稀疏引导生成运动指令，避免了中间地图表示和凸近似，支持任意凸/凹多边形足迹，并在多种机器人平台上实现实时避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面机器人携带的非凸复杂有效足迹导致传统局部导航方法（凸近似、栅格化）在狭窄环境中消除可行运动，需要一种能精确处理真实足迹几何的导航方法。

Method: 将精确符号距离评估器嵌入MPPI控制器；足迹表示为简单多边形（通用）或矩形覆盖（快速评估）；在每次MPPI rollout中将观测障碍点转换到预测本体坐标系并与足迹进行精确距离评估；所有操作在JAX中批量化并使用GPU并行加速。

Result: 加速了批量化距离评估；在凸足迹规划器失败的场景中保持了可行运动；在密集静态和动态障碍物下保持鲁棒性；无需逐平台训练即可部署于差速、阿克曼、全向和混合模式平台。

Conclusion: 将精确足迹几何与采样预测控制相结合，为多种机器人提供了一种实用、无需训练的足迹感知局部导航方案。

Abstract: Ground robots often carry payloads, implements, or other attachments that turn their effective footprint into complex, non-convex shapes. Navigating safely through clutter then requires reasoning about this true geometry, yet most local planners simplify it with convex or inflated proxies and rasterize sensor data into occupancy grids or distance fields. Both choices eliminate feasible motions when clearance is comparable to the footprint geometry. We present EXACT-MPPI, a training-free local navigation framework that maps local point-cloud observations and sparse guidance directly to motion commands, without any intermediate map representation. The framework embeds an analytic, exact signed-distance evaluator into a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The footprint is represented as a simple polygon for general convex or concave planar shapes, with a rectangle-cover specialization for faster evaluation of rectilinear footprints, enabling footprint-aware collision costs without convex decomposition, inflation, or learned encoders. During each MPPI rollout, observed obstacle points are transformed into the predicted body frame and evaluated against the footprint. All operations are batched in JAX, leveraging GPU parallelism for real-time receding-horizon control. Experiments show that EXACT-MPPI accelerates batched distance evaluation over a learned point-to-robot baseline, preserves feasible motion where convex-footprint planners fail, and remains robust under dense static and moving obstacles. The same framework deploys on differential-drive, Ackermann, omnidirectional, and hybrid-mode platforms by changing only the footprint description and motion model without per-platform training. Pairing exact footprint geometry with sampling-based predictive control thus offers a practical, training-free path to footprint-aware local navigation across diverse robots.

</details>


### [56] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhAIL（物理AI排行榜），一个基于Franka FR3机器人的开放真实机器人基准测试平台。该平台采用分布式评估方法论：以成功时间累积分布函数（CDF）作为评估基元，引入人类相对吞吐量（HRT）标量评分（带bootstrap置信区间）和Kolmogorov-Smirnov显著性检验。在四个公开VLA策略上的实验表明，KS检验能在N≤30次 rollout内分辨出二值阈值指标无法区分的相近对比，最佳VLA策略比人类参考慢约7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA策略的真实机器人评估仍基于固定超时下的二值成功率，且通常使用N≤25次rollout的小样本量，缺乏置信区间和配对统计比较，导致难以可靠分辨相近策略的性能差异。

Method: 提出PhAIL基准平台，采用基于时间-成功累积分布函数（CDF）的评估方法。主要包括：（1）人类相对吞吐量（HRT）评分——以同一设备人类遥操作为锚点的无量纲标量，附带bootstrap置信区间；（2）Kolmogorov-Smirnov显著性检验——按对象计算后宏平均。

Result: 在四个公开VLA上，宏平均KS检验在N≤30次rollout下成功分辨了GR00T vs. ACT和OpenPI vs. ACT这两组二值指标无法区分的对比；OpenPI vs. GR00T这一最接近的对组在预算内无法分辨。最佳VLA相较人类参考慢约7倍（RMST比值）。

Conclusion: PhAIL提出的基于CDF的评估方法（HRT评分+KS检验）在小样本量下比传统二值成功率指标具有更高的统计分辨率，为VLA策略的可靠比较提供了新范式。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [57] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARS策略通过自适应地在多模态阶段注入噪声、单模态阶段采用确定性学习，在保持生成策略多模态能力的同时显著提升训练与推理效率，实验显示成功率提升16.67%、推理延迟降低83.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式策略依赖随机噪声初始化和迭代去噪以实现多模态行为，导致训练复杂度高、推理效率低；然而并非机器人任务的所有阶段都需要行为多样性，因此作者提出仅在真正需要多样性的阶段自适应地引入随机性。

Method: 提出Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS)策略，自适应地在需要多模态行为的阶段激活生成式噪声注入，在单模态阶段切换为高效的确定性学习，实现"在正确的时间注入适量的噪声"。

Result: 在8个仿真和4个真实世界任务中，MARS在真实测试中成功率提升16.67%，推理延迟降低83.20%。反直觉的是，在近确定性的任务上，MARS通过更有效地建模细微动作多样性，训练效率甚至超过确定性策略。

Conclusion: MARS弥合了生成式策略的多模态能力与确定性模型高效训练推理之间的差距，为机器人操作任务提供了一种效率与表现兼顾的解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [58] [Joint Angle Estimation with Customized Wristband Based on Online Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29771)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaobin Chen,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于在线增量学习的定制腕带系统，用于估计腕关节角度。该方法采用两阶段策略：第一阶段利用IMU实时数据通过在线学习更新模型；第二阶段仅使用腕带进行角度估计。实验表明该方法能适应不同测试配置（左右手、佩戴位置、不同受试者）带来的数据漂移，误差约15度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感器研究应用过于简单，难以适应不同情境下的数据漂移问题（如左右手腕差异、佩戴位置偏差、受试者个体差异等），缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出两阶段在线增量学习方法：第一阶段利用IMU作为地面真值，基于佩戴者手腕运动特征进行在线学习更新模型；第二阶段仅依靠腕带传感器进行腕关节角度估计。模型训练与数据采集同步完成。

Result: 传感器在应变变化下表现良好，系统在不同场景下腕关节轨迹估计误差约为15度。

Conclusion: 该在线增量学习方法能有效适应数据漂移，提高可穿戴传感器在不同配置下的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Intelligent wearable technology plays an increasingly important role in human-computer interaction, motion, and health monitoring. To ensure comfort and practicality of use, one common form for motion monitoring is to utilize soft wearable sensors. However, many research applications regarding wearable sensors are simplistic and difficult to adapt to different situations. This study proposes a system for estimating the angle of the wrist joint using a customized wristband based on an online incremental learning approach. It is a two-stage estimation method: the first stage updates the model based on the wearer's wrist movement characteristics using online learning, integrating real-time data from an IMU as ground truth. The second stage utilizes the updated model for estimation of wrist joint angle solely with the wristband. In other words, model training is completed during data acquisition, allowing the trained model to be used for subsequent angle estimation. This method offers advantages in adapting to data drift caused by variations in different testing configurations, such as the left and right wrists of the same subject, deviations in the wearing position on the same wrist, and even differences among various subjects. The results indicate that the sensors exhibit good performance under strain variations, and the wrist joint trajectory estimation of the proposed system has an approximate error of 15 degree in different scenarios.

</details>


### [59] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出未来体验条件化（FEC）方法，通过将闭环策略条件化于短时未来视频的潜在表征，为多步机器人操作提供结构化先验。在模拟环境中，未来片段通过LLM推理器、数字孪生滚动和视频扩散模型三阶段生成。实验表明，生成式未来可提升BC及BC+RL性能，且BC+RL取得最优结果；正确未来加速学习，错误未来则使性能归零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步机器人操作需要在场景演化不确定下进行探索和策略适应，现有方法缺乏有效的结构化先验来引导探索和策略微调。

Method: 提出Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC)框架：1）LLM推理器基于任务本体和当前场景状态进行推理；2）无机器人数字孪生滚动模拟预期物体运动；3）无掩码视频扩散模型合成与机器人一致的未来片段。将未来片段的潜在表征作为闭环策略的额外条件输入，主要结合行为克隆（BC）和BC+强化学习（RL）进行实例化。

Result: 在RoboCasa和CALVIN基准上，生成式未来优于无未来条件化；错误未来导致性能下降。BC+RL取得最强总体结果。在8个CALVIN任务的学习曲线分析中，GTFuture学习最快，GenFuture比NoFuture更早达到更高水平，WrongFuture全程保持零性能。

Conclusion: 短时未来视频可作为探索和策略适应的有用结构化先验，即使在未来预测不完美的情况下也能提升性能，但错误未来会对策略产生负面影响。

Abstract: Multi-step robot manipulation requires acting under uncertainty about how the scene will evolve, making exploration and policy adaptation challenging. We study whether short-horizon, task-consistent future videos can provide useful structured priors for control and reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. We formalize this idea through Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), a simple interface that conditions closed-loop policies on a latent representation of a short future video. In our simulation setup, future clips are generated in three stages, an LLM reasoner operating over a task ontology initialized from the current scene state, a robot-free digital-twin rollout of the intended object motion, and a mask-free video diffusion model that synthesizes a robot-consistent future clip without requiring segmentation at inference. We instantiate this future-conditioning interface primarily with BC and BC+RL, and compare against a future-conditioned Streaming Flow Policy (SFP) baseline on RoboCasa and CALVIN under NoFuture, GTFuture, GenFuture, and WrongFuture. Generated futures improve performance over no-future conditioning, while mismatched futures degrade it, and our BC+RL instantiation achieves the strongest overall results. An average BC+RL learning-curve analysis across 8 CALVIN tasks further shows that GTFuture improves fastest, GenFuture improves earlier and to a higher level than NoFuture, and WrongFuture remains at zero throughout training. These results suggest that short-horizon future videos can serve as useful structured priors for exploration and policy adaptation under imperfect future predictions. https://enact2026.github.io/

</details>


### [60] [Fisher-Preserving Guidance: Training-Free Manifold Constraints for Safe Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29937)
*Hao Ren,Zetong Bi,Yiming Zeng,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Zhaoliang Wan,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过低秩Jacobian分解计算Fisher保持更新，并引入Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity作为不确定性信号，在视觉导航的路径点预测中显著提升扩散策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉导航中用于路径点预测时，标准采样和测试时引导会产生不可靠或低效的轨迹，因为更新会偏离训练流形。需要一种无需额外训练的方法来避免大Fisher漂移，同时优化任务目标。

Method: 提出Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection，通过低秩Jacobian分解计算Fisher保持更新（每步仅需一次反向传播），并引入Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity作为不确定性信号，实现鲁棒的多样本动作融合。

Result: 在玩具和真实导航基准（包括Maze2D、PushT、仿真及真实机器人视觉导航）上，相比强扩散策略基线，性能一致提升，无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 所提方法是一种训练免费、实时可用的推理方法，能有效避免扩散模型在导航中的流形漂移问题，提升轨迹可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models are effective for waypoint prediction in visual navigation, but standard sampling and test time guidance can produce unreliable or inefficient trajectories when updates drift off the training manifold. We propose Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection, a training-free inference method that avoids large Fisher drift associated with off-distribution actions while optimizing a task objective. Our method computes the Fisher-preserving update via a low-rank Jacobian factorization, requiring only a single backward pass per step and enabling real-time use. We further introduce Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity as an uncertainty signal and use it for robust multi-sample action blending. Experiments on toy and realistic navigation benchmarks, including Maze2D with TSDF-based guidance, PushT with official Diffusion Policy weights, and visual navigation in simulation and on real robots, demonstrate consistent improvements in performance over strong diffusion-policy baselines without additional training.

</details>


### [61] [Replicable Simulation-Based Robot Validation through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973)
*Argentina Ortega,Samuel Wiest,Frederik Pasch,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将数据溯源（Provenance）与FAIR原则（可发现、可访问、可互操作、可复用）集成到基于仿真的机器人测试流程中，通过追踪工件间的链接并附加机器可读元数据来解决测试可复现性问题。作者在现有仿真测试框架中增加了溯源追踪与元数据收集机制，并将其应用于移动机器人导航数据集，最后讨论了词汇对齐、属性选择等集成障碍并给出了建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于仿真的机器人测试面临可复现性不足的问题，因为测试的配置、执行和后处理过程缺乏透明的文档化。现有方法往往将溯源和元数据视为事后工作，仅局限于最终数据集，导致无法端到端地重建实验证据。

Method: 1) 提出将数据溯源与FAIR原则嵌入测试流程，而非事后附加；2) 在现有仿真测试框架中增加溯源追踪和元数据收集机制；3) 将该机制应用于移动机器人导航数据集，生成带有结构化溯源和FAIR对齐元数据的增强数据。

Result: 成功在仿真测试框架中集成了溯源追踪与元数据收集功能，并产出富含结构化溯源和FAIR元数据的机器人导航数据集。

Conclusion: 溯源与FAIR元数据必须内建到生成数据的测试流程中，而非事后补充；集成过程中面临词汇对齐、属性选择、领域标准采纳等挑战；提供了在机器人验证流程中实施以溯源为中心的FAIR元数据的可操作性建议。

Abstract: Robot behavior is often validated through simulation-based testing, yet the replicability of such campaigns depends critically on transparent documentation of how tests are configured, executed, and post-processed. We argue that data provenance, coupled with the FAIR principles (findability, accessibility, interoperability, and reusability), addresses this gap by explicitly tracking links between artifacts and by attaching machine-readable metadata about file origins and key design decisions. Moreover, provenance and metadata cannot be treated as an afterthought confined to final datasets; they must be integrated into the testing processes that generate those datasets so that evidence can be reconstructed end-to-end. We demonstrate this by augmenting an existing simulation-based testing framework with provenance tracking and metadata collection mechanisms, and by using these extensions to enrich a mobile robot navigation dataset with structured provenance and FAIR-aligned metadata. Finally, we discuss obstacles encountered in this integration -- such as vocabulary alignment, attribute selection, and adoption of domain standards -- and provide actionable recommendations for implementing provenance-centric, FAIR metadata in robotics validation workflows.

</details>


### [62] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yifan Han,Yanming Shao,Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BORA是一个面向真实世界灵巧操作的离线到在线RL后训练框架，通过冻结VLA基础模型并引入基于VLM认知token和动作块的批判者（critic）以及人在回路的块级残差自适应机制，显著提升了灵巧操作策略的性能，在五个复杂任务上平均成功率绝对提升33%，未见物体泛化提升43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在灵巧操作中面临高维手部控制和累积执行错误的挑战，真实世界的RL后训练对弥合视觉引导动作生成与物理可靠执行之间的差距至关重要，但高维灵巧探索会导致时序不一致、样本效率低下和硬件风险。

Method: BORA采用离线到在线的两阶段框架：离线阶段构建同时接收VLM认知token和动作块的批判者，实现动作条件价值引导；在线阶段冻结VLA基础模型，引入轻量级人在回路（HiL）块级残差自适应机制，结合离线继承的批判者和干预驱动奖励来纠正执行偏差。

Result: 在五个复杂真实世界灵巧操作任务上，BORA在标准设置下平均成功率绝对提升33%，在未见物体泛化场景下提升43%，显著优于纯模仿学习和传统解耦RL基线方法。

Conclusion: BORA通过离线批判者学习与在线残差自适应相结合的方式，有效解决了VLA模型在灵巧操作中的高维探索和执行误差累积问题，为真实世界灵巧VLA模型的RL后训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>


### [63] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Jinhui Ye,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Jie Zhang,Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Pei Lin,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Shuai Bai,Jingren Zhou,Jiazhao Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Ye Wang,Yiyang Huang,Zixing Lei,Wujian Peng,Delin Chen,Yingming Zheng,Jingyang Fan,Xianwei Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Haoyang Li,Anzhe Chen,Tong Zhang,Xuejing Liu,Yuchong Sun,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Chenxu Lü,Zhibo Yang,Tao Yu,Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA是一个统一的具身基础模型，通过视觉-语言-动作建模将操作、导航和轨迹预测等异构决策问题整合到单一框架中。该模型扩展了Qwen的视觉-语言堆栈，通过DiT动作解码器支持连续动作生成，并采用具身感知提示调节适配多种机器人平台。在大规模多源数据上联合预训练后，Qwen-VLA在多个操作、导航和轨迹基准上实现了一致的多任务性能和跨场景、跨具身的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能研究通常为操作、导航等单一任务设计专用模型，导致能力碎片化且在任务、环境和机器人形态间泛化能力有限。该工作旨在探索是否可以将异构的具身决策问题统一到单个视觉-语言-动作模型中。

Method: 提出Qwen-VLA，扩展Qwen的视觉-语言建模堆栈，引入基于DiT的动作解码器实现连续动作和轨迹生成。采用大规模联合预训练策略，整合机器人操作轨迹、人类第一人称演示、合成仿真数据、视觉-语言导航数据等多种数据源。引入具身感知提示调节，使用机器人特定文本描述指定当前具身形态和控制约定。将操作、导航和轨迹预测统一为动作-轨迹预测框架。

Result: 在多个基准上取得优异结果：LIBERO 97.9%，Simpler-WidowX 73.7%，RoboTwin-Easy/Hard 86.1%/87.2%，R2R OSR 69.0%，RxR SR 59.6%，真实ALOHA实验平均OOD成功率76.9%，DOMINO动态操作零样本成功率26.6%。

Conclusion: 研究表明异构具身决策问题可以统一在单个视觉-语言-动作框架中，Qwen-VLA通过大规模多源预训练和具身感知设计实现了跨任务、跨环境和跨机器人形态的泛化能力，验证了单一基础模型解决多样具身任务的可行性。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


### [64] [Gaze2Act: Gaze-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Interactive Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30282)
*Kuangji Zuo,Gen Li,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Boyu Ma,Shijia Han,Xinyu Zhou,Xichen Yuan,Chuhao Zhou,Jiaqi Bai,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gaze2Act框架，利用人类注视作为动态直观的意图信号，通过跨视角语义匹配将第一人称注视映射到机器人视角，实现粗到细的目标指定，并在Unitree G1人形机器人上16个真实任务中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言指令不足以精确传达人类意图，尤其在区分相似物体、指定操作位置及应对目标动态变化时存在局限，需要更直观的交互信号。

Method: 通过跨视角语义匹配桥接第一人称与机器人视角的差距，将注视映射为物体掩码和注视点实现粗到细的目标指定；通过感知层提示和行为层条件化将注视线索集成到策略中。

Result: 在7类任务、16个真实机器人任务上，Gaze2Act在意图准确率和任务成功率均达到SOTA，显著优于基线方法，在物体区分、精细交互和动态意图引导方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 人类注视是一种自然、低负担且高表达力的意图信号，适用于人在回路的VLA控制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown strong potential for robot learning by following language instructions. However, in practice, language alone is often insufficient to precisely convey human intent. It is difficult to describe which exact object to interact with among similar candidates, where to act on the object, or how the target may change during execution. To address this limitation, we propose Gaze2Act, a novel VLA framework that leverages human gaze as a dynamic and intuitive intent signal for complex interactive manipulation. Gaze2Act first bridges the ego-exo view gap by mapping first-person gaze into the robot's perspective through cross-view semantic matching, producing both an object mask and a gaze point for coarse-to-fine target specification. These cues are then integrated into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning, allowing the robot to attend to relevant regions and execute precise interactions under dynamic intent. In a systematic evaluation across seven task categories and 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate. It notably outperforms baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering. These results demonstrate that human gaze provides a natural, low-burden, and highly expressive modality for human-in-the-loop VLA control.

</details>


### [65] [A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30313)
*Yufei Jia,Zhanxiang Cao,Mingrui Yu,Heng Zhang,Shenyu Chen,Dixuan Jiang,Meng Li,Xiaofan Li,Yiyang Liu,Junzhe Wu,Zheng Li,XiLin Fang,Tingyu Cui,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Anqi Wang,Zifan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Chenyu Cao,Zhenbiao Huang,Ziang Zheng,Jie Lu,Xin Ma,Zhengyang Wei,Xiang Zhao,Tianyue Zhan,Ye He,Yuxiang Chen,Yizhou Jiang,Yue Li,Haizhou Ge,Yuhang Dong,Fan Jia,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiwa Deng,Zhixing Chen,Hanyang Shao,Chenxin Dong,Yixuan Li,Yizhi Chen,Bokui Chen,Kaifeng Zhang,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Xiang Li,Yue Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLab是一种异构CPU仿真/GPU学习架构，通过统一运行时解耦CPU并行仿真与GPU策略更新，在相同硬件配置下将端到端训练效率提升3-10倍，并支持跨平台执行（macOS、AMD ROCm、Intel XPU），证明GPU仿真并非高效训练的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于仿真的机器人强化学习默认假设高效训练需要将物理引擎置于GPU上，这一假设限制了系统架构选择。作者希望重新审视这一假设，探索CPU仿真与GPU学习异构架构的可能性。

Method: 提出UniLab异构架构，采用CPU并行仿真（MuJoCoUni和MotrixSim后端）与GPU策略学习（PPO、SAC、FlashSAC、TD3、APPO）解耦的方式，通过统一运行时管理数据移动、缓冲和同步，形成高效的端到端训练循环。

Result: 在代表性仿真机器人控制任务上，相同硬件配置下端到端训练效率提升3-10倍；降低对NVIDIA CUDA软件栈的依赖；支持Apple macOS、AMD ROCm和Intel XPU等跨平台执行。

Conclusion: GPU仿真是实现高效训练的有效路径，但并非必要路径。UniLab拓宽了机器人强化学习训练的实际系统选择范围。

Abstract: Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.

</details>


### [66] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Fenghao Yu,Zhehuan Chen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yejin Choi,David Held,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWits是一个双手机器人基准测试，通过自动化任务生成管道构建推理为中心的意外场景，评估机器人在几何、材料和装配推理方面的认知能力。实验表明，预训练VLA在种子任务上初步成功，但在变异任务中表现脆弱，缺乏推理和策略适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试主要关注技能执行层次，缺乏对机器人在意外挑战下进行推理、适应和创造性问题解决等认知推理能力的系统评估。

Method: 提出自动化任务生成管道，采用多智能体协作框架，包括种子任务生成与验证、度量生成、场景生成和任务变异四个智能体；基于此构建了30个种子任务和208个变异任务，涵盖几何、材料和装配三个推理维度。

Result: 预训练VLA在种子任务上经单任务微调后表现初步成功，但在变异任务上表现困难，暴露出在需要推理、策略适应和对欺骗性或受限环境鲁棒性的操作任务中的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 当前机器人策略在认知推理和意外场景适应方面存在显著差距，RoboWits基准测试为系统评估和推动机器人认知推理能力发展提供了有效平台。

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.

</details>


### [67] [DynaFLIP: Rethinking Robotics Perception via Tri-Modal-Dynamics Guided Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30350)
*Jusuk Lee,Seungjae Lee,Jonghun Shin,Hoseong Jung,Sungha Kim,Daesol Cho,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaFLIP是一种面向机器人操作的动力学感知多模态预训练框架，通过构建图像-语言-3D光流三元组，并采用单纯形体积最小化结合余弦正则化和对比学习的方法，将运动理解融入视觉表征学习，在仿真和真实场景中实现最高+22.5%的泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作流水线依赖为静态识别或视觉-语言对齐预训练的视觉编码器，忽略了场景中的运动动力学信息，导致下游策略需要从头学习运动理解，制约了泛化能力。

Method: 构建图像-语言-3D光流三元组作为训练监督，通过最小化三种模态在共享超球面空间中的单纯形体积实现对齐，同时引入余弦正则化避免几何歧义，并联合对比学习目标防止坍缩。

Result: DynaFLIP在学习到的表征聚焦于与操作控制相关的区域，在多种仿真与真实设置中持续超越基线方法，在分布外场景下最高提升22.5%。

Conclusion: 机器人泛化能力可通过训练视觉表征编码"世界在动作下如何变化"而非仅编码"存在什么"来显著提升。

Abstract: Robot manipulation critically depends on perception that preserves the action-relevant aspects of a scene. Yet most robot learning pipelines are built upon visual encoders pre-trained for static recognition or vision-language alignment, leaving motion understanding to downstream policies. We introduce DynaFLIP, a dynamics-aware multimodal pre-training framework that pushes motion understanding upstream into perception. We construct image-language-3D flow triplets from heterogeneous human and robot videos, and use these triplets as training-time supervision to shape an image-only encoder. Our key idea is to encourage the three modalities to span a small simplex volume in the shared hyperspherical space -- a smaller simplex volume indicating stronger alignment. To avoid the geometric ambiguity and trivial collapse of naive volume minimization, we combine simplex-volume minimization with a cosine regularizer and a contrastive objective. Our analyses show that DynaFLIP focuses on control-relevant regions critical for manipulation. The resulting dynamics-aware representations serve as reusable visual backbones and consistently outperform baselines across diverse downstream policies, including VLAs. We validate this across diverse simulation and real-world setups, with gains reaching +22.5% under out-of-distribution scenarios. Our results suggest that robot generalization improves when visual representations are trained to encode not just what is present, but how the world changes under action.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [68] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法虽然修改不同的具体权重，但都作用于一个共同的功能子空间，通过抑制（而非覆盖）知识来实现编辑。训练一个二元掩码可逆转80%的编辑效果，证明该机制普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）主要通过输出行为评估，但其内部机制尚未被充分理解。作者希望探究这些方法是否共享一个共同的工作机制，不论修改的是哪个具体事实。

Method: 在编辑后的权重上训练一个紧凑的二元掩码（binary mask）来逆转编辑效果，通过分析掩码的影响（特别是消除后层注意力过集中）来揭示共同机制；同时通过注入掩码观察编辑成功率下降来验证机制的必要性。

Result: 该掩码能在训练集上逆转80%的编辑，测试集上逆转超过70%；注入掩码使编辑成功率从98%降至38%；分析表明掩码通过消除后层过集中注意力来逆转编辑。

Conclusion: ROME和MEMIT通过抑制（而非覆盖）知识来编辑事实，这解释了为何它们无法将改动传播到相关事实。所发现的共同功能子空间可用于检测和防御未经授权的编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [69] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动态，通过TradeArena测试平台发现：规划嵌入漂移、有效秩收缩等可测量的失败前兆信号；理性级收缩依赖于推理过程，而意图级收缩则持续存在；结构化风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号但非普适性能增强器；51只股票日内实验揭示相关性盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在金融决策中是否存在可测量的失败前兆信号，以及风险反馈能否作为对齐信号，从而理解LLM金融推理何时在对齐、漂移或失败。

Method: 基于TradeArena交易智能体测试平台（含风险报告、执行模拟、记忆和可回放轨迹），分析LLM在市场压力下的理性、持仓和干预的演化；使用80个滚动失败锚点、多种嵌入方式（hash/LSA/Transformer/白盒隐藏状态）进行流形诊断；进行压力测试（消除CoT、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计报告）；51只股票日内实验结合滚动Markowitz基线分析相关性盲点。

Result: 存在可测量的失败前兆：规划嵌入漂移、融合计划-风险表征可区分正常与预回撤状态、有效秩收缩；理性级收缩在无推理时消失而意图级收缩仍在；结构化风险反馈可改善部分模型校准或收益/回撤，但存在短视收益高而对齐诊断弱的情况；存在相关性盲点——LLM理性常为集中暴露于耦合资产提供理由。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈和表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时在对齐、漂移或失败——支持研究主张而非盈利主张。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [70] [Access Sets Matter: Budgeting Expert Reads for Scalable Weight-Space Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29489)
*Yuanyi Wang,Yanggan Gu,Su Lu,Yifan Yang,Zhaoyi Yan,Congkai Xie,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MergePipe是一个预算感知的执行层，将大模型合并问题转化为专家访问集问题，通过选择性读取专家delta块，在有限I/O预算下实现模型合并，可减少高达一个数量级的专家读取I/O并实现11倍加速，同时保持与完整读取合并相当的参数精度和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型尺度下，权重空间模型合并的主要瓶颈并非计算操作本身，而是必须读取的专家权重集合所带来的巨大I/O开销。现有方法缺乏对读取预算的显式控制。

Method: MergePipe将LLM合并建模为专家访问集问题：在共享权重坐标系下，给定合并算子和检查点族，在显式I/O预算约束下选择需要访问的专家delta块。通过索引参数块、构建确定性访问计划、生成可重放的执行清单来执行预算受限的合并。预算计划在构造上保证预算合理性，满预算时恢复为完整读取合并。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama合并任务中，MergePipe将专家读取I/O减少最多一个数量级，实现最高11倍加速。预算扫描显示参数偏差在O(10^{-3})量级，下游基准测试无单调退化。

Conclusion: 通过将合并问题重新定义为预算约束下的访问集选择问题，MergePipe在不牺牲模型质量的前提下显著降低了I/O开销，为大规模模型合并提供了实用的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Weight-space model merging is usually formulated as an algebraic operation on checkpoints, yet at LLM scale the limiting resource is often the set of expert weights that must be read. We introduce MergePipe, a budget-aware execution layer that casts LLM merging as an \emph{expert access-set} problem: given a merge operator and a checkpoint family in a shared weight coordinate system, choose which expert delta blocks to access under an explicit I/O budget. MergePipe indexes parameter blocks, builds deterministic access plans, and executes the induced budgeted merge with replayable manifests. The plan is budget-sound by construction and recovers the full-read merge at full budget; for fixed-coefficient additive operators, the omitted-update error is bounded by the norm of omitted deltas. Across Qwen and Llama merging workloads, MergePipe reduces expert-read I/O by up to an order of magnitude and achieves up to $11\times$ speedups. Representative budget sweeps show $O(10^{-3})$ parameter deviation from full-read merges and no monotonic degradation on downstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [71] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出差异电路脆弱性（Differential Circuit Vulnerability）这一头部级度量，用于比较强化学习（RL）和监督微调（SFT）在微调大语言模型时对内部计算电路的破坏程度。实验表明：SFT更快适应目标任务但导致更严重的电路破坏和灾难性遗忘，而RL保留更多基础电路但适应速度较慢，揭示了电路保留是RL抗遗忘能力更强的机制性原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究从行为层面发现RL比SFT更能保留先验能力（基于策略梯度更新更接近基础策略），但缺乏对内部计算电路的机制层面理解。本文旨在探究这一行为优势是否有对应的电路级保留机制支撑。

Method: 提出差异电路脆弱性（Differential Circuit Vulnerability）作为头部级度量指标，量化微调过程中电路的退化程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上进行科学问答任务适应，对比RL和SFT两种微调方式。

Result: 发现明确的机制性权衡：SFT更快适应目标任务但造成显著的电路破坏和先验能力遗忘；RL保留更大比例的基础电路结构，但任务适应速度较慢。

Conclusion: 电路保留程度可能是RL相比SFT更能抵御灾难性遗忘的机制性解释。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [72] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种基于轨迹感知和大语言模型推理的分子先导优化智能体，将工具选择建模为序列决策问题，在保持分子结构相似性的同时实现多步ADMET性质优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先导优化方法多为单步分子优化，未能考虑序列设计决策的长期影响，需要一种能够前瞻性推理的多步优化方法。

Method: 将分子优化工具选择建模为序列决策问题，利用大语言模型推理能力进行轨迹感知的决策，在结构约束下进行前瞻性分子优化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务上，TRACE相比基线模型实现了更高的优化成功率、更大的性质提升和更高的有效性，同时保持了分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的序列决策方法能够有效克服单步优化的局限性，为分子先导优化提供更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [73] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人非完美信息纸牌游戏Big 2中，通过自强化学习框架比较了多种算法，发现PPO在性能上优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning，并证实了适度熵正则化和当前策略自对弈的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非完美信息多人博弈中智能体在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手条件下的表现，而Big 2作为四人非完美信息纸牌游戏，为相关研究提供了受控实验环境。

Method: 构建了Big 2的自强化学习框架，在统一环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议下，对策略梯度方法（PPO）与值近似方法（蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA、Q-learning）进行受控对比；并研究了熵正则化和不同自对弈策略（当前策略自对弈、检查点自对弈、固定对手训练）的影响。

Result: PPO在对抗随机、贪心和启发式Big 2对手时，性能优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning；适度熵正则化通过防止策略过度确定性来提升PPO；当前策略自对弈相比检查点自对弈和固定对手训练，在有限预算下提供了更强的课程学习效果。

Conclusion: Big 2可作为研究非完美信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下深度强化学习的有用受控环境，PPO结合适度熵正则化与当前策略自对弈是处理此类复杂博弈的有效方法。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [74] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的物理探索中，其潜在空间以物理世界几何结构为组织原则自发形成空间语义结构，预测性能与语义对齐共同提升，且KL正则化强度是关键调控因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究世界模型在无语言监督条件下从物理探索中学到的表征的组织原则，验证物理几何结构是否是潜在空间语义结构形成的核心驱动力。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型执行随机具身探索，通过方向准确率、位置RSA等指标评估潜在空间的空间语义结构，并设置20个时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系，同时进行KL正则化（beta=0.1 vs 0.001）的双重敲除实验验证共享驱动假说。

Result: 训练后方向准确率0.677（随机编码器0.547），位置RSA 0.192（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐呈显著负相关（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；高KL正则化（beta=0.1）导致几何结构与语义能力在50000步时同时崩溃至接近随机水平，降低beta至0.001可恢复。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则，KL正则化强度控制着模型对几何结构的访问能力，这对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体有直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [75] [Evolving Features vs Evolving Entire Trees with GP for Interpretable Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30119)
*Thalea Schlender,Peter A. N. Bosman,Tanja Alderliesten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用遗传编程多目标进化可解释的特征集，并与不同树诱导策略结合，以及联合优化生存树结构和非线性分裂逻辑，以在保持浅层树可解释性的同时提升预测性能。实验表明进化特征构建能提升预测性能，完整联合进化最有潜力生成性能良好的可解释浅层生存树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析中，生存树需要在准确性和可解释性间取得平衡。传统生存树通常需要长成大树才能捕捉复杂关系，但这损害了可解释性；同时贪心构建方法可能忽略全局最优分裂组合，限制了预测性能。因此需要一种方法在保持浅层树（可解释）的同时提升预测性能。

Method: 使用遗传编程（Genetic Programming）多目标进化可解释的高阶特征组合，研究其与不同树诱导策略的交互；进一步提出联合进化方法，同时优化生存树结构和非线性分裂逻辑。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集和两种不同生存树深度下，进化特征构建能提升不同树诱导策略的预测性能。完整联合进化具有最高潜力，能提出多个可解释的浅层生存树并保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 进化方法（尤其是联合进化）是生成高性能且可解释的浅层生存树的有效途径，可在保持模型可理解性的同时提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Survival analysis concerns the task of predicting the time until an event occurs. Often used in the medical field, survival analysis deals with incomplete (i.e., censored) data, for instance, from patients who did not experience the event during the duration of the study. For practical use, both accuracy and interpretability are important.
  Survival trees are easy-to-follow survival models that split the patient cohort recursively into discrete patient groups. Whilst survival trees can capture complex relationships, they typically need to grow large, threatening interpretability. Moreover, survival trees are often built using greedy approaches that may overlook globally optimal split combinations, limiting predictive performance.
  Shallow survival trees require expressive, higher-order feature combinations to achieve competitive accuracy. We therefore use genetic programming to multi-objectively evolve inherently inspectable feature sets and study how they interact with different tree induction strategies. We further introduce an evolutionary approach that jointly optimises the survival tree structure and the non-linear split logic.
  Our findings demonstrate that evolutionary feature construction improves predictive performance across different tree induction strategies on two real-world datasets and two different survival tree depths. Full joint evolution has the overall highest potential to propose multiple inherently inspectable shallow survival trees of good performance.

</details>


### [76] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COM策略，通过引入几何约束来保留时间序列令牌的连续性和有序性，从而提升基于令牌的时间序列大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于令牌的时间序列大语言模型忽视了时间序列令牌固有的连续性和有序性，这严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束，以保留时间序列令牌的连续性和有序性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上，COM一致地提升了基于令牌的TS-LLMs的性能，取得了有竞争力的结果和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列令牌嵌入中的连续性和有序性对于基于令牌的TS-LLMs的有效性至关重要。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [77] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow提出了一种基于Koopman动力学专家的流匹配方法，通过置信度感知的胜者全得机制和残差动力学修正，有效解决了标准流匹配在多模态、多尺度时序数据生成中的频谱收缩和模式覆盖不足问题，在多个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时序信号常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（如振荡和高频变化）。标准流匹配使用单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器，在 heterogeneous 时间分布中，不同模态可能经过相近的流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度，导致估计器学习到过度平滑的局部传输场近似，造成分支特定动力学衰减、频谱失真和模式覆盖率差。

Method: 提出PrismFlow，包含两个核心组件：(1) Koopman启发的动力学专家，在潜在空间中学习残差校正，使局部非线性时间演化可被线性转移近似；(2) 置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）目标，仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，对其他专家进行梯度掩码，鼓励模态特定专业化。采样时，选中的专家向全局传输场添加残差动力学校正。

Result: 在多个基准上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准流匹配的频谱收缩问题，Context-FID提升15.6%，Discriminative Score提升38.6%，在低数据量场景下保持鲁棒性，并有效适用于预测和插补任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过动力学专家和WTA机制，在不破坏流匹配稳定性的前提下恢复细粒度、高频时间结构，实现了多模态时序数据生成的质量提升。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [78] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态平衡方法BMLR，通过从标签侧重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度，从而解决多模态学习中因模态映射难度差异导致的模态不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态学习方法主要通过增强弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解模态不平衡，但这些策略仅补偿优化速率差异，往往以牺牲强模态的优化能力为代价，且未深入分析模态层面学习速率差异的根本原因。

Method: 提出Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR)，首次从标签侧设计促进多模态平衡。通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态与共享标签空间之间的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并为每个模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR持续提升了多模态性能，并展现出与多种模型设计的强兼容性。

Conclusion: 从标签侧入手重塑跨模态标签空间是一种有效的多模态平衡新范式，能够在不牺牲强模态优化能力的前提下解决模态学习速率差异问题。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [79] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏差和噪声的三元统计框架研究Platonic表征假说（PRH），发现：(1) 信号层面，Platonic对齐源于对象-属性的普遍关系，通过线性表征假说（LRH）编码，稀疏自编码器提取的线性特征表现出更强的跨模态对齐；(2) 偏差层面，不同架构和训练程序导致的隐式偏差可通过中心化和归一化部分缓解；(3) 噪声层面，有限样本训练导致表征噪声，词频与对齐度呈强正相关。最终提出一个改进LRH的统计模型来解释跨架构表征对齐现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Platonic Representation Hypothesis（PRH）的内在机制，理解为什么不同AI架构中会涌现出对齐的表征。

Method: 将表征分解为信号、偏差和噪声三部分；利用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征；通过中心化和归一化缓解偏差；分析词频与对齐度的相关性。

Result: 稀疏表征比稠密表征展现出更强的跨模态对齐；中心化和归一化能一致提升跨模型对齐；词频与LLM和文本嵌入模型中的对齐度存在强正相关。

Conclusion: 提出了一个改进线性表征假说（LRH）的统计模型，能够解释多种现代AI架构中表征对齐的现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [80] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对量化基准测试中的可靠性问题，将经典配对二项样本量计算方法（Miettinen, 1968）适配到量化基准中，推导出一个保守的最小可检测效应（MDE）界δ*，并辅以未配对先导审计和MMLU提示模板对比研究，证明n=100子样本上的多数方差为二项抽样噪声，且提示模板方差会淹没量化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试中，模型在低精度（如NF4）与全精度（如FP16）之间的性能差异常常被基准测试本身的不可靠性所掩盖。研究者需要一个形式化的方法来区分真实的量化退化与随机抽样噪声，从而为基准设计者提供可预先承诺的可靠性预算。

Method: 1) 将Miettinen配对二项样本量公式适配到量化基准场景，推导MDE界δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，其中m为配对项数，ρ_d为FP16-NF4不一致率。2) 在4个模型×4个基准（k=5折，n=100）上实证验证。3) 并行开展MMLU提示模板对比研究，比较量化噪声与提示噪声的量级。4) 提供五行的预注册模板。

Result: 1) 假设ρ_d=0.10时，所有NF4-FP16差异均低于MDE界。2) 多数跨折标准差落在二项参考值√(p(1-p)/n)的±1.5个百分点内，表明n=100子样本上的方差主要为二项抽样噪声。3) OPT-WinoGrande边界案例（|Δ|=3.2 pp）在ρ_d=0.10时低于MDE，但在ρ_d=0.05时高于MDE，揭示了规划权衡。4) MMLU提示模板差异范围（2-10 pp）达到或超过最大量化差异（3.2 pp），提示模板不固定会吸收量化效应。

Conclusion: 量化基准设计者应当在使用基准前预先声明可靠性预算（MDE界），并将提示模板固定化以分离量化噪声与模板噪声。所提界将"我的量化声称有多可靠"转化为一行预算公式，并配套预注册模板以增强研究可重复性。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [81] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文蒸馏形式化为潜在记忆管理问题，通过将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器构建模块化记忆库，结合检索、路由和自门控机制实现显式记忆选择与安全激活，并引入缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法显著优于基线，自门控机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆增强了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏后的潜在记忆应如何存储、检索以及在非预言机环境下安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立的LoRA适配器，构建模块化记忆库；2) 查询时检索候选记忆，将查询路由至最合适的适配器；3) 采用自门控机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；4) 引入缓存共享以降低推理时的管理开销。

Result: 该方法在检索场景下显著优于基线，自门控机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏形式化为潜在记忆管理问题是有效的，模块化记忆库结合门控机制能够实现更安全、更鲁棒的潜在记忆激活。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [82] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用稀疏自编码器(SAEs)研究LoRA微调引起的表示几何变化，发现LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表示结构，与预训练SAE特征的对齐程度较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已被广泛用于大模型适配，但其内部表示变化尚不明确，理解这些变化对于机制可解释性、适配分析和安全审计至关重要。

Method: 引入delta激活框架分离适配器贡献，使用Gemma-2-9B在rank 4/8/16/32下训练适配器特定SAE，通过余弦相似度、主角度分析和CKA评估特征空间对齐。

Result: LoRA特征字典与预训练SAE特征几何对齐较弱；适配器特定SAE重构delta激活效果更优；特征密度随rank和深度增加，几何分歧在不同rank间保持稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调可诱导出预训练可解释性字典无法完全捕获的特征结构，对微调语言模型的机制可解释性和安全审计有重要意义。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [83] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Guidance框架，通过利用生成过程的固有几何结构来控制扩散模型。该方法学习条件期望算子的奇异函数，将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）直接投影到采样轨迹上，无需重训练或反向传播。在CIFAR-10上，条件精度提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍，且无需辅助模型即可实现空间控制。框架还揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，确定了最优引导时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型控制方法需要重训练或反向传播，计算成本高且不稳定。作者希望利用生成过程的固有几何结构，实现无需重训练和反向传播的高保真控制。

Method: 首先，将数据被噪声逐步破坏的过程建模为条件期望算子，表征其中少数信息丰富的特征为该算子的奇异函数。然后，通过自监督目标学习这些奇异函数。最后，将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）投影到该基上，直接作用于采样轨迹。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，条件精度比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍。同一套表示同时支持标签引导、CLIP引导和基于掩码的空间控制，无需辅助模型。揭示了引导有效性的相变现象。

Conclusion: Spectral Guidance是一种高效、稳定的扩散模型控制框架，无需重训练或反向传播，在精度和速度上均显著优于现有方法，并提供了对生成过程的理论洞察。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control.
