Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [120] [Neural-Behavioral Representation of Natural Whole-body Movement in Monkeys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29355)
*Jieshi He,Puzhe Li,Yanan Sui,Mu-ming Poo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种针对自由运动猴子的神经-行为记录与建模框架，通过结合大规模硬膜外皮层信号和多视角运动捕捉，利用自回归编码器-解码器模型重建并解码了全身运动，证明无需物理约束即可从神经活动解码自然全身行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动解码研究受限于受限任务和有限肢体运动，难以理解皮层如何表征自然全身行为，主要挑战在于运动多样性及大规模全身运动神经表征的不可及性。

Method: 构建了一个定制的数据采集平台，同步记录自由运动猴子的大规模硬膜外皮层信号（覆盖感觉和运动相关区域）与多视角运动捕捉数据；使用自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑的行为先验，并以神经信号为条件解码全身运动。

Result: 模型在不依赖显式物理约束的条件下，成功解码了准确且真实的猴全身运动。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用大规模颅内神经活动解码灵长类动物的自然全身运动提供了一种新颖的概念验证方法。

Abstract: Understanding how cortical activity represents natural whole-body behaviors in primates remains challenging. Limited by the diversity of movements and inaccessibility of large-scale neural representation of whole-body kinematics, previous motor decoding studies focused on constrained tasks and limited limb movements. Here, we present a neural-behavioral recording and modeling framework for freely moving monkeys, combining large-scale epidural cortical signals from distributed sensory- and motor-related areas with synchronized multi-view motion capture through a custom-made data collection platform. We reconstructed whole-body monkey kinematics and learned a compact behavior prior using an autoregressive encoder-decoder model. Conditioned on neural signals, the model decoded accurate and realistic whole-body movement without explicit physical constraints. Our results provide a novel proof-of-concept approach for decoding natural whole-body movements in primates using large-scale intracranial neural activity.

</details>


### [121] [Solving Integer Linear Programming with Parallel Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29366)
*Kyuil Sim,Sanghyeok Choi,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种免求解器、基于采样的整数线性规划优化框架，通过局部平衡提议和并行回火技术（包括标准温度回火和惩罚回火）直接在离散可行区域进行探索，无需训练或外部求解器，在多个基准上超越SCIP，与Gurobi竞争力相当，且对分布偏移具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ILP方法依赖精确求解器或启发式方法，而学习型方法泛化能力差且依赖外部求解器，缺乏一种免训练、免求解器的直接离散优化方法。

Method: 提出免求解器、基于采样的优化框架；利用线性结构设计局部平衡提议构建转移核，避免梯度近似；引入并行回火技术，包括标准温度回火和创新的惩罚回火（调节约束屏障同时保留可行解目标景观）。

Result: 在四个基准上一致优于SCIP；在200秒预算内，两个任务上匹配或超越Gurobi；对分布偏移的鲁棒性显著优于学习型方法；在MIPLIB 2017上无需问题特定调参即可与经典求解器竞争。

Conclusion: 基于采样的免求解器方法在ILP问题上具有竞争力，兼具性能优势、泛化能力和鲁棒性，为ILP优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Integer Linear Programming (ILP) serves as a versatile framework for modeling a wide range of combinatorial optimization problems, typically addressed by sophisticated exact solvers or heuristics. While learning-based approaches have recently shown their effectiveness, they suffer from poor generalization to out-of-distribution instances and inherent dependence on external solvers. In this work, we propose a solver-free, sampling-based optimization framework for ILP that directly explores discrete feasible regions without training or external solvers. Exploiting the linear structure of ILP, we employ a Locally-Balanced Proposal to construct a transition kernel, thereby avoiding the gradient approximation. To overcome the highly multimodal nature of ILP energy landscapes, we integrate Parallel Tempering. In addition to standard temperature tempering, we introduce penalty tempering, which modulates constraint barriers while preserving the objective landscape over feasible solutions. Empirically, our method consistently outperforms SCIP across all four benchmarks, matches or exceeds Gurobi on two of four tasks within a 200-second budget, and is substantially more robust to distribution shift than learning-based methods. Furthermore, on MIPLIB 2017 instances, our framework remains competitive with classical solvers without any problem-specific tuning.

</details>


### [122] [TRACER: Persistent Regularization for Robust Multimodal Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29380)
*Hesam Asadollahzadeh,Feng Liu,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACER框架，通过理论分析证明加权移动平均（WMA）教师优于标准指数移动平均（EMA）教师，用于多模态对比微调中的正则化，有效缓解灾难性遗忘并提升分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型微调时，主流策略常导致分布外（OOD）鲁棒性下降（灾难性遗忘）。标准EMA教师存在坍缩问题，缺乏有效的正则化手段来保留预训练知识。

Method: 1) 建立多模态对比微调的理论框架，推导闭式解与几何分解；2) 证明WMA教师相比EMA教师具有持久正则化力且无偏收敛；3) 提出TRACER：对比学习结合WMA引导的多视角蒸馏。

Result: 在CLIP微调实验中，TRACER在三种骨干架构上均取得一致的OOD准确率与校准增益，且对超参数选择鲁棒。

Conclusion: WMA教师有效解决了EMA坍缩问题，TRACER是一种原理清晰且实用的多模态模型鲁棒微调方法。

Abstract: Mainstream strategies for finetuning pretrained multimodal models often degrade out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for multimodal contrastive finetuning, yielding closed-form solutions and a geometric decomposition for each strategy. This framework shows that self-distillation is more effective than other regularization approaches to retain the knowledge of the pretrained model. Our analysis reveals a largely overlooked limitation: standard Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teachers, widely used in robust finetuning, suffer from collapse. To solve this, we prove that a Weighted Moving Average (WMA) teacher maintains a persistent regularizing force over finite horizons and yields bias-free convergence in the task subspace while preserving orthogonal knowledge. These insights motivate **TRACER** (**T**rajectory-**R**obust **A**nchoring for **C**ontrastive **E**ncoder **R**egularization), which combines contrastive learning with WMA-guided multi-perspective distillation. Extensive experiments on CLIP finetuning demonstrate consistent OOD accuracy and calibration gains across three backbone architectures, and comprehensive ablations confirm that TRACER is both principled and robust to hyperparameter choices. Code is available at [https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER](https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER).

</details>


### [123] [GDSD: Reinforcement Learning as Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29398)
*Xiaohang Tang,Keyue Jiang,Che Liu,Qifang Zhao,Xiaoxiao Xu,Sangwoong Yoon,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation (GDSD)，通过优势引导的自教师蒸馏直接优化扩散大语言模型的去噪器，绕过了基于ELBO似然替代方法中的训练-推理不匹配偏差，在规划、数学和编程任务上优于现有方法，最高提升+19.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ELBO的强化学习方法虽然与预训练对齐，但引入训练-推理不匹配（TIM）偏差，因为用ELBO作为似然替代会导致性能退化。作者旨在设计一种无偏的RL方法用于扩散LLM的策略优化。

Method: GDSD从逆向KL正则化RL的闭式最优解推导出优势引导的自教师，通过无归一化的目标函数将dLLM的去噪器logits匹配到教师分布，将RL转化为无似然的自我蒸馏，从而避免TIM偏差。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B模型上的规划、数学和编程基准测试中，GDSD一致优于最先进的ELBO方法，训练奖励更稳定，测试准确率提升高达+19.6%。

Conclusion: 直接对去噪器进行自我蒸馏，不依赖ELBO似然替代，能够为扩散LLM提供更稳定、更有效的强化学习过程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can be used to improve the policy (denoiser) of diffusion large language models (dLLMs), while being hindered by the intractability of the policy likelihood. A dominant and efficient family of methods replaces the likelihood in standard RL with its evidence lower bound (ELBO), estimated from randomly masked sequences. Despite being well aligned with pre-training, these approaches introduce bias through training--inference mismatch by using the ELBO as a likelihood surrogate, which can degrade performance. In this work, we propose Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation (GDSD) to directly distill the denoiser of dLLMs from an advantage-guided self-teacher, derived from the closed-form optimum of reverse-KL regularized RL. GDSD matches the dLLM's denoiser logits to the teacher's via a normalization-free objective, which reduces RL to likelihood-free self-distillation and thus bypasses the TIM biases. Recent ELBO-based methods emerge as instances of applying different distillation divergences, but with diagnosable pathologies that GDSD avoids. On planning, math, and coding benchmarks with LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B, GDSD consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art ELBO-based methods with a more stable training reward dynamics, achieving test-accuracy improvements of up to $+19.6\%$. These results suggest that direct denoiser self-distillation, without relying on an ELBO likelihood surrogate, can provide a more stable and effective RL procedure for dLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/GaryBall/GDSD.

</details>


### [124] [Rethinking Post-Training Recipes for Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29401)
*Haoxin Liu,Yichen Zhou,Rajat Sen,B. Aditya Prakash,Abhimanyu Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PostTime方法，通过后训练LLM使其能够利用多模态上下文信息修正数值时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的预测结果，在TimesX基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM仅能处理纯数值时间序列数据，无法像LLM那样融合多模态、非数值的上下文信息（如文本、图像等），而这些信息在实际场景中往往影响时间序列走势。本文旨在弥合这一差距，实现多模态时间序列预测。

Method: 提出PostTime后训练框架，结合监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），同时设计了自动生成推理轨迹的方法。PostTime训练LLM根据多模态上下文生成条件化的预测干预决策（修改、保留或忽略TSFM的先验预测）。

Result: 在TimesX多模态预测基准上使用Gemma-3-4B LLM和TimesFM-2.5 TSFM进行评估，PostTime显著优于单独的TSFM、仅用LLM的基线以及现有的多模态预测方法。

Conclusion: 结合LLM的后训练与TSFM的强数值先验，能够有效实现多模态上下文引导的时间序列预测，为融合非数值信息到时间序列基础模型提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) excel at zero-shot unimodal forecasting using numerical data, but unlike LLMs they cannot consume multimodal, non-numerical context that often shape real-world trajectories. In this work, we bridge this gap and argue for a multimodal time-series forecasting approach that post-trains LLMs to act as context-guided revisors over strong numerical TSFM priors. We introduce PostTime, a post-training recipe combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), along with a methodology to generate automated reasoning traces for forecast revisions. PostTime teaches an LLM to generate context-conditioned forecast interventions -- decisions to revise, preserve, or ignore the TSFM prior based on the multimodal context. We evaluate this approach on the TimesX multimodal forecasting benchmark using a Gemma-3-4B LLM and TimesFM-2.5 TSFM, and show that it significantly outperforms standalone TSFMs, LLM-only baselines, and existing multimodal forecasting approaches.

</details>


### [125] [Information-Directed Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29405)
*Keru Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息导向采样（IDS）框架，通过条件互信息量化离线数据后的残差不确定性，证明IDS能继承Thompson采样的信息比界，并在贝叶斯线性奖励模型中实现亚线性遗憾界。实验表明，当离线数据有信息但存在偏差或低概率残差不确定性时，IDS通过针对性在线探索带来显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据集虽然能降低不确定性，但无法消除探索需求——它只是改变了需要探索的剩余不确定性（残差不确定性）。现有方法通常先用离线数据预热策略，再通过有限在线交互进行微调，但缺乏对残差不确定性的形式化刻画和最优探索策略的理论指导。

Method: 1. 用条件互信息 $I(χ;τ_{1:T}\mid\mathcal{D}_N)$ 形式化残差不确定性；2. 提出信息导向采样(IDS)框架（参数$\eta\ge0$），通过平衡瞬时遗憾与信息增益选择动作；3. 证明比率证书：任何Thompson采样满足的信息比界都能被IDS继承；4. 在贝叶斯线性奖励模型中推导出条件互信息的对数行列式形式，给出IDS的遗憾界。

Result: 1. IDS在已知动力学贝叶斯线性奖励模型中达到$\widetilde O(Hd\min\{\sqrt T, T\sqrt{C^\dagger/N}\})$遗憾界；2. 发现暖启动场景中IDS会选择信息性探针而Thompson采样不会，导致常数因子的贝叶斯遗憾分离；3. 在D4RL离线到在线强化学习实验中验证了IDS的有效性。

Conclusion: IDS最适用于以下场景：离线数据具有信息量，但留下偏见性或低概率的残差不确定性，需要针对性的在线动作来解决。这一机制共同存在于离线强化学习、离线黑盒优化和贝叶斯优化中。

Abstract: Decision-making from offline datasets typically warm-starts a policy or score model from fixed offline data and then refines it with limited online interaction. Offline data reduces uncertainty, but it does not remove the need for exploration; it changes what remains to be explored. We formalise this residual uncertainty by the conditional mutual information $I(χ;τ_{1:T}\mid\mathcal{D}_N)$ between a learning target $χ$ and the online trajectories after conditioning on the offline dataset. This view leads naturally to information-directed sampling (IDS), a family parameterised by $η\ge 0$ that selects actions by trading off instantaneous regret against information gain. We prove a generic offline-to-online Bayesian regret bound for IDS through a ratio certificate: any information-ratio bound satisfied by a reference Thompson-sampling policy over the same randomised policy class is inherited by IDS. In a known-dynamics Bayesian linear-reward model, the conditional mutual information has a log-determinant form, and vanilla IDS ($η=0$) satisfies $\widetilde O\!\left(Hd\min\left\{\sqrt T,\,T\sqrt{C^\dagger_{β,\mathrm{IDS}_0}(N,T)/N}\right\}\right),$ where the coverage coefficient is tied to the visitation distribution induced by vanilla IDS itself. We also identify a warm-start regime with a dominated but informative probe in which vanilla IDS selects the probe while Thompson sampling never does, giving a constant-factor Bayesian regret separation. Controlled bandit experiments and D4RL offline-to-online RL experiments validate this mechanism: IDS is most beneficial when offline data is informative but leaves biased or low-probability residual uncertainty that targeted online actions can resolve, a regime shared by offline RL, offline black-box optimization, and Bayesian optimization.

</details>


### [126] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过SCM3K基准测试发现，马尔可夫边界在理论上能确定预测所需的最少特征集，但实际中通过因果发现恢复边界后再训练的效果不佳，原因包括发现算法优化目标与预测不一致、误分类成本不对称、以及存在多个优于全特征的特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究马尔可夫边界在表格预测中是否真正有用，以及为何理论上最优的特征选择方案在实际因果发现+预测pipeline中失效。

Method: 在SCM3K基准（3450个任务，40-1000个特征，6种SCM族）上，用6种回归器评估：1) 使用Oracle马尔可夫边界 vs 全特征集；2) 使用因果发现恢复边界后再训练的自然pipeline。

Result: 使用Oracle边界显著提升预测效果，且特征空间越大越稀疏时提升越明显；但因果发现+预测的pipeline未能超越全特征集，现有发现算法在到达边界最有效的区域前就已耗尽计算预算。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界需要预测对齐的发现算法才能发挥实际价值；需要对预测与因果发现之间的目标不一致问题进行校正。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [127] [How Much Is a Dataset Worth? Scaling Laws, the Vendi Score, and Matrix Spectral Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29448)
*Jeff A. Bilmes,Gantavya Bhatt,Arnav M. Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了神经缩放定律目标函数和Vendi Score均为子模函数，并提出了矩阵谱函数这一更广泛的子模目标函数家族。通过开发基于 secular 方程的更新算法（避免重复特征分解，实现约35,000倍加速），使得在ImageNet-1K规模数据集上直接优化Vendi Score成为可能。实验发现，设施选址（facility location）在预测训练子集价值方面表现最佳，而Vendi Score在中等分数范围内有效，但过度优化反而会降低下游性能。此外，均匀随机采样的固定大小子集在评估分数和下游性能上表现出显著集中性，且即使控制了大小、类别平衡和训练预算等因素，性能仍呈现平滑的从好到差的变化范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据评估方法（神经缩放定律、Vendi Score）缺乏统一的数学框架，且Vendi Score等子模目标在大规模数据集上的直接优化计算成本过高，无法有效比较不同目标函数对训练子集价值的预测能力。

Method: 从理论上证明Vendi Score是矩阵谱函数的特例，并引入弱矩阵单调函数导出弱子模矩阵谱函数族；开发基于 secular 方程的贪心优化更新算法，避免重复特征分解，将边际增益评估复杂度降低O(m)倍；在ImageNet-1K规模上进行实证比较。

Result: 设施选址在多个数据集上表现最优；Vendi Score在中等范围有效但过度优化有害；均匀随机子集在评估分数和下游性能上高度集中；即使控制多种因素，性能仍呈现连续变化范围。

Conclusion: 提出了矩阵谱函数这一统一的子模目标框架，并通过高效算法实现了大规模数据评估。研究揭示了不同数据评估目标函数的实际表现差异及局限性，为数据子集选择提供了理论指导和实践工具。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws appraise data through dataset size, while the Vendi Score uses quantum entropy to measure dataset value. We show both that common neural-scaling-law objectives and the Vendi Score are submodular. We further show that the Vendi Score is a special case of a broader class of submodular objectives that we call matrix spectral functions. This also includes determinantal (DPP) objectives, as well as many others. We also introduce weakly matrix monotone functions and show how they lead to weakly submodular matrix spectral functions, yielding a broad family of practical objectives for data appraisal. We develop secular-equation-based updates that avoid repeated eigendecompositions during greedy optimization, reducing marginal-gain evaluation for $m$-dimensional embeddings by an $O(m)$ factor relative to oracle queries. This yields an average empirical speedup of about 35,000x, making direct optimization of the Vendi Score feasible on ImageNet-1K-scale datasets. Thus enabled, we compare how well several objectives predict the value of training subsets for held-out test performance under fixed-size, class-balanced, and fixed training-budget regimes, including the Vendi Score, DPPs, facility location, and three new matrix spectral variants. Across multiple datasets, facility location performs the best. Direct optimization also reveals that, while the Vendi Score is predictive over moderate score ranges, pushing the objective to higher values can make it a poor downstream performance proxy. We also find that uniformly at random fixed-size subsets, both unconstrained and class-balanced, are remarkably concentrated in both appraisal scores and held-out performance. Finally, we show that size, class balance, and training budget do not alone determine data value: even when controlling for these factors, performance ranges smoothly from good to bad.

</details>


### [128] [Forget Less, Generalize More: Unifying Temporal and Structural Adaptation for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29453)
*Qian Chang,Ciprian Doru Giurcaneanu,Runsong Jia,Xia Li,Guoping Hu,Xiufeng Cheng,Jinqing Yang,Mengjia Wu,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DSRD框架，通过双尺度保持动态统一建模时序记忆与结构上下文，在14个真实基准上实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图表示学习方法采用固定时间衰减方案或预定结构传播深度，难以泛化到具有不同交互频率和拓扑特征的图。

Method: 提出双尺度保持动态(DSRD)框架，包含：(i) 双尺度适应的保持状态，在单一循环公式中联合建模时序动态与结构传播；(ii) 自适应衰减核，通过学习时间敏感参数自动平衡短期响应与长期保持。

Result: 在14个真实世界基准上，链接预测和节点分类任务均达到SOTA，在直推式和归纳式设置下均具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DSRD通过理论分析建立了事件级并行聚合与高效循环状态更新的等价性，并提供了学习动态的稳定性和有界性保证。

Abstract: Representation learning on dynamic graphs requires capturing complex dependencies that evolve across both time and structure. Existing approaches typically adopt fixed temporal decay schemes or predetermined structural propagation depths, limiting their ability to generalize across graphs with diverse interaction frequencies and topological characteristics. We propose Dual-Scale Retentive Dynamics (DSRD), a unified framework that maintains a retentive representation state encoding both temporal memory and structural context. DSRD introduces two key components: (i) a retentive state with dual-scale adaptation that jointly models temporal dynamics and structural propagation within a single recurrent formulation, and (ii) adaptive decay kernels with learnable time-sensitivity parameters that automatically balance short-term responsiveness and long-term retention based on the underlying interaction patterns. We provide theoretical analysis establishing the equivalence between event-wise parallel aggregation and efficient recurrent state updates, as well as stability and boundedness guarantees for the learned dynamics. Extensive experiments on 14 real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DSRD consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on both link prediction and node classification tasks, with strong generalization across transductive and inductive settings.

</details>


### [129] [A Full-Pipeline Framework for Evaluating Membership Inference Attacks in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29454)
*Ding Chen,Xinwen Cheng,Xuyang Zhong,Xinping Chen,Xiaolin Huang,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统性的评估框架，用于衡量不同上下文（数据、架构、算法、后训练模块）对成员推断攻击（MIA）效能的影响，并制定了标准化的威胁模型和评估指标，最终为从业者提供了隐私审计指南和工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA研究缺乏系统性的评估框架来刻画不同上下文（如数据集、架构、算法等）对攻击效能的影响，导致在实际部署中表现良好的算法可能因缺乏对特定真实场景的考量而失去统计相关性。

Method: 提出了一个全面的评估框架，系统性地刻画机器学习全流程（数据、架构、算法、后训练模块）中的隐私风险；采用三种互补指标（平衡准确率、低FPR下的TPR、低FNR下的TNR）以应对不同误分类成本；形式化了两种标准化威胁模型并将现有MIA适配为对应变体以实现公平基准测试。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，特定MIA方法的效能高度依赖于假设的威胁模型和所选评估指标。

Conclusion: 将研究发现提炼为可操作的指南，并提供了一个即用型审计工具包，以帮助从业者进行更好的隐私评估。

Abstract: While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) are the prevailing method for identifying training data, their application has expanded into privacy auditing and machine unlearning. Nevertheless, the field lacks a systematic framework for evaluating how different contexts affect MIA efficacy. Without such a characterization, practitioners risk deploying algorithms that perform well on benchmarks but become statistically irrelevant when faced with the nuances of specific, real-world datasets. To bridge this gap and provide actionable insights, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework that systematically characterizes privacy risks across the entire machine learning pipeline, spanning data, architectures, algorithms, and post-training modules. Designed to inherently capture diverse operational contexts, our framework rigorously evaluates state-of-the-art MIAs across a broad spectrum of training configurations. To account for varying misclassification costs in real-world deployments, we employ three complementary metrics: Balanced Accuracy for symmetric costs, alongside TPR at low FPR (or TNR at low FNR) for asymmetric scenarios where false alarms or missed detections are strictly penalized. Furthermore, recognizing that existing MIAs assume divergent adversary capabilities, we formalize two standardized threat models and adapt these attacks into corresponding variants to ensure an equitable benchmark. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that the efficacy of specific MIA methodologies is highly sensitive to the assumed threat models and chosen evaluation metrics. Ultimately, we distill these findings into actionable guidelines and provide a ready-to-use auditing toolkit, empowering practitioners to conduct better privacy assessments.

</details>


### [130] [Honest Lying: Understanding Memory Confabulation in Reflexive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29463)
*Prakhar Dixit,Sadia Kamal,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Reflexion风格的智能体在ALFWorld和HumanEval环境中存在记忆虚构（memory confabulation）问题：智能体存储自信但错误的任务解释，并在多轮试验中持续依赖这些错误内容。作者提出反射重复率（RRR）指标检测此问题，并通过程序化提取轨迹级失败信号替代开放式自我诊断，将正确目标提及率从0%提升至86%，RRR从0.64降至0.10，解决了16个冻结环境中的3个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑Reflexion风格智能体的核心假设——智能体能准确诊断自身失败，发现该假设在系统层面可能失效，智能体会将错误信念存储为记忆并在后续轮次中持续依赖。

Method: 1. 提出记忆虚构（memory confabulation）概念；2. 设计反射重复率（RRR）指标，通过日志检测对错误反射内容的重复依赖；3. 提出缓解方案：用程序化提取轨迹级失败信号替代开放式自我诊断。

Result: 在ALFWorld中发现16个冻结环境（121条反射均未提及正确目标对象），HumanEval中发现4个类似案例。缓解方案将正确目标提及率从0%提升至86%，RRR从0.64降至0.10，解决3/16的冻结环境。

Conclusion: 反射记忆可能强化而非纠正错误信念，开放式自我诊断存在根本性缺陷，需要更可控的失败信号提取机制。

Abstract: Reflexion-style agents rely on self-generated reflections as memory, implicitly assuming that agents can accurately diagnose their own failures.We show that this assumption can fail systematically: across ALFWorld and HumanEval, agents store confident but incorrect interpretations of the task and continue acting on them across trials,even though the environment resets to the correct task each time. We call this failure mode memory confabulation and introduce the Reflection Repetition Rate (RRR), a log-based metric that detects repeated reliance on incorrect reflective content.Using RRR, we identify 16 frozen environments in ALFWorld, where 0 of 121 reflections mention the correct target object, and 4 analogous cases in HumanEval. Our mitigation replaces open-ended self-diagnosis with programmatic extraction of trajectory-level failure signals, increasing correct object mention from 0% to 86%, reducing RRR from 0.64 to 0.10, and solving 3 of 16 frozen ALFWorld environments, suggesting that reflective memory can reinforce false beliefs rather than correct them.

</details>


### [131] [Composing Non-Conjugate Factor Graphs with Closed-Form Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29467)
*Mykola Lukashchuk,Kyrylo Yemets,Wouter M. Kouw,Dmitry Bagaev,İsmail Şenöz,Jeff Beck,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了由五种因子图原语（双线性因子、指数链接、Gamma先验、高斯似然、等式节点）构成的任意概率模型，在平均场分解下保持闭式变分消息传播，并展示了从静态集成到分裂分支路由的深度组合可实现通用函数逼近，在时间序列预测中提供了校准的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将概率构建块堆叠为更深层架构通常会破坏闭式推理，本文旨在证明闭式推理可以被保留。

Method: 识别五种因子图原语并证明其保持闭式变分消息传播；通过消息族（高斯变量保持高斯、精度变量保持Gamma）在平均场分解下的闭合性实现；非共轭接口（指数链接）通过高斯矩母函数和Gamma充分统计量保持可处理性。

Result: 展示了从静态集成到输入依赖门控再到分裂分支路由的递增深度组合；堆叠路由层可编码任意决策树，建立了具有闭式推理的通用函数逼近；在集成时间序列预测中，贝叶斯混合专家模型的门控函数是推断而非学习的，在五个基准数据集上提供了校准的不确定性。

Conclusion: 通过识别和组合特定的因子图原语，可以在深层概率架构中保留闭式变分推理，实现通用函数逼近，并在实际应用（如时间序列预测）中提供校准的不确定性估计。

Abstract: Stacking probabilistic building blocks into deeper architectures typically breaks closed-form inference. We show that closed-form inference can be preserved. We identify five factor-graph primitives: a bilinear factor, an exponential link, a Gamma prior, a Gaussian likelihood, and an equality node, and prove that any model composed from them admits closed-form variational message passing. The construction works because each primitive preserves a small set of message families: under mean-field factorization, messages on Gaussian variables remain Gaussian and messages on precision variables remain Gamma, while the only non-conjugate interface, the exponential link, remains tractable through the Gaussian moment-generating function and the sufficient statistics of the Gamma family. We demonstrate composition at increasing depth, from static ensembles through input-dependent gating to split-branch routing, and show that stacking routing layers encodes arbitrary decision trees, establishing universal function approximation with closed-form inference. Applied to ensemble time-series forecasting, the framework yields a Bayesian mixture of experts in which gating functions are inferred rather than learned, providing calibrated uncertainty over expert selection across five benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [132] [Gradient Perturbation: Learning to Perturb Gradients for Adaptive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29494)
*Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了梯度扰动的统一框架，并基于此设计了自适应类别级梯度扰动方法LPG，在平衡分类、长尾分类和噪声标签学习任务中取得了一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向传播路径中的扰动（特征扰动、logit扰动、标签扰动）已被广泛研究，但反向传播路径中的梯度扰动缺乏系统性探究。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立梯度扰动的统一视角。

Method: 1）建立梯度扰动的统一框架，将SAM、梯度裁剪、梯度噪声注入等方法统一纳入该框架；2）提出LPG（Learning to Perturb Gradients），自适应地对logit级别的梯度进行类别级扰动，实现类别感知训练；3）通过PAC-Bayesian分析建立梯度扰动界与泛化保证之间的理论联系。

Result: 在平衡分类、长尾分类和噪声标签学习三个任务上，LPG持续优于现有方法，且可作为即插即用模块与现有方法结合使用。

Conclusion: 梯度扰动是一种有效的训练增强手段，类别自适应的梯度扰动能够统一处理多种训练场景，并且具有理论上的泛化保证。

Abstract: Deep neural network training involves both forward propagation (from features through logits to loss) and backward propagation (from loss through gradients to parameter updates). While perturbations along the forward chain, including feature perturbation, logit perturbation, and label perturbation, have been extensively studied, the backward chain's gradient perturbation has received little systematic investigation. In this paper, we establish a unified framework for gradient perturbation, revealing that existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), gradient clipping, and gradient noise injection can all be interpreted as imposing specific forms of gradient perturbation. Analogous to the recently proposed Logit Perturbation Learning (LPL), we conjecture that amplifying the gradient norm for a class acts as positive augmentation (enhancing learning), while dampening it acts as negative augmentation (suppressing overfitting). Based on these observations, we propose Learning to Perturb Gradients (LPG), which adaptively perturbs logit-level gradients at the class level to achieve category-aware training. We also establish theoretical connections between gradient perturbation bounds and generalization guarantees via PAC-Bayesian analysis. Experiments on balanced classification, long-tail classification, and noisy label learning demonstrate that LPG consistently outperforms existing methods and can be combined with them as a plug-in module.

</details>


### [133] [On-Policy Replay for Continual Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29495)
*Yan Chen,Taojie Zhu,Meng Zhang,Xin Chen,Jiaqi Huang,Dongyang Xu,Yizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出On-Policy Replay（OPR）方法，通过回放模型自身在历史提示上生成的、经奖励过滤的（提示，模型响应）对作为普通SFT样本，在没有教师模型、辅助损失或在线蒸馏的情况下，有效缓解了持续监督微调中的灾难性遗忘问题。在三个7-8B指令微调骨干模型上的TRACE基准测试中，OPR一致降低了遗忘，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的最严苛压力测试上将BWT从-13.93提升至-0.65（10%回放预算），相比调优的Vanilla Replay基线降低了46%的|BWT|。本文还通过KL收缩解释将OPR与先前在线策略蒸馏方法统一在同一轴上，并发现低分回放效果一致差于Vanilla Replay，表明OPR的有效成分在于在线策略分布而非响应质量本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续监督微调（SFT）在适应大语言模型到一系列下游任务时存在严重的灾难性遗忘问题。虽然现有在线策略方法（如自蒸馏损失）能减少遗忘，但它们需要额外的前向传播、对调度敏感，并存在教师模型的风格漂移问题。本文旨在设计一种更简单、无教师模型、无需辅助损失的在线策略方法来解决遗忘问题。

Method: OPR方法将在线策略信号路由到训练数据源而非训练目标中。具体做法是：用最新的模型检查点在少量历史提示上生成输出，通过任务奖励过滤这些生成结果，然后将幸存下来的（提示，模型响应）对作为普通的SFT样本进行回放训练。该方法不需要教师模型、辅助损失或在线蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B、Llama3.1-8B-Instruct三个骨干模型上的TRACE持续学习基准测试中，OPR一致降低了遗忘。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的顺序SFT压力测试中，BWT从-13.93提升至-0.65（10%回放预算）和-2.29（1%预算），在所有三个骨干模型上相比调优的Vanilla Replay基线实现了42-46%的|BWT|减少。

Conclusion: OPR通过简单的数据源路由在线策略信号即可有效缓解灾难性遗忘，且性能优于需要复杂训练目标的在线策略蒸馏方法。低分回放效果一致差于Vanilla Replay的发现表明，OPR的关键在于在线策略分布本身而非响应质量，这为理解持续学习中的回放机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Continual supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the de facto recipe for adapting large language models (LLMs) to a stream of downstream tasks, but it suffers from catastrophic forgetting of earlier capabilities. Recent work shows that on-policy signals -- training on the model's own outputs -- reduce forgetting more reliably than off-policy supervision. Existing on-policy methods route this signal through a new training objective (e.g., self-distillation losses with a teacher copy), inheriting an extra forward pass, schedule sensitivity, and stylistic drift from the teacher.We instead route the on-policy signal through the training data source. Our method, On-Policy Replay (OPR), rolls out the most recent checkpoint on a small budget of historical prompts, filters the generations by a task reward, and replays the surviving (prompt, model response) pairs as ordinary SFT examples. There is no teacher, no auxiliary loss, and no on-the-fly distillation. Across three 7--8B instruction-tuned backbones (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B, Llama3.1-8B-Instruct) on the TRACE continual-learning benchmark, OPR consistently reduces forgetting; on the sharpest stress test (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Sequential SFT BWT -13.93), OPR lifts BWT to -0.65 at a 10% replay budget and to -2.29 at a 1% budget -- a 46% reduction in |BWT| over a tuned Vanilla Replay baseline, with 42--46% reductions observed across all three backbones. We give a KL-shrinkage interpretation that places OPR and prior on-policy distillation methods on a single axis, and we present a counterintuitive finding that explains why Vanilla Replay is already a strong baseline: low-score replay is uniformly worse than Vanilla Replay, demonstrating that the active ingredient in OPR is the on-policy distribution, not the response quality alone.Our code is available at https://github.com/Yancey2024/OnPolicyReplay.

</details>


### [134] [Convex Basins in Single-Index Model Loss Landscapes: Applications to Robust Recovery under Strong Adversarial Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29497)
*Santanu Das,Sagnik Chatterjee,Jatin Batra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对具有非单调链接函数（如GeLU和Swish）的高斯单指标模型（SIMs），在存在重尾噪声和对抗性腐败的背景下，提出了具有近线性样本和时间复杂度的鲁棒恢复算法，核心贡献在于证明了在对抗污染下，高斯平方损失景观中存在一个维度无关、恒定半径的凸盆地，并通过鲁棒谱初始化有效达到，从而为鲁棒梯度下降提供热启动，最终达到O(σ√ε)的估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒恢复技术（如线性回归、严格单调链接函数、相位恢复）无法扩展到非对称非单调链接函数（如GeLU和Swish），而这些函数在现代门控神经架构中作为标量基元自然出现，因此需要填补这一空白，首次为非单调非线性SIMs建立鲁棒恢复保证。

Method: 提出了一种新的结构理解：在对抗污染下，高斯平方损失景观在真实参数周围存在一个维度无关、恒定半径的凸盆地；利用鲁棒谱初始化在对抗污染下高效到达该盆地；随后通过鲁棒梯度下降进行精细优化，收敛至最终估计误差。

Result: 算法在O~(nd)时间复杂度和O~(d)样本复杂度下，达到O(σ√ε)的最终估计误差，其中ε为污染比例。这是首次对泛型非单调链接函数实现同时具有鲁棒性和高效性的恢复保证。

Conclusion: 本文通过结构洞察（凸盆地的存在性与可达性）首次为广泛类别的非线性非单调SIMs建立了鲁棒恢复的理论保证，弥合了现有技术在处理非对称非单调链接函数方面的空白，对现代门控神经架构的理论理解具有重要价值。

Abstract: We study the problem of robustly learning Gaussian Single Index Models (SIMs) in the presence of heavy-tailed noise and a constant fraction of adversarially corrupted covariates and responses. Prior work on robust recovery has considered settings such as linear regression (Pensia et al., JASA 2024), strictly monotonic link functions (Awasthi et al., NeurIPS 2022), and phase retrieval (Buna and Rebeschini, AISTATS 2025). However, these techniques do not extend to generic asymmetric non-monotonic link functions such as \textsc{GeLU} and \textsc{Swish}, which arise naturally as scalar primitives in modern gated neural architectures. We close this gap by giving the first robust recovery algorithm with near-linear sample and time complexity for generic non-monotonic link functions, thereby establishing the first robust recovery guarantees for a broad family of nonlinear SIMs for which \textit{no guarantees were previously known}. Our central contribution is a new structural understanding of the Gaussian squared-loss landscape under adversarial contamination. Crucially, we prove that for a broad class of nonlinear non-monotonic SIMs, a dimension-independent, constant-radius convex basin exists around the ground truth and is efficiently reachable via robust spectral initialization even under adversarial contamination. Prior works fail to establish both guarantees simultaneously, thereby either breaking down under adversarial contamination or failing to handle generic non-monotonic link functions. Together, these structural insights yield a principled warm start for robust gradient descent that provably converges to a final estimation error of $O(σ\sqrtε)$ in $\tilde{O}(nd)$ time with $\tilde{O}(d)$ samples, where $ε$ is the contamination fraction.

</details>


### [135] [Quotient DAGs for Off-Policy Evaluation:Forward-Flow Importance Sampling and Exact Slate Propensities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29500)
*Ziwen Xie,Shaowen Xiang,Hongyu He,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于商DAG（有向无环图）的视角，通过前向流比率为评估目标合并等价历史，解决了离线策略评估中因生成过程细节（如自回归slate推荐的顺序）与评估目标（如无序slate）不匹配导致的方差和计算问题。针对slate推荐场景，提出了Forward-DP算法，利用子集DAG动态规划计算精确的无序倾向性，无需阶乘枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略评估中，标准重要性采样方法会将被行为策略生成过程（如有序序列）的细节视为有意义，而评估目标（如无序slate）可能忽略这些细节，导致不必要的方差和计算鸿沟，因为精确计算无序slate的倾向性需要枚举所有生成顺序，计算代价过高。

Method: 1. 提出商DAG视角，将评估等价的历史合并，并在合并图上用目标策略与行为策略的前向流比率（forward-flow ratios）分配权重。2. 针对slate推荐问题，在集合充分的下一项交互接口下，提出Forward-DP算法，一种子集DAG动态规划方法，无需阶乘枚举即可计算精确的无序倾向性。

Result: Forward-DP算法能够计算精确的无序倾向性，避免了阶乘枚举的计算开销，使得基于倾向性的评估和模型选择在上下文相关的自回归slate记录器上变得可行和实用。

Conclusion: 所提出的商DAG视角和Forward-DP算法有效解决了自回归slate推荐中因生成顺序与评估目标不匹配导致的方差和计算问题，为实际场景中的离线策略评估提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation estimates how a target policy would perform using data collected by a different behavior policy, which is crucial when online testing is costly or risky, such as in recommendation or healthcare. Standard importance sampling reweights each logged trajectory, but it can treat details of the generation process as meaningful even when the evaluation target ignores them: for example, an autoregressive slate recommender may generate an ordered sequence of items while the reward and downstream estimator depend only on the unordered slate. This creates nuisance variance and a computational gap, since exact unordered slate propensities require summing over all generation orders. We introduce a quotient-DAG view that merges histories equivalent for evaluation and assigns weights using target-to-behavior forward-flow ratios on the merged graph. For slate recommendation under a set-sufficient next-item interface, this yields Forward-DP, a subset-DAG dynamic program that computes exact unordered propensities without factorial enumeration. The resulting propensity primitive enables practical propensity-based evaluation and model selection for context-dependent autoregressive slate loggers.

</details>


### [136] [K-FinHallu: A Hallucination Detection Benchmark for Multi-Turn RAG in Korean Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29523)
*Eunbyeol Cho,Yunseung Lee,Mirae Kim,Jeewon Yang,Youngjun Kwak,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-FinHallu는 한국 금융 도메인에서 다중 턴 RAG 환각 탐지를 위한 최초의 벤치마크로, 정당한 기권을 고려한 계층적 분류체계를 제안하며 최신 LLM들도 정당한 기권에서 가장 낮은 성능을 보임을 발견함.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 기존 환각 탐지 벤치마크가 단일 턴, 영어 중심 태스크에 국한되어 있어 한국 금융 도메인의 다중 턴 대화 역학과 언어·규제적 미묘함을 다루지 못함.

Method: 한국 금융 문서 기반 다중 턴 대화를 구축하고, 맥락 응답 가능성에 기반한 계층적 분류체계로 환각을 주입한 K-FinHallu 벤치마크를 제안. 최첨단 및 오픈소스 LLM을 환각 탐지기로 평가.

Result: 최강 모델도 미세한 금융 진단 및 거절 행동에 어려움을 겪음. 8B 모델 미세조정 시 최첨단 LLM과 경쟁력 있는 성능을 보였으나, 정당한 기권은 모든 평가 모델에서 가장 취약한 축으로 남음.

Conclusion: 한국 금융 도메인의 다중 턴 RAG 환경에서 정당한 기권 처리 능력이 모든 LLM에서 부족함을 확인, 향후 환각 탐지 연구의 방향성을 제시.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced financial automation through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), yet hallucinations remain a critical barrier to deployment in high-stakes environments. Existing benchmarks focus on single-turn, English-centric tasks, leaving the multi-turn dynamics and linguistic-regulatory nuances of the Korean financial domain unaddressed. We introduce K-FinHallu, the first benchmark for hallucination detection in multi-turn Korean financial RAG. We construct multi-turn dialogues from authentic Korean financial documents and inject hallucinations under a proposed hierarchical taxonomy based on context answerability that explicitly accounts for justified abstention. Benchmarking frontier and open-source LLMs as hallucination detectors, we find that even the strongest models struggle with fine-grained financial diagnostics and refusal behavior. While fine-tuning an 8B model on our training split yields performance competitive with frontier LLMs, justified abstention remains the weakest axis across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [137] [AsymVLM: Asymmetric Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29535)
*Yilin Feng,Ahmed Burak Gulhan,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型中视觉与文本令牌的异构特性，AsymVLM分别采用视觉令牌预填充前自适应剪枝和文本令牌解码时阈值驱逐策略，在节省高达54% FLOPs的同时，在文档和图表理解任务上超越现有方法2-3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法对视觉语言模型中的视觉和文本令牌采用统一处理，忽略了二者在空间冗余性（视觉令牌）和因果依赖性（文本令牌）上的本质差异，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: 提出非对称压缩框架AsymVLM：1）视觉令牌方面，使用学习的重要性评分器结合每样本自适应预算进行预填充前的激进剪枝；2）文本令牌方面，采用基于时间阈值的驱逐策略，仅在超出固定预算时进行压缩。

Result: 在FLOPs节省上最高达54%，在文档和图表理解任务上比现有方法提升2-3%，在文本主导场景中显著优于标准LLM缓存压缩方法。

Conclusion: 视觉与文本令牌的异构特性决定了应采用差异化的压缩策略，非对称处理能够在保持准确率的同时实现更高的计算效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) process thousands of visual tokens per image alongside comparatively few text tokens, yet existing compression methods treat both modalities uniformly. We observe that the two modalities have fundamentally different properties: vision tokens are spatially redundant and dominate prefill, while text tokens are causally dependent and accumulate during decoding. Based on this asymmetry, we propose and empirically evaluate AsymVLM, which applies aggressive pruning to vision tokens before prefill using a learned importance scorer with per-sample adaptive budgeting, and temporal threshold-based eviction to text tokens only when they exceed a fixed budget. Our experiments indicate that AsymVLM achieves the highest FLOPs savings (up to 54%) among state-of-the-art methods while outperforming existing approaches by 2--3% on document and chart understanding tasks where visual information is spatially localized and query-specific, and maintaining competitive accuracy on holistic benchmarks. In text-dominated scenarios, our eviction strategy substantially outperforms standard LLM cache compression methods by adapting to the short-context nature of VLM.

</details>


### [138] [Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29547)
*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Adam优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性（LGI）度量动态调整步长，解决非平滑损失景观中自适应优化器的梯度抖动问题，在QAT和高噪声小批量学习任务中优于AdamW和Prox-SGD，最高提升6%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构（如ReLU激活、量化算子）包含非平滑组件，破坏了平滑损失景观假设，导致Adam等自适应优化器出现梯度抖动（Clarke次微分内信号冲突引起的剧烈振荡），造成收敛差和泛化次优。

Method: 提出S-Adam，核心是LGI度量——通过随机方向导数方差估计Clarke次微分直径，并引入自适应阻尼机制exp(-λρ)在高不稳定区域减速更新、平滑区域保持快速收敛；收敛分析采用微分包含理论。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上最高提升6%准确率，TinyImageNet上提升3%；有效缓解梯度振荡；证明几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke平稳点，达到最优O(1/√T)收敛率。

Conclusion: S-Adam通过LGI度量感知局部几何不稳定性并动态调节步长，有效解决了非平滑优化中的梯度抖动问题，在QAT和高噪声小批量学习场景中显著优于现有优化器。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [139] [Why Larger Models Learn More: Effects of Capacity, Interference, and Rare-Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29548)
*Jing Huang,Daniel Wurgaft,Rachit Bansal,Laura Ruis,Naomi Saphra,David Alvarez-Melis,Andrew Kyle Lampinen,Christopher Potts,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过理论论证和实验验证，解释了为什么大模型能学到小模型学不到的任务：由于数据驱动的资源竞争，小模型将神经元分配给高频或低复杂度任务，而大模型通过降低梯度干扰机制，能够为罕见及复杂任务分配足够资源，从而学到这些任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大模型相比小模型能学习更多任务的根本驱动因素，尤其是幂律缩放现象背后的机制。

Method: 先提出基于幂律缩放的现象学论证，然后在合成任务混合数据集上研究模型缩放效应，观察单调缩放曲线；进一步通过在OLMo模型（4M到4B参数）上预训练不同频率和复杂度的新任务进行验证。

Result: 小模型优先将神经元分配给高频或低复杂度任务，导致在罕见和复杂任务上表现差；大模型通过降低梯度干扰机制，为常见任务分配足够资源后使其梯度更新变弱，从而不覆盖罕见任务特征。OLMo实验验证了这一结论。

Conclusion: 提供了一个以数据为中心的视角解释大模型为何优于小模型，可用于指导模型规模选择和训练数据混合策略等实际问题。

Abstract: Larger models learn tasks smaller models do not. What drives this phenomenon? We develop a simple phenomenological argument that power-law scaling already suggests that a larger model will be able to learn a part of the data distribution that a smaller model fails to learn, even with infinite training data. To validate this claim and identify its causes, we study the effects of model scaling on a synthetic setup consisting of a mixture of tasks that show monotonic scaling curves. The results point to a data-induced competition over resources (neurons). Specifically, smaller models allocate their neurons to high frequency or low complexity tasks, and so they learn solutions that perform poorly on rare and complex tasks. Moreover, this happens even when solutions capable of expressing the desired task exist. We then assess how a larger model circumvents this data-centric bottleneck, finding that it traces to a reduced interference mechanism: larger models can allocate enough resources to common tasks that the gradient updates for those tasks become weak, which means that they do not overwrite rare-task features as they slowly accumulate. Finally, to further validate these claims, we pretrain OLMo models (4M to 4B parameters) on novel tasks of varying frequency and complexity. The results mirror those from our synthetic data experiments: only the larger OLMo models learn the infrequent and complex tasks, and these larger models embed more task features in their representations and show less gradient interference between tasks. Overall, we offer a data-centric account of why larger models learn tasks that smaller models fail to. This helps explain why larger models are better in practice, and it can inform practical questions concerning model sizing and training data mixtures.

</details>


### [140] [Cluster-Level Attention-Guided Parallel Decoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29607)
*Heqiang Qi,Wei Huang,Mingyuan Bai,Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对掩码扩散语言模型，本文提出CLAD方法，将并行提交单元从token粒度提升到簇粒度，通过置信度聚类和自注意力引导的冲突感知选择，实现1.77x-8.47x的加速且保持精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDLM的无训练采样器在去噪步骤中逐个token决定是否提交，忽略了高置信度预测往往以连续片段形式出现的事实，导致并行提交的粒度不足，限制了推理效率。

Method: 首先将相邻的高置信度token聚合成置信度诱导簇作为片段级更新单元；然后利用同一前向传播的自注意力图估计簇间依赖关系，实现冲突感知的互兼容CICs选择，进行并行提交。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型家族、四个推理和代码生成基准上，CLAD相比Vanilla解码实现1.77倍至8.47倍加速，并在大多数设置中保持可比较的任务精度。

Conclusion: 将并行提交粒度从token提升到簇级别，结合自注意力引导的冲突感知选择，能有效加速MDLM推理而不显著损失精度。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) enable parallel decoding by predicting all masked positions at each denoising step, yet existing training-free samplers usually decide which positions to commit at token-level granularity. We revisit this granularity and observe that reliable predictions often emerge as contiguous high-confidence spans, suggesting that the unit of parallel commitment can be larger than a single token. We first group adjacent high-confidence candidates into confidence-induced clusters (CICs) as span-level update units. We then use self-attention maps from the same forward pass to estimate inter-cluster dependencies, enabling conflict-aware selection of mutually compatible CICs for parallel commitment. This yields CLAD (Cluster-Level Attention-Guided Decoding), a training-free cluster-level decoder for MDLMs. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream model families across four reasoning and code-generation benchmarks show that CLAD achieves 1.77x--8.47x speedups over Vanilla decoding while maintaining broadly comparable task accuracy in most settings.

</details>


### [141] [Relational Rank Geometry in Transformers: Detecting and Steering Hidden-State Relation Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29634)
*Mazen Kobrosly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer隐藏状态中基于秩的关系几何结构（relation rank geometry），通过Plücker符号熵检测多元关系（r=3,...,6）在隐藏状态空间中留下的方向签名，并在Llama系列模型（8B/70B/405B）中验证其存在性和鲁棒性。进一步通过干净/损坏干预实验证明，这种关系几何可以被定向操控（steer），并行为上恢复正确答案，建立从关系探测到关系框架干预的可控桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer隐藏状态解释主要关注局部或低阶对象（神经元、稀疏特征、注意力头、残差流方向等），缺乏对高阶（多元）关系几何结构的研究。本文旨在探索元组间的秩索引几何（rank-indexed geometry），并验证其可检测性和可操控性。

Method: 1) 使用Plücker符号熵（Plücker sign entropy）检测r元关系在隐藏状态空间中的方向签名一致性，对比真实关系元组与打乱元组的随机控制审计。2) 多模板审计验证表面变化下的鲁棒性。3) 设计边缘网格干净/损坏干预实验（edge-grid clean/corrupt intervention），在保持行/列框架和答案格式不变的情况下改变YES/NO关系图，将损坏隐藏状态的关系框架向干净目标或安慰剂目标修补，并对比质心控制、等范数控制、位点/顺序控制等基线。

Result: 1) 在Llama-8B/70B/405B中，真实关系元组在预期秩k=r（r=3,...,6）处表现出显著的方向签名一致性，打乱元组无此效应。2) 多模板审计中405B所有行均保持正预期秩边际，8B/70B在构造器特异性混合单元中保持正行。3) 70B和405B的干净目标关系框架路径（clean-targeted relation-frame paths）能恢复干净答案行为和残差关系几何，而质心控制、等范数控制等基线恢复效果可忽略或显著低于干净框架路径。

Conclusion: Transformer隐藏状态中存在可检测的、鲁棒的多元关系几何结构（relation rank geometry），并且这种几何结构可以通过关系框架干预进行定向操控，从而行为上影响模型输出。这建立了从关系探测到关系框架干预的受控桥梁，为理解Transformer如何处理高阶关系提供了新的几何视角和验证手段。

Abstract: Transformer hidden states are often interpreted through local or low-order objects: neurons, sparse features, attention heads, residual-stream directions, or activation patches. This paper studies a complementary object: the rank-indexed geometry of relations among token tuples. I use Plucker sign entropy to test whether r-argument relations leave arity-matched orientation signatures in hidden-state space. Across Llama-family 8B, 70B, and 405B checkpoints, true relation tuples show stronger orientation-sign consistency at the expected rank k=r for r=3,...,6 than scrambled tuples under matched random-control audits. Multi-template audits show that the effects survive surface variation, with all tested 405B rows retaining positive expected-rank margins and 8B/70B retaining positive rows with constructor-specific mixed cells. I then ask whether the same relation geometry can be steered. In an edge-grid clean/corrupt intervention assay over 32 prompts, the row/column scaffold and answer format stay fixed while the YES/NO relation map changes, and the corrupt hidden-state relation frame is patched toward clean or placebo targets. In 70B and 405B, clean-targeted relation-frame paths recover clean-answer behavior and residual relation geometry, while centroid-only and equal-norm controls show negligible recovery. Site/order controls further separate marker-site importance from ordered clean-frame geometry: target clean shape and cross-prompt clean shape recover behavior and residual geometry at the marker interface, whereas corrupt-donor transfer, same-site permutation/reflection, wrong-site clean deltas, centroid-only motion, and equal-norm noise fail or remain far below clean-frame paths. The result is a controlled bridge from relation probing to relation-frame intervention: relation rank geometry can be detected, targeted, and behaviorally validated in transformer hidden states.

</details>


### [142] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对随机上下文多臂赌博机中的s-稀疏奖励设置（每个上下文奖励向量的L1范数不超过s），本文设计了两种互补的算法，达到了$\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$的样本复杂度，并给出了匹配的下界，显著改进了先前工作中额外的$\Theta(|A|^9)$依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Erez等人, 2024, 2025）在稀疏奖励设置下的样本复杂度存在额外的$\Theta(|A|^9)$依赖，存在巨大差距需要填补；同时，需要将理论结果推广到实际的组合半赌博机场景（如多类列表分类）。

Method: 1) 基于决策估计系数（DEC）的信息论方法：将上下文赌博机问题转化为结构化观测下的上下文决策制定问题，设计探索-优化算法，利用s-稀疏奖励诱导出紧的DEC界；2) 低方差探索的专用算法方法：设计具体、可计算的算法，并推广到上下文组合半赌博机场景。

Result: 样本复杂度达到$\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$，推广泛化到Natarajan类，并给出匹配的下界（对数因子内最优），从而封闭了先前工作的差距。

Conclusion: 通过两种互补方法解决了稀疏奖励上下文赌博机中的样本复杂度最优性问题，既提供了信息论框架下的最优率分析，又提供了可实际运行的算法，并拓展到组合半赌博机等更广泛场景。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [143] [Opir: Efficient Multi-Task Safety Classification for Toxicity, Jailbreaks, Hate Speech, and Harmful Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29659)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Opir系列编码器护栏模型，基于GLiClass架构，涵盖二分类安全检测、多标签毒性分类、越狱分类及零样本不安全提示/响应分类等任务，训练于996类三级分类体系，在12项安全分类任务和17项类别任务上与8个当代护栏系统对比，在多数基准上达到或超越最先进基线，且部署开销显著更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用需要实时安全过滤，但现有方案面临两难：大型护栏模型成本高，而小模型又难以区分良性敏感文本与真正有害内容。作者旨在构建一组轻量级、高效的编码器护栏模型，在保持高性能的同时降低部署成本。

Method: 基于GLiClass架构构建Opir系列模型，包含多任务模型（二分类、多标签毒性、越狱、零样本分类）和边缘部署变体（<100M参数）。训练数据结合三级分类体系（996类，16顶层/126中层/854叶子标签）下的不安全提示、对抗挖掘的难负样本、良性安全样本、生成响应、多语言翻译及Aegis2/WildGuard子集。同时开源评估框架，支持多种后端和12类安全基准。

Result: 在12项安全分类任务和17项类别任务上，对8个当代护栏系统（含GLiNER2和生成式护栏模型），Opir变体在多数基准数据集上与最强开源基线相当或领先，同时部署规模显著更小。

Conclusion: Opir系列模型证明了轻量级编码器护栏模型有能力在安全过滤任务上匹配甚至超越更大规模生成式模型的表现，同时显著降低部署成本和推理延迟，为实时LLM安全过滤提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time safety filtering for large language model (LLM) applications requires classifiers that can detect unsafe prompts, toxic language, jailbreak attempts, and unsafe responses without the cost profile of large guardrail models, and that can distinguish benign sensitive text from genuinely covert harmful content. In this paper, we introduce Opir, a family of encoder-based guardrail models built on the GLiClass architecture. Opir includes multi-task models for binary safe/unsafe classification, multi-label toxicity classification, jailbreak classification, and zero-shot unsafe prompt and response categorization. We also release edge variants with fewer than 100M parameters dedicated to binary safe/unsafe categorization. The models are trained on a three-level taxonomy containing 996 categories across 16 top-level labels, 126 mid-level labels, and 854 leaf labels. Opir's training data combines taxonomy-grounded unsafe prompts, adversarially mined hard negatives, benign safety-preserving examples, generated response examples, multilingual translations, and portions of the Aegis2 and WildGuard training subsets. We also open-sourced an evaluation harness that supports GLiClass and GLiNER2 backends as well as decoder-based models, and covers binary safety classification, multi-label categorization, toxicity, jailbreak detection, prompt safety, response safety, response refusal, and prompt subcategory views across public benchmark families. Across an expanded comparison spanning 12 safety-classification tasks and 17 category tasks against eight contemporary guardrail systems -- including both GLiNER2-based and generative guardrail models -- Opir variants are competitive on or ahead of the strongest open-weight baselines on the majority of benchmark datasets while operating with a substantially smaller deployment footprint.

</details>


### [144] [A Geometric View of SRC: Learning Representations for Stable Residual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29673)
*Vangelis P. Oikonomou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何塑造的训练方法，在不涉及SRC残差或预测的情况下，通过促进类内自表达、抑制跨类重构路径和类间跨度对齐，来提升稀疏表示分类（SRC）在测试时的残差排序稳定性，并给出了理想化跨度层面的残差边界理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRC作为基于重构残差分类的典型方法，其可靠性高度依赖于学习到的表示的几何结构。现有方法缺乏对残差排序稳定性的理论刻画，以及引导训练过程塑造有利几何结构的明确目标。

Method: 采用严格的训练-测试分离策略：SRC仅作为固定的测试时规则，训练中不进行微分或展开。提出跨度层面的理想化分析框架，定义残差间隔（residual margin）来形式化残差排序稳定性，并刻画三类几何障碍（跨度重叠、主导、通过小主角的接近重叠）。在此基础上，设计几何塑造目标：促进掩码类内自表达、抑制跨类重构路径和类间跨度对齐、防止坍缩，训练中不使用SRC残差或预测。

Result: 在COIL-100（图像）、TREC（文本）和EEG连接数据上，所有表示在相同的固定SRC/OMP推理下进行评估，报告了残差间隔和几何诊断指标。交叉熵作为参考几何在相同评估协议下进行比较。

Conclusion: 跨度层面的理论分析揭示了残差间隔坍缩的几何成因，所提出的几何塑造目标能够在不依赖SRC训练信号的情况下改善表示结构，提升测试时残差分类的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based inference assigns a class by comparing class-wise reconstruction residuals; Sparse Representation Classification (SRC) is a canonical instance whose reliability depends on the geometry of the learned representation. We adopt a strict training-inference separation: SRC is used only as a fixed test-time rule and is never differentiated, unrolled, or optimized during training. In a span-level idealization based on class-conditional spans and their associated projection residuals, we formalize residual-ordering stability through a residual margin and characterize geometric obstructions -- span overlap, dominance, and near-overlap via small principal angles -- that can collapse this margin in worst-case directions. This span-level theory is primary: it specifies when the idealized residual family is well-separated, and it provides a conditional solver-level interpretation for practical residual approximations (e.g., OMP) insofar as they remain close to the span-level residual ordering. Under explicit coverage and separation assumptions, we derive a quantitative lower bound on the (idealized) residual margin. Guided by these targets, we propose geometry-shaping objectives that promote masked within-class self-expressiveness, discourage cross-class reconstruction pathways and inter-class span alignment, and prevent collapse -- without invoking SRC residuals or predictions during training. Experiments on images (COIL-100), text (TREC), and EEG connectivity evaluate all representations under identical fixed SRC/OMP inference and report residual margins and geometric diagnostics; cross-entropy is included only as a reference geometry under the same evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [145] [A Systematic Evaluation of Molecular Mixture Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29698)
*Roel J. Leenhouts,Nathan K. Morgan,William Green,Jan G. Rittig,Florence H. Vermeire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分解混合物性质预测误差的评估框架，将误差拆分为纯化合物贡献和非理想相互作用贡献，并构建了7个匹配的纯组分-混合物数据集。实验发现，高绝对精度可能掩盖对非理想混合行为的差恢复能力，且在严格分子分割下性能显著下降，表明向未见分子迁移是混合物机器学习的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测机器学习主要关注纯化合物，而实际应用常涉及混合物及分子间相互作用。已有混合物数据集增多，但评估仍只关注绝对精度，无法区分纯组分贡献与非理想混合偏差。

Method: 提出一个误差分解评估框架，包含：(1)泄漏感知的数据分割协议；(2)理想混合基线模型；(3)过量性质(excess-property)度量指标。同时构建了7个匹配的纯组分与混合物物理化学性质数据集。

Result: 高绝对精度可能掩盖对非理想混合行为的差恢复能力；在严格分子分割条件下模型性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 向未见分子迁移是分子混合物机器学习中的核心挑战，评估应超越绝对精度，关注非理想混合行为的捕捉能力。

Abstract: Machine learning for molecular property prediction has focused largely on pure compounds, even though many practical applications depend on mixtures with intermolecular interactions. Recent work has expanded the availability of mixture datasets, but evaluation still focuses mainly on absolute accuracy. However, absolute errors in mixtures conflate pure-component contributions with deviations from ideal mixing. We propose an evaluation framework that decomposes mixture-property error into pure-compound and interaction (non-ideal) components. The framework combines leakage-aware split protocols, ideal-mixture baselines, and excess-property metrics. To support reproducible benchmarking, we curate seven matched pure and mixture physicochemical property datasets. Across multiple mixture-property tasks and model families, we find that strong absolute accuracy can mask poor recovery of non-ideal mixture behavior, and that performance drops substantially under strict molecule splits. These results identify transfer to unseen molecules as a central challenge in molecular mixture machine learning and motivate evaluation beyond absolute accuracy alone.

</details>


### [146] [The Little Book of Generative AI Foundations: An Intuitive Mathematical Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29713)
*Tianhua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书提供了从PCA到能量模型等现代生成式AI数学基础的紧凑、面向推导的导论，旨在使生成建模的结构更易于理解而不丧失数学实质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为数学好奇的研究者、从业者和学生构建生成式AI的数学基础，弥合对生成模型理解的门槛。

Method: 采用面向推导的连贯路线，从PCA、概率PCA开始，依次连接变分自编码器、扩散模型、归一化流、自回归分解、GANs、Wasserstein GANs和基于能量的模型。

Result: 形成了一本紧凑的导论性著作，梳理了主要生成模型家族之间的数学联系。

Conclusion: 该书作为基础构建的入门材料，有助于读者理解生成模型的推导过程及其相互关系，而不仅仅是罗列最新架构或实现细节。

Abstract: This book provides a compact, derivation-oriented introduction to the mathematical foundations of modern generative artificial intelligence. Rather than surveying every recent architecture or implementation detail, it develops a coherent route through the ideas connecting major families of generative models, from PCA, probabilistic PCA, variational autoencoders, and diffusion models to normalising flows, autoregressive factorisations, GANs, Wasserstein GANs, and energy-based models. The aim is to make the structure of generative modelling more accessible without removing the mathematical substance needed to understand how these models are derived and related. The book is intended as a foundation-building primer for mathematically curious researchers, practitioners, and students.

</details>


### [147] [EMAG: Differentiable 4D Gaussian Mixture Splatting for EEG Spatial Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29731)
*Alex Lazarovich,Ofir Itzhak Shahar,Gur Elkin,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMAG框架，通过各向异性4D时空高斯混合模型从稀疏低密度电极重建高密度脑电信号，在三个公开基准上以2-16倍超分辨率因子达到SOTA性能，并具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD-EEG硬件成本高、设置时间长，限制了其在临床和研究中的可及性，因此需要从稀疏低密度电极重建高密度信号的方法。

Method: 提出EMAG，将脑电源表示为各向异性4D时空高斯的混合，每个高斯由4x4精度矩阵参数化，实现空间各向异性扩散及时空耦合；通过可微高斯场在电极位置的前向渲染实现端到端训练，无需显式源定位监督。

Result: 在Localize-MI、SEED、SEED-IV三个公开基准上，EMAG在大多数超分辨率因子（2x至8/16x）下超越当前SOTA方法。

Conclusion: EMAG不仅实现了优越的EEG超分辨率性能，其显式高斯参数化还支持脑源配置的可视化与可解释性，为源定位和生物标志物发现等临床应用开辟了途径。

Abstract: High-density electroencephalography (HD-EEG) enables fine-grained measurement of cortical activity but requires expensive hardware and lengthy setup times, limiting its clinical and research accessibility. We propose EMAG (EEG Mixture of Anisotropic Gaussians), a differentiable framework that reconstructs HD-EEG signals from a sparse subset of low-density (LD) electrodes by representing brain electrical sources as a mixture of anisotropic 4D space-time Gaussians. EMAG places a mixture of multiple Gaussians at each point of a spherical brain grid, each parameterized by a full 4 x 4 precision matrix, enabling anisotropic spatial spreads and explicit coupling between spatial and temporal dimensions. The forward model renders scalp EEG via differentiable Gaussian field contributions at electrode locations, enabling end-to-end training without explicit source localization supervision. We evaluate EMAG on three public EEG benchmarks (Localize-MI, SEED, and SEED-IV) at super-resolution factors of 2x through 8/16x. EMAG outperforms the current state-of-the-art EEG super-resolution method at most super-resolution factors on three standard benchmarks (Localize-MI, SEED, SEED-IV). The explicit Gaussian parameterization further enables direct visualization and interpretability of learned brain source configurations, potentially opening avenues for clinical and neuroscientific applications, such as source localization or biomarker discovery.

</details>


### [148] [MMTM: Tri-Modal Topic Modeling for Long-Form Video via Similarity-Gated Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29765)
*Ali Abusaleh,Bhuvanesh Verma,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MMTM是一个模块化流水线，通过语音识别、音频/视觉嵌入和BERTopic聚类，结合确定性相似度门控融合，实现长视频主题发现。在三模态联合建模下，主题质量显著提升：噪声从0.27降至0.06，转换率从0.70降至0.21，归一化熵从0.84升至0.92；聚类有效性提升5-12倍。同时发布了代码和54小时人工验证的多模态视频主题语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中的主题发现是一个挑战，现有方法往往只依赖单一模态（如仅文本或仅视觉），难以充分利用多模态信息来生成连贯且时域稳定的主题。

Method: 提出MMTM模块化流水线，集成语音识别、音频与视觉嵌入，通过确定性相似度门控融合策略将多模态特征与BERTopic聚类结合，实现三模态联合主题发现。

Result: 在德语（Tagesschau）和英语（NBC）新闻广播上跨语言评估：噪声从0.27降至0.06，转换率从0.70降至0.21，归一化熵从0.84升至0.92；Calinski-Harabasz指数提升5-12倍；德语NPMI从0.77升至0.86，但英语语料效果未迁移。发布代码和54小时人工验证语料库。

Conclusion: 三模态联合建模相比单模态显著提升了主题的连贯性和时域稳定性，聚类有效性大幅提升；但词汇连贯性提升依赖于语料特性（长文本）。

Abstract: We introduce MMTM, a modular pipeline for topic discovery in long-form video that integrates speech recognition, audio and visual embeddings, and BERTopic clustering through a deterministic similarity-gated fusion. Evaluated cross-lingually on German (Tagesschau) and English (NBC) broadcast news, joint tri-modal modeling substantially improves topic quality: noise drops from 0.27 to 0.06, transition rate from 0.70 to 0.21, and normalized entropy rises from 0.84 to 0.92, indicating more coherent and temporally stable topics. Cluster validity (Calinski-Harabasz) improves by 5-12X across embedding spaces. Lexical coherence (NPMI) rises from 0.77 to 0.86 on German but is corpus-dependent and does not transfer to the shorter NBC broadcasts. We release the pipeline code and a human-validated 54-hour multimodal video topic corpus with dual-annotator visual evaluation and LLM-assisted labeling.

</details>


### [149] [Hista and Numca: Estimate State Value Effectively for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29782)
*Zizhe Chen,Jiqian Dong,Yizhou Tian,Garry Yang,Yongqiang Chen,Zhitang Chen,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态值估计基准SVEB，发现PPO中的critic退化为粗粒度分组平均基线。为此提出Numca（利用数值跨度作为可分级里程碑）和Hista（利用LLM隐藏状态加权平均不连续轨迹及其回报）两种方法，在不显著增加计算开销的情况下提升状态值估计准确性和训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管状态值估计对强化学习训练的稳定性至关重要，但在LLM后训练中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1) 提出SVEB基准评估现有RL框架中的状态估计；2) Numca利用数值跨度作为可分级里程碑进行状态值估计；3) Hista利用LLM隐藏状态作为表示，对不连续的轨迹及其回报进行加权平均。

Result: 两种方法均能获得更准确的状态值估计，并在不同RL算法和模型规模下提升训练性能，且未引入显著计算开销。

Conclusion: 状态值估计在LLM后训练中至关重要，所提方法通过更精准的值估计有效提升了RL训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) refines large language models (LLMs) by directly optimizing model behavior through reward signals. While accurate state value estimation is critical for stable training in classical RL, it remains an underexplored challenge in LLM post-training. In this work, we introduce the State Value Estimation Benchmark (SVEB) to assess state estimation within existing RL frameworks and show that critics in standard approaches like PPO collapse to a coarse group-average baseline. To address this, we propose two techniques: Numca, which leverages numerical spans as gradable milestones for state value estimation, and Hista, a framework that uses LLM's hidden states as representation to weighted average disjoint rollouts and their return. Extensive experiments demonstrate that both methods yield more accurate state value estimates and enhance training performance across different RL algorithms and model sizes without incurring significant computational overhead.

</details>


### [150] [The Interplay Between Interpolation and Aggregation in Regression: Optimal Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29819)
*Mikael Møller Høgsgaard,Kasper Green Larsen,Liang-Yu Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了回归问题中插值与聚合之间的相互作用。证明了 γ-图维度可以刻画一大类自然聚合过程的可学习性。此外，证明了通过中位数聚合三个插值假设这一极其简单的聚合过程在这类聚合过程中是最优的，并且严格优于适当学习。最后，展示了某些假设类只能通过聚合无限多个假设或使用非插值聚合规则来学习，而任何有限的插值聚合都无法达到哪怕是最基本的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究回归问题中插值（interpolation）与聚合（aggregation）之间的理论相互作用，特别是探究不同聚合过程的学习能力极限。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，引入γ-图维度（γ-graph dimension）来刻画可学习性，并分析了多种聚合过程，包括中位数聚合等。

Result: (1) γ-图维度可以刻画一大类自然聚合过程的可学习性；(2) 通过中位数聚合三个插值假设是最优的聚合过程，且严格优于适当学习；(3) 某些假设类只能通过聚合无限多个假设或非插值聚合规则学习，有限插值聚合无法达到基本性能。

Conclusion: 揭示了回归中插值与聚合之间的理论边界，证明了简单的中位数聚合方法的最优性，并指出了有限插值聚合的根本局限性。

Abstract: This work investigates theoretically the interplay between interpolation and aggregation in regression. We establish that the $γ$-graph dimension characterizes learnability for a broad class of natural aggregation procedures. Furthermore, we prove that an extremely simple aggregation procedure, combining three interpolating hypotheses via the median, is optimal among all these aggregation procedures, and is strictly more powerful than proper learning. Finally, we show that some hypothesis classes are learnable only by aggregating infinitely many hypotheses or by using non-interpolating aggregation rules (which may predict outside the range of their inputs), and any finite interpolating aggregation fails to achieve even trivial performance.

</details>


### [151] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有错误切片发现方法（SDMs）往往生成与模型推理过程脱节的解释、仅能近似误差来源的问题，本文利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）直接依赖可理解语义概念的特性，提出CB-SLICE方法。该方法通过检测共享概念预测失败的样本来发现错误切片，并识别导致每个切片失败模式的关键概念。实验表明，CB-SLICE在发现已知偏差方面优于现有方法，并提供更丰富、更忠实的错误解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误切片发现方法（SDMs）生成的解释与模型推理过程脱节，只能近似逼近误差根源，导致不准确。需要一种能够直接关联模型推理过程的error slice识别方法。

Method: 利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的预测直接依赖人类可理解语义概念的特性；提出CB-SLICE方法：将共享概念预测失败的样本进行分组（形成error slice），并识别每个切片失败模式中最关键的关键词概念。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，CB-SLICE在发现已知偏差方面优于现有最先进方法，并提供更丰富、更忠实的模型错误解释。

Conclusion: 概念瓶颈模型中的概念表示能够为error slice识别提供直接链接到误差来源的细粒度解释，CB-SLICE是一种更准确且可解释性更强的错误切片发现方法。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [152] [HARP: Hadamard-Preconditioned Adaptive Rotation Processor for Extreme LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29843)
*Artur Zagitov,Gleb Molodtsov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARP 提出了一种可学习的结构化双面正交处理器，替代 PTQ 中固定的随机 Hadamard 变换，通过稀疏蝴蝶状块正交阶段和混合基数调度适配非2幂维度，在保持精确全精度等价的同时，实现逐层与后端自适应的量化基旋转，在2-4比特设置下提升困惑度和零样本准确率，并保持高效部署（128 tok/s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于非相干性的 PTQ 方法使用固定的随机 Hadamard 变换（RHT）来缓解激活异常值和各向异性权重曲率对低比特量化的影响，但固定变换无法根据层、校准分布或量化器自适应调整旋转基，限制了量化鲁棒性的进一步提升。

Method: HARP 设计了一个可学习的结构化双面正交处理器，将旋转矩阵分解为稀疏蝴蝶状块正交阶段的乘积，支持非2幂维度（混合基数调度），初始化时与 RHT 处理器仅差一个固定置换。该处理器仅在校准数据上拟合，实现逐层和后端自适应的量化基旋转。

Result: 在1B到70B参数规模的模型上，2-4比特设置下，HARP 在困惑度和零样本准确率上均优于固定 RHT，且部署效率达到128 tok/s（对比 FP16 的61 tok/s）。

Conclusion: HARP 证明了可学习的结构化正交变换能够有效替代 PTQ 中固定的随机旋转，在保持计算效率和全精度等价的前提下，显著提升极端低比特量化的性能。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying LLMs under memory and bandwidth constraints. However, extreme low-bit quantization remains highly sensitive to activation outliers and anisotropic weight curvature. Existing incoherence-based PTQ methods mitigate this issue with fixed randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which improve quantization robustness but cannot adapt the rotated basis to the layer, calibration distribution, or quantizer. We introduce HARP (Hadamard-preconditioned Adaptive Rotation Processor), a learnable structured two-sided orthogonal processor that replaces fixed Hadamard mixing while preserving exact full-precision equivalence. HARP represents each rotation as a product of sparse butterfly-like block-orthogonal stages, supports non-power-of-two dimensions via Mixed-Radix schedules, and initializes to the RHT processor up to a fixed permutation. Fitted only on calibration data, HARP adapts the quantization basis to each layer and backend. Across 2-4 bit settings on models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, HARP improves perplexity and zero-shot accuracy over fixed RHT. Importantly, HARP preserves deployment efficiency, reaching 128 tok/s versus 61 tok/s for FP16.

</details>


### [153] [Feedback-to-Rubrics: Can We Learn Expert Criteria from Inline Comments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29857)
*Kotaro Yoshida,So Kuroki,Yuki Imajuku,Taishi Nakamura,Ryunosuke Iwai,Haruki Goda,Takuya Akiba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从人工或LLM生成的草稿中的内联评论学习可重复使用的自然语言评分标准（rubrics）的方法，并通过迭代优化减少评分标准预测与实际评论之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在写作和审阅中的实用性依赖于上下文相关的隐性标准（如专家偏好或组织规范），这些标准难以直接获取，因此需要从已有的内联评论中自动学习可复用的评分标准。

Method: 从积累的内联评论中推断评分标准，并通过观察基于评分标准的预测与参考评论之间的逐评论不匹配，迭代优化评分标准。

Result: 在真实审阅场景和受控场景下均验证了该方法能够将内联评论蒸馏为可复用的评分标准，并支持评论预测、评分标准理解和自动稿件修订。

Conclusion: 内联评论可以有效地蒸馏为可复用的评分标准，从而提升LLM在审阅辅助中的实用性和适应性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for writing and review support, but their usefulness depends on context-dependent criteria, such as expert preferences or organization-specific conventions, that are often tacit, undocumented, and difficult to elicit directly. We propose a problem setting for learning reusable natural-language rubrics from accumulated inline comments on artifacts such as human-written or LLM-generated drafts. Our method infers rubrics from these comments and iteratively refines them by observing comment-wise mismatches between rubric-conditioned predictions and reference comments. We evaluate the proposed method in real-world review settings and in controlled settings with reference rubrics. These results show that inline comments can be distilled into reusable rubrics that support comment prediction, rubric understanding, and automatic artifact revision.

</details>


### [154] [STAP: A Shuffle-Tokenized App Predictor with Ultra Long Context for Vocabulary-Free Mobile App Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29863)
*Chengyu Fan,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAP模型，基于Transformer和随机洗牌机制，消除固定应用词汇表依赖，实现跨数据集零样本预测和冷启动场景下的移动应用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动应用预测模型依赖固定应用词汇表，无法跨不同应用生态系统泛化；且许多模型依赖用户特定知识，在冷启动场景下部署困难。

Method: STAP采用Transformer架构，通过洗牌机制将真实应用身份替换为随机分配的虚拟索引，并利用超长上下文设计处理行为序列以补偿丢弃的语义信息。

Result: 在两个跨洲数据集上实现强跨数据集零样本预测准确性（现有固定词汇表方法均不适用），在各自数据集内冷启动性能与领先模型相当。

Conclusion: 理论分析证明给定足够长的上下文，尽管映射是匿名的，预测分布仍能收敛到正确分布；同时引入的部署策略可在连续推理中保持足够长上下文且延迟可接受。

Abstract: Predicting the next mobile application a user will launch is essential for intelligent device resource management and proactive assistance. Existing models rely on fixed app vocabularies, which prevents them from generalizing across different app ecosystems. Many also depend on user-specific knowledge, which complicates deployment in cold start scenarios. We propose STAP, a Transformer-based model that eliminates the need for a fixed vocabulary. STAP replaces true app identities with randomly reassigned virtual indices via a shuffle mechanism, and compensates for discarded semantic information by processing behavioral sequences with an ultra-long context design. A theoretical analysis shows that, given a sufficiently long context, the predicted distribution converges to the correct one despite the anonymity of the mapping. Experiments on two datasets from different continents demonstrate that STAP achieves strong cross-dataset zero-shot prediction accuracy -- a setting where all existing fixed-vocabulary methods are inherently inapplicable -- while its cold start performance within each dataset remains competitive with leading models. Furthermore, we introduce a deployment strategy that enables the model to retain a sufficiently long context during continuous inference while keeping latency within acceptable bounds.

</details>


### [155] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Cayley表补全作为离散代数规则外推的基准测试问题，类比矩阵补全中的隐式几何偏置，指出张量分解结合平坦性先验能产生对离散结合律的隐式算法偏置，并呼吁建立精确恢复的理论界及扩展至更广泛的离散公理发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习在连续领域表现优异，但在精确算法规则或离散代数规则的外推上持续失败，说明缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的隐式偏置。作者希望建立Cayley表补全这一基准问题，推动该方向的研究。

Method: 将Cayley表补全类比于矩阵补全：矩阵分解+权重衰减→低线性秩的几何偏置；算子值张量分解+平坦性先验→对离散结合律的算法偏置。

Result: 提出Cayley表补全作为离散代数的标准测试问题；指出已有初步结果（平坦性先验可诱导结合律偏置）；但缺乏形式化的精确恢复理论界。

Conclusion: 呼吁建立Cayley表补全的精确恢复理论界，并挑战社区将连续平坦性先验推广到自动发现更广泛的离散算法公理，无需组合搜索。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [156] [LaRA: Layer-wise Representation Analysis for Detecting Data Contamination in RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29888)
*Minju Gwak,Minseo Kwak,Dongseok Lee,Guijin Son,Alan Ritter,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LaRA框架，通过层表示分析检测RL后训练阶段LLM中的数据污染，利用扰动敏感性、方向坍缩和局部表示刚性三种互补指标，在推理模型上优于传统输出级检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练能提升LLM推理能力，但数据污染问题鲜有研究，可能损害泛化能力和评估可靠性。现有基于输出信号（似然/熵）的检测方法对RL训练模型不可靠，因为RL通过轨迹级奖励而非token似然塑造行为。

Method: 提出LaRA框架，包含三种互补指标：扰动敏感性、方向坍缩、局部表示刚性，在控制扰动下测量层表示几何偏差，并跨层和指标聚合构建检测协议。

Result: 污染会导致渐进式跨层几何偏差：扰动敏感性放大、方向坍缩增强、局部刚性提升。在RL训练的推理模型上，LaRA的检测协议优于现有输出级基线方法。

Conclusion: 层表示分析能有效检测RL后训练LLM中的数据污染，LaRA框架为这一开放问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has shown to improve reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, there has been little exploration on the problem of data contamination in RL post-training, potentially undermining generalization and evaluation reliability of the training process itself. Existing detection methods primarily rely on output-level signals such as likelihood or entropy, which become unreliable for RL-trained models since RL shapes behavior through trajectory-level rewards rather than token likelihoods.
