Abstract: Learning-assisted algorithm design often has to make reliable search decisions under small evaluation budgets, where committing to a single metaheuristic can be unreliable. We propose WASHH, a Whale-guided Adaptive Selection Hyper-Heuristic for continuous black-box optimization. WASHH uses WOA as the main exploitation backbone, but treats PSO-style memory, GWO-style leader averaging, DE-style variation, local coordinate search, and anchor-guided refinement as selectable search behaviors. An online reward controller allocates evaluations according to observed improvements, while anchor refinement exploits inexpensive reference configurations such as box centers or default model settings without bypassing black-box evaluation. On ten 30-dimensional benchmark functions with 10 independent runs and 12,000 evaluations, WASHH achieves the best average rank, 1.10, and is best or tied best on all ten functions. It strictly improves over WOA on eight functions and ties WOA at the numerical optimum on Rastrigin and Griewank. We further study SVC hyperparameter configuration for breast cancer diagnosis under a 300-evaluation budget. WASHH obtains the lowest mean validation log loss among the compared optimizers, suggesting that anchor-aware selection hyper-heuristics are a practical lightweight direction for LEAD systems.

</details>


### [195] [EvoGM: Learning to Merge LLMs via Evolutionary Generative Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29295)
*Tao Jiang,Xinmeng Yu,Chenhao Yi,Yiling Wu,Yan Li,Ran Cheng,Dongmei Jiang,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出EvoGM框架，通过可学习的生成式建模（双生成器+循环一致性学习）替代传统随机算子进行模型合并系数优化，在多轮进化流程中迭代提升合并质量，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化模型合并方法依赖随机、人工设计的算子，忽略了系数空间的性能分布结构，缺乏对合并系数优化的系统性学习机制。

Method: 提出EvoGM框架，采用双生成器架构与循环一致性学习，从历史搜索轨迹构建胜者-败者配对，自适应采样并优化合并候选参数，集成到多轮进化流程中。

Result: 在多种基准上显著超越现有最优基线，在已见和未见任务上均表现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 可学习的生成式建模能有效替代手工设计的启发式操作，提升模型合并的数据效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Evolutionary model merging provides a powerful framework for the automated, training-free composition of LLMs through parameter-space search. However, existing methods predominantly rely on stochastic, hand-crafted operators that overlook the underlying performance landscape of the coefficient space. We propose Evolutionary Generative Merging (EvoGM), a framework that transcends manual heuristics by employing learnable generative modeling to optimize merging coefficients. Specifically, EvoGM features a dual-generator architecture with cycle-consistent learning to adaptively sample and refine promising merging candidates. By constructing winner-loser pairs from historical search trajectories, our framework effectively captures high-performance parameter distributions and maximizes data efficiency. This generative process is seamlessly integrated into a multi-round evolutionary pipeline, where elite merged models iteratively serve as new expert foundations. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that EvoGM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, exhibiting robust performance on both seen and unseen tasks. Code and data are available at https://github.com/JiangTao97/evogm.

</details>


### [196] [Runtime Analysis of a Compact Genetic Algorithm on a Truly Multi-valued OneMax Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29477)
*Martin S. Krejca,Carsten Witt*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文改进了多值紧凑遗传算法（cGA）在G-OneMax函数上的运行时间上界，从O(n r³ log²(n) log(r))降至O(n r log³(n) log³(r))，匹配了仅依赖两个值的简单多值OneMax函数的已知界（仅差多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多值分布估计算法的运行时分析此前局限于每个维度仅区分两类值的多值目标函数，本质上仍可用伪布尔方法处理。Adak和 Witt（GECCO 2025）首次对真正依赖全部r个类别的多值OneMax函数进行了cGA运行时分析，但所得界不够紧，本文旨在改进该结果。

Method: 利用改进的具有高自环概率过程的漂移定理，以及专门推导的浓度不等式，分析多值cGA中概率质量如何逐步向r值频率矩阵的越来越小的区间移动。

Result: 将运行时间上界从O(n r³ log²(n) log(r))改进为O(n r log³(n) log³(r))（在更新强度K最优选择下），与仅依赖两个值的简单r值OneMax函数的已知界仅差多对数因子。

Conclusion: 本文显著缩小了多值cGA在完全多值问题与二元简化问题之间的运行时间分析差距，所得界在多项式因子上与简单情形匹配。

Abstract: Recently, the runtime analysis of multi-valued estimation-of-distribution algorithms in the framework of Ben Jedidia et al. (TCS 2024) has made significant advancements. However, almost all existing analyses are limited to multi-valued objective functions that in each dimension only distinguish between two types, also called categories, of values and hence can be treated with similar methods as pseudo-Boolean problems. Only recently, Adak and Witt (GECCO 2025) have presented a first runtime analysis of a multi-valued compact genetic algorithm (cGA) on the multi-valued OneMax function G-OneMax$\colon \{0,\dots,r-1\}^n \to \mathbf{N}$ defined by G-OneMax$(x_1,\dots,x_n)=\sum_{i=1}^n {x}_i$ and truly depending on all $r$ categories. We improve their runtime result from $\textrm{O}\bigl(n r^3 \log^2( n)\log (r)\bigr)$ to $\textrm{O}\bigl(n r \log^3(n)\log^3(r)\bigr)$, both for an optimal choice of the update strength $K$. Our result matches, up to polylogarithmic factors, the existing bound for the simpler $r$-valued OneMax function depending essentially only on two values and analyzed in several previous works. To show the new bound, we use improved drift theorems for processes with high self-loop probabilities and specifically derived concentration inequalities to analyze how probability mass in the multi-valued cGA moves into successively smaller and smaller intervals of the $r$-valued frequency matrix.

</details>


### [197] [Selection Hyper-heuristics Can Automatically Adjust the Learning Period to Optimally Solve Pseudo-Boolean Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29916)
*Benjamin Doerr,Pietro S. Oliveto,John Alasdair Warwicker*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文针对随机梯度超启发式算法在优化LeadingOnes基准问题时，自动设置学习周期参数τ的问题进行了研究，证明了该算法能以1-o(1)的概率选择最优邻域大小，并达到理论最优收敛时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度超启发式算法在优化LeadingOnes问题时需要手动设置学习周期参数τ，而τ的选取并非 trivial 任务。本文旨在自动设置该参数，减轻用户负担。

Method: 提出一种自动设置学习周期τ的方法，使得超启发式算法能以1-o(1)的概率选择最优邻域大小，并分析其优化LeadingOnes问题的时间复杂度。

Result: 证明了该超启发式算法能在1-o(1)的迭代比例中选出最优邻域大小，并以最优时间（忽略低阶项）完成LeadingOnes基准的优化。

Conclusion: 本文实现了随机梯度超启发式算法中学习周期参数的自动设置，在LeadingOnes问题上达到了理论最优性能。

Abstract: The Random Gradient hyper-heuristic was recently shown to be able to learn the optimal neighbourhood size when optimizing the LeadingOnes benchmark via the Randomised Local Search (RLS) meta-heuristic. However, for this to happen, a learning period of a certain length $τ$ had to be used, differently from classic hyper-heuristics, which change their behaviour based on the success of only the previous iteration. In this paper, we show how to automatically set this new parameter value, relieving the user from the non-trivial task of controlling this novel algorithm parameter. We prove that the resulting hyper-heuristic selects the optimal neighbourhood size in a $1-o(1)$ fraction of the iterations and, consequently, optimises the LeadingOnes benchmark in the best possible time (apart from lower-order terms) achievable with these neighborhood sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Unifying Semantic Path Order and Weighted Path Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29393)
*Teppei Saito,Nao Hirokawa*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文将单调语义路径序和加权路径序统一为归约序与归约对，并探讨其作为基项全序归约序的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调语义路径序和加权路径序是证明项重写系统终止性的强大归约序，但两者尚未被统一处理，缺乏一个统一的框架来整合它们的优势。

Method: 提出一种统一的归约序与归约对形式，将单调语义路径序和加权路径序纳入同一框架，并讨论其作为基项全序归约序的适用性。

Result: 成功实现了两种路径序在归约序与归约对层面的简单统一，并验证了其在基项全序场景下的可用性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架简化了两种路径序的理论基础，并为项重写系统的终止性证明提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: Monotonic semantic path orders and weighted path orders are powerful reduction orders for proving termination of term rewrite systems. In this paper we present their simple unification as reduction orders and reduction pairs. We also discuss the use of it as ground total reduction orders.

</details>


### [199] [Strong (D)QBF Dependency Schemes via Pure Paths with Applications to Proof Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29763)
*Leroy Chew,Tomáš Peitl*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dpure依赖方案，将其添加到DQRAT证明系统中可使其与最强大的(D)QBF证明系统p等价，并实现了原型检查器DQRAT-check，验证了Dpure在求解器Qute中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的(D)QBF认证面临挑战，大多数技术可通过独立扩展规则p模拟，但现代RAT格式尚未达到该规则的强度，DQRAT证明系统仅停留在理论层面。

Method: 提出名为Dpure的新型依赖方案，将其添加到DQRAT证明系统中，证明添加后DQRAT与Independent Extended QU-Res p等价；实现原型检查器DQRAT-check；将Dpure集成到依赖学习求解器Qute中验证其性质。

Result: Dpure的加入使DQRAT证明系统与最强大的已知(D)QBF证明系统p等价；DQRAT-check检查器原型实现成功；Dpure具有先前依赖方案的其他性质，并成功集成到Qute求解器中。

Conclusion: Dpure是弥合理论证明系统与实用RAT格式之间差距的关键成分，推动了(D)QBF认证从理论到实践的进展。

Abstract: Certification for Quantified Boolean Formulas (QBF) and Dependency Quantified Boolean Formulas (DQBF) is an ongoing challenge. Recent proof complexity work has shown that the majority of QBF and DQBF techniques can be p-simulated by using the independent extension rule.
  In propositional logic, extension rules are supported by proof checkers using a more general RAT (Resolution Asymmetric Tautology) rule. The next step in (D)QBF certification would be to update these modern RAT formats to match the strength of this independent extension rule.
  In this paper we first introduce a new dependency scheme called Dpure. This rule is the missing ingredient that when added to Blinkhorn's proof system DQRAT allows it to be provably p-equivalent to the Independent Extended QU-Res, the most powerful of the known QBF and DQBF proof systems. Up until now, DQRAT has only existed in theory, so we implement a prototype checker DQRAT-check which includes our extra rule. In addition to its inclusion in our proof checker we show Dpure has other properties that have been found for previous dependency schemes, and each of these observations has potential in solving/checking including the sound integration into the dependency learning solver Qute.

</details>


### [200] [A Rust-to-Lean Verification Pipeline with AI Provers: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30106)
*Natalia Klaus,Palina Tolmach,Juan Conejero*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个将 Rust 密码学代码转换为 Lean 4 中机器可验证正确性证明的流水线，该流水线结合了符号提取工具、形式化密码学规范库和 AI 证明器，所有证明均由 Lean 内核检验。在以太坊基金会 zkEVM 验证项目中，该流水线成功应用于 Plonky3 和 RISC Zero 的密码学原语，AI 还独立完成了两个此前标记为 "sorry" 的定理证明。论文详细描述了架构、运行实例、AI 可自动完成的证明类别及仍需人工处理的部分，并讨论了工程上的差距与发现的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低 Rust 密码学代码形式化验证的准入门槛，并探索如何更有效地在验证流水线中利用 AI 证明器来自动化证明义务的闭合，从而加速形式化验证在工业级密码学库中的应用。

Method: 构建了一条集成化的验证流水线，包含三个组件：(1) 符号提取工具（Charon/Aeneas 或 Hax）将 Rust 代码提升到 Lean 4；(2) 形式化密码学规范库（ArkLib 和 CompPoly）提供数学目标定义；(3) AI 证明器（Aristotle 和 Aleph）自动闭合产生的证明义务。所有证明最终由 Lean 内核独立验证以确保可靠性。

Result: 成功对 Plonky3（FRI folding、Mersenne31 和 KoalaBear 域算术、Horner 多项式求值）和 RISC Zero（Merkle 包含性验证）中的密码学原语进行了形式化验证。Aleph 独立完成了 Plonky3 中 compute_log_arity_for_round 两个边界风格定理的证明（此前为 sorry 状态）。论文还系统报告了哪些类证明义务可由 AI 自动闭合、哪些需要人工介入，以及发现的工程问题（工具链漂移、提取限制、缺失引理、策略差距、代码生成摩擦点）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一条结合符号提取、形式化规范和 AI 证明的 Rust 形式化验证流水线是可行的，且 AI 可以自主完成部分证明义务。论文指出的工程差距和发现的缺陷为后续改进提供了明确方向。作者希望该贡献能降低形式化验证的采用门槛并促进 AI 在验证流水线中的更有效使用。

Abstract: We describe a verification pipeline that takes production Rust cryptographic code and produces machine-checked correctness proofs in Lean 4. The pipeline combines three components: symbolic extraction tools (Charon and Aeneas, or Hax) that lift Rust into Lean 4; formal cryptographic specification libraries (ArkLib and CompPoly, from the Verified zkEVM project) that provide the mathematical targets; and AI provers (Aristotle from Harmonic AI and Aleph from Logical Intelligence) that close the resulting proof obligations. Every proof is checked by the Lean kernel, so AI output cannot compromise soundness.
  Within the scope of the Ethereum Foundation's zkEVM Verification Project, we applied the pipeline to cryptographic primitives in Plonky3 (FRI folding, Mersenne31 and KoalaBear field arithmetic, Horner polynomial evaluation) and RISC Zero (Merkle inclusion verification). In addition, Aleph authored proofs of two bounds-style theorems in Plonky3's compute_log_arity_for_round that previously stood as sorry.
  The paper describes the architecture, walks through a running example based on Aleph's two proofs, reports which classes of proof obligations AI closed and which required manual work, and discusses the engineering gaps we encountered: Lean 4 toolchain drift across tools and specific Aeneas/Hax extraction limits. We also document concrete missing lemmas, tactic gaps, and code-generation friction points discovered during proof development. We hope this contribution lowers the barrier to adoption of formal verification and facilitates more effective use of AI in this pipeline. The result is a working pipeline for formal verification of Rust, with kernel-checked proofs and reproducible artefacts.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [TraceCodec: A Compiler-Backed Neural Codec for Stateful Multi-Flow Network Traffic Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29941)
*Junhui Ding,Xinchen Zhang,Xiaohui Xie,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: TraceCodec提出了一种状态感知的神经编解码器，通过将数据包提升为带显式流槽和传输线索的定时动作，并学习连续逐包隐层，再经确定性编译器降级为PCAP，从而解决传统解码接口中学习行为与协议确定性后果纠缠的问题。在CICIDS2017 Monday上，该方法在数据包计数、协议组成和流种群上达到了0.03%以内的误差，而原始字段基线在相同策略下流计数和TCP状态扭曲达数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据包生成器普遍直接从原始包字段解码，导致学习到的行为选择与协议确定性后果纠缠，迫使数据包实现依赖事后启发式修复，这种解码接口被视为根本瓶颈。

Method: 将每个数据包提升为带显式流槽和传输线索的定时包动作（packet action），学习连续逐包隐层（per-packet latent），再通过确定性编译器将解码后的动作降级回PCAP，负责端点分配、TCP状态、合法性约束和包渲染。

Result: 在CICIDS2017 Monday上，TraceCodec的数据包计数、协议组成和流种群误差在0.03%以内；原始字段基线在相同策略下流计数和TCP状态扭曲达数量级。结构诊断显示TraceCodec保留了原始字段解码器碎片化的TCP状态转换和多流交错。

Conclusion: 这项工作为高保真数据包轨迹生成建立了新的基础，表明状态感知的神经编解码方案是替代原始字段解码的根本性改进方向。

Abstract: Critical networking workflows require high-fidelity packet captures (PCAPs) for testing, security analysis, and protocol validation, not just statistical flow-level summaries. Recent packet generators have demonstrated protocol-constrained PCAP synthesis, but they universally decode directly to raw packet fields. That interface entangles learned behavioral choices with deterministic protocol consequences, which forces packet realization to depend on post-hoc heuristic repair. We identify this decode interface as the fundamental bottleneck and present TraceCodec, a state-aware neural codec for stateful multi-flow traces. TraceCodec lifts each packet into a timed packet action with explicit flow slots and transport cues, then learns a continuous per-packet latent. A deterministic compiler lowers decoded actions back to PCAPs, owning endpoint assignment, TCP state, legality constraints, and packet rendering. The latent layer exposes a generator-facing sequence space, so downstream traffic models can operate on packet-action latents rather than raw header fields. On CICIDS2017 Monday, TraceCodec matches packet count, protocol composition, and flow population to within 0.03%. Raw-field baselines under the same non-repair policy distort flow counts and TCP state by orders of magnitude. Structural diagnostics show that TraceCodec preserves TCP state transitions and multi-flow interleaving that raw-field decoders fragment. This work establishes a new foundation for high-fidelity packet-trace generation.

</details>


### [202] [Intent-Based Orchestration in Open RAN: An ns-3 Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30079)
*Pouya Agheli,Grégoire Lefebvre*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于ns-3的可扩展仿真框架，用于评估开放RAN架构中基于意图和语义感知的控制。该框架集成了外部RIC组件，并通过内部dApps支持细粒度控制，实现跨不同时间尺度的意图驱动RAN编排。以RRM为例，在现实可观测性约束下实现了基于意图的dApp，并引入意图满意度得分（ISS）来量化信息传递质量。仿真结果表明，该方法在适度牺牲分组投递率和吞吐量的情况下，显著提高了ISS并降低了无线资源使用和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放RAN架构中缺乏能够有效评估基于意图和语义感知控制的仿真工具，现有方案难以支持跨不同时间尺度的细粒度RAN编排，且缺乏能够量化意图满足程度的统一度量标准。

Method: 基于ns-3构建可扩展仿真框架，集成外部RIC组件和内部dApps；引入ISS度量（结合失真导向和感知导向评估）；以RRM为用例，在现实KPM约束下制定调度问题。

Result: 基于意图的RRM能够提升ISS，显著降低无线资源使用和计算开销，但代价是分组投递率和吞吐量适度下降。

Conclusion: 所提框架和ISS度量能够有效支持开放RAN中的意图驱动控制，在资源效率与控制质量之间实现了可接受的权衡。

Abstract: This paper presents an extensible ns-3-based simulation framework for evaluating intent-based, semantics-aware control in Open RAN architectures. The framework integrates external Radio Access Network (RAN) Intelligent Controller (RIC) components and supports fine-grained control via internal distributed applications (dApps), enabling intent-based RAN orchestration across different timescales while maintaining standardized network behavior. As an illustrative use case, we implement an intent-based dApp for radio resource management (RRM) under realistic observability constraints. The scheduling problem is formulated using realistic key performance measurements (KPMs) available to dApps, together with a newly introduced Intent Satisfaction Score (ISS), which quantifies the delivery of intent-relevant information by combining distortion- and perception-oriented measures. Simulation results show that intent-based RRM can improve ISS while significantly reducing radio resource usage and computational overhead, at the cost of a moderate reduction in packet delivery ratio and throughput.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Generalized Software Product Line Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28989)
*Federico Bruzzone,Walter Cazzola,Luca Favalli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与工作台无关的软件产品线（SPL）协议，该协议基于轻量级依赖单元"原子"，采用自底向上的方法进行特征提取、配置和产品派生。协议通过SPL服务器（工作台无关）和SPL客户端（含工作台特定后端与通用前端）解耦技术空间，已在语言产品线（LPL）场景中通过Neverlang、Java、Go、Prolog和JavaScript的原型实现验证了其通用性和可互换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPL工程方法通常绑定特定的技术空间（如特定编程语言和组合器）及IDE，且提取机制对底层技术空间有强假设，这种紧耦合阻碍了异构开发环境下的复用、演化和采用。

Method: 提出一个通用的、与工作台无关的SPL协议：采用自底向上方法，基于轻量级依赖单元"原子"；架构围绕SPL服务器（工作台无关）和SPL客户端（含工作台特定后端与通用前端）组织；对底层软件工件做最少假设以确保广泛适用性。

Result: 通过原型实现展示了架构中多个子系统可自由互换而不影响其他部分，并在语言产品线（LPL）场景中验证了协议的具体适用性，同时保持工作台无关性。实现包括：Neverlang语言工件、Java SPL客户端后端、Go+Prolog的SPL服务器、JavaScript SPL客户端前端。

Conclusion: 所提协议能够解耦SPL工程对特定技术空间的依赖，实现异构开发环境下的特征提取、配置与产品派生，具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Software product line (SPL) engineering has been successfully applied to software development by obtaining software systems as compositions of modular features. Existing approaches to SPL engineering, however, are typically bound to a specific technological space (such as, a programming language and a composer) and integrated development environment (IDE), and rely on extraction mechanisms that make strong assumptions on the underlying technological space. This tight coupling hinders reuse, evolution, and adoption of heterogeneous development environments. We propose a general, workbench-agnostic protocol for extracting feature models from existing software artifacts and for configuring and deriving software products. The protocol follows a bottom-up approach based on lightweight dependency units called "atoms", and organizes the extraction and configuration process around an SPL server (workbench-independent) and an SPL client with a workbench-specific backend and a generic frontend. The protocol makes few assumptions on the underlying software artifacts and is therefore applicable to varied SPLs. The applicability of this approach is presented through a prototypical implementation of the architecture in which several subsystems interact and can be swapped freely without affecting the others. In particular, we focus on the application of such a protocol in the context of language product lines (LPLs), demonstrating its applicability to concrete scenarios while preserving workbench-agnosticism. From bottom to top, the implementation comprises: Neverlang language artifacts, a Java SPL client backend, an agnostic and reusable SPL server written in Go and Prolog, and a JavaScript SPL client frontend.

</details>


### [204] [LogDx-CI: Benchmarking Log Reduction Tools for LLM Root-Cause Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28876)
*Bowen Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了LogDx-CI基准，系统比较了11种CI失败日志缩减工具在35个真实GitHub Actions失败案例上的效果，发现：混合grep+tail路由器在成本-质量帕累托前沿占优；智能体循环中缩减工具的质量差距大幅缩小但成本差异持续存在；跨模型家族的摘要-诊断组合（gpt-5-mini摘要器+Claude Haiku诊断器）优于同家族组合，反驳了自调用偏差假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI失败日志规模大（中位数5k行，最大200k行）且噪声多，编码智能体依赖上游工具将日志缩减为可管理的上下文，但该领域缺乏对各种缩减工具在保留足够证据供下游LLM诊断方面的公开实证比较。

Method: 构建LogDx-CI基准，包含35个真实GitHub Actions失败案例，比较11种上下文缩减工具（包括原始日志、tail、grep、三种RTK模式、两种LLM map-reduce摘要器、三种混合路由器），使用3种LLM诊断器家族（Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、OpenAI gpt-5-mini）及Sonnet 4.6工具使用智能体进行评分。

Result: (1) 混合grep+tail路由器主导成本-质量帕累托前沿，前两名方法得分0.670/0.666，每案例约$0.03，质量与独立grep相当但token数减少4.5倍；(2) 智能体循环中，缩减工具的质量差距从单次0.42缩小到0.059（缩小7倍），但弱上下文迫使智能体多发出2-4倍工具调用；(3) 跨家族LLM摘要-诊断对（gpt-5-mini摘要+Claude Haiku诊断）比同家族对平均高出0.071，反驳了自调用偏差假设；gpt-5-mini摘要器在智能体循环中排名第一（得分0.749），每案例仅0.37次工具调用，成本比Haiku摘要器低10倍（$0.18 vs $1.75）。

Conclusion: 系统比较揭示了不同日志缩减策略在成本和质量之间的权衡，混合方法在单次设置中表现最优，智能体循环可以弥补弱上下文的不足但会增加成本，跨模型家族的摘要-诊断组合可以带来更好的效果。

Abstract: CI failure logs are large (median 5k lines, max 200k in this corpus) and noisy. Coding agents that try to debug them depend on an upstream tool to reduce the log to a manageable context, but the field has had no public empirical comparison of which reductions preserve enough evidence for downstream LLM diagnosis. We introduce LogDx-CI, a benchmark that compares 11 context-reduction tools (raw, tail, grep, three RTK modes, two real LLM map-reduce summarizers, three hybrid routers) on 35 real GitHub Actions failure cases, scored by 3 LLM debugger families (Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, OpenAI gpt-5-mini) plus a Sonnet 4.6 tool-using agent. We report three load-bearing findings. (1)~Hybrid grep+tail routers dominate the cost-quality Pareto frontier; the top two methods score 0.670 / 0.666 at $\sim$ \$0.03 per case, same-ballpark quality as standalone grep at $4.5\times$ fewer tokens. (2)~In the agent-loop regime, the quality range across reduction tools collapses $7\times$ (single-shot spread 0.42 $\to$ agent-loop spread 0.059); the agent rescues weak contexts via follow-up tool calls. However, cost differences persist: weak contexts force the agent to issue 2--4$\times$ more tool calls to recover. (3)~A cross-family LLM-summary pair (gpt-5-mini summarizer feeding a Claude Haiku debugger) beats the same-family pair by $+0.071$ averaged across four diagnoser variants, falsifying the self-call-bias hypothesis on this task. The gpt-5-mini summarizer is also the agent-loop \#1 method (score 0.749) at $0.37$ tool-calls per case and $10\times$ lower reducer cost than the Haiku summarizer (\$0.18 vs \$1.75 per case). All data, code, per-case bundles, and reproducibility infrastructure are public.

</details>


### [205] [Towards Demystifying and Repairing LLM-in-the-Loop Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28893)
*Yujie Ma,Jialin Rong,Chenxi Yang,Lili Quan,Xiaofei Xie,Yongqiang Lyu,Qiang Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个LLM-in-the-loop漏洞数据集LLMCVE，包含205个严格定义的漏洞。研究发现LLM更多作为攻击目标或传播载体而非根因，且现有修复方法对此类漏洞修复效果不佳，尤其对提示注入漏洞的Pass@1仅28.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究从传统软件层面分析LLM引入的漏洞，忽略了LLM实际造成的危害，缺乏对真实世界LLM-in-the-loop漏洞的系统性理解。

Method: 收集2,888个多源漏洞（覆盖230个流行LLM组件），通过人工分析筛选出205个严格符合LLM-in-the-loop概念的漏洞，构建LLMCVE数据集，并评估现有基于智能体的漏洞修复方法（如SWE-Agent）的性能。

Result: LLM更多作为攻击目标或传播载体而非根因；现有修复方法对LLM-in-the-loop漏洞修复困难，提示注入漏洞的Pass@1仅28.57%。

Conclusion: LLM-in-the-loop漏洞具有与传统软件漏洞不同的特性，现有修复方法难以精确修复，需开发针对性防御技术。

Abstract: Large Language Models(LLMs) have been actively integrated into modern software systems as critical components. LLM-in-the-loop vulnerabilities, where vulnerabilities are introduced by LLMs and their dependent downstream components, such as frameworks, introduce new risks. Although some benchmark datasets have been constructed to study the impact of such vulnerabilities, most works still remain at the analysis from the conventional software level, ignoring the harm actually caused by LLMs. Understanding real-world LLM-in-the-loop vulnerabilities is still an open problem. To address this gap, we build the first LLM-in-the-loop vulnerability dataset, LLMCVE, to facilitate the risk analysis of LLM-integrated software. To do so, we first collect 2,888 multi-source vulnerabilities across 230 popular LLM components. Then, through manual analysis, we identify 205 vulnerabilities that strictly fall under the concept of LLM-in-the-loop vulnerability. Through analysis, we found that LLMs more often play as targets or propagation vectors rather than the root cause of these vulnerabilities. Furthermore, based on LLMCVE, we evaluate the repairing capabilities of existing agent-based vulnerability repair methods, such as SWE-Agent. Experimental results demonstrate that compared to conventional software vulnerabilities, LLM-in-the-Loop vulnerabilities are more challenging to precisely fix, especially for those involving prompt injections where the Pass@1 rate is only 28.57%.

</details>


### [206] [Converted, Not Equivalent: Benchmarking Codebase Conversion via Observational Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29054)
*Linxin Song,Jiefeng Chen,Yue Huang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Chi Wang,Jieyu Zhao,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出T2J-Bench基准，用于代码库转换任务评估。通过355次盲转实验发现，最佳系统整体通过率仅26.7-28.9%，所有系统自评成功率虚高66.6-97.8个百分点。核心问题在于智能体过度信任自身局部验证，而非预算或模型强度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码转换评估多基于浅层结果驱动（如单次前向损失），导致两个实现可能在表面指标匹配但梯度、优化器行为或短期训练动态上存在根本差异。智能体常因过度信任自身局部验证例程而错误宣布成功，这一问题在代码库转换场景中尤为严重。

Method: 将代码库转换重新定义为固定等价合约下的迁移问题，并设计三层固定验证器：Spec（接口兼容性）、Numeric（前向输出、损失、梯度和目标特定张量）、Behavioral（固定种子下的短期训练动态）。在355次盲转尝试中评估多个系统。

Result: 最佳系统整体通过率仅26.7-28.9%（Spec通过率高达91.1%）；4.7倍token预算差异仅带来2.2倍通过率差距；所有系统自评成功率虚高66.6-97.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 代码转换失败的根本原因在于智能体的自我验证与合约不一致（contract-misaligned self-validation），而非预算或模型能力限制。需要更关注合约对齐的验证机制。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly act as codebase-scale collaborators that can assist with codebase conversion, but this progress has exposed a critical weakness: agents often over-trust their own local validation routines and declare success on artifacts that satisfy surface checks while violating the semantic contracts users actually care about. This problem is especially acute in codebase conversion, where prior evaluation is largely outcome-driven and therefore unstable: two implementations can match on a shallow outcome, such as a single forward loss, while diverging in gradients, optimizer behavior, or short-horizon training dynamics. We introduce T2J-Bench, a benchmark for codebase conversion that reformulates conversion as transfer under a fixed equivalence contract. A fixed verifier then compares source and converted codebases through three ordered stages: Spec (interface admissibility), Numeric (forward outputs, losses, gradients, and objective-specific tensors), and Behavioral (short training dynamics under fixed seeds). Across 355 blind conversion attempts, the best system reaches only 26.7--28.9% overall pass rate despite Spec pass rates up to 91.1%; a 4.7x token-budget spread yields only a 2.2x pass-rate spread; and all systems overestimate success by 66.6--97.8 points relative to the fixed evaluator. This suggests that failures stem more from contract-misaligned self-validation than from limited budget or backbone strength.

</details>


### [207] [SCDBench: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Smart Contract Decompilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29059)
*Kaihua Qin,Dawn Song,Arthur Gervais*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCDBench是一个面向基于大语言模型(LLM)的智能合约反编译的基准数据集与评估方法，包含600个真实合约。评估通过格式完整性、可编译性、ABI恢复和语义一致性四个递进阶段进行。实验表明，前沿LLM虽能生成结构化且可编译的Solidity代码，但语义一致性远未解决（最优模型仅完美反编译42/600个合约），引入同模型编译修复可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约反编译评估存在数据集狭窄、指标不一致、语义一致性检查有限等问题，随着LLM开始生成看似合理但语义偏离的Solidity代码，这一评估缺口变得愈发重要。

Method: 构建包含600个真实Solidity合约的数据集，提供配对字节码输入、真实源码和可重放的语义检查点；设计四阶段递进评估方法：格式完整性→可编译性→ABI恢复→通过差分重放验证语义一致性；评估Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.3-Codex、GLM-5等模型在零样本反编译及编译修复设置下的性能。

Result: 前沿LLM通常能生成结构化且可编译的Solidity代码，但语义一致性表现不佳；最优模型仅完美反编译42/600个合约（7%）；引入同模型编译修复可显著提升性能，且额外成本适中。

Conclusion: SCDBench为智能合约反编译提供了严谨可复现的评估基准，有望加速开发更可靠的智能合约反编译器，提升区块链安全性与透明度。

Abstract: Smart contract decompilation aims to recover high-level source code from bytecode, but evaluating decompilers remains difficult because existing studies use narrow datasets, inconsistent metrics, and limited semantic consistency checks. This gap is increasingly important as large language models (LLMs) begin to generate source-like Solidity that may compile and appear plausible, even when its semantics diverge from the original contract. We introduce SCDBench, a dataset and benchmark methodology for LLM-based smart contract decompilation. The dataset contains 600 real-world Solidity contracts with paired bytecode inputs, ground-truth source code, and replayable semantic checkpoints. SCDBench evaluates decompiler outputs through four cumulative stages: format completeness, compilability, Application Binary Interface (ABI) recovery, and semantic consistency via differential replay. We evaluate Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.3-Codex, and GLM-5 in a zero-shot decompilation setting, including GLM-5 variants with and without extended reasoning and a zero-shot compilation-repair setting. The results show that frontier LLMs can often produce structured and compilable Solidity, but achieving semantic consistency remains far from solved: the best-performing frontier model perfectly decompiles only 42/600 contracts. We further show that introducing same-model compilation repair substantially improves performance at modest additional cost. SCDBench establishes a common ground for rigorous, reproducible evaluation and aims to accelerate the development of reliable smart contract decompilers for blockchain security and transparency.

</details>


### [208] [Code-QA-Bench: Separating Code Reasoning from Documentation Memorization in Repository-Level QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29277)
*Jun Zhang,JianYing Qu,Hanwen Du,Zhongkai Sun,Yehua Yang,Qiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Code-QA-Bench框架，通过"先答案后问题"的生成流程和三条件实验设计（闭卷、仅代码、完整文档），自动合成仓库级代码理解基准，区分真正代码理解与文档回忆/预训练记忆。在SWE-Bench的10个Python仓库上生成528个代码可推导任务和100个文档依赖任务，实验表明代码访问是主导因素（+0.23），文档增益有限（+0.071）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码理解基准无法区分模型是真正理解代码逻辑，还是仅仅依赖于文档记忆或预训练知识，因此需要一个系统性的评估框架来分离这三者的贡献。

Method: (1) 答案优先生成管道：工具增强型智能体先探索源码生成经验证的黄金答案，再推导问题；(2) 三条件实验设计：闭卷、仅代码（去文档）、完整文档三种条件下评估，通过差值量化文档效用和记忆影响。

Result: 在4个前沿模型上，代码访问带来+0.23的平均增益（相比闭卷）；文档在文档依赖任务上仅提供+0.071的额外收益；在代码可推导任务上，仅代码条件与完整文档条件表现相当。

Conclusion: Code-QA-Bench能有效分离代码理解与文档记忆/预训练记忆，代码访问是代码理解任务的主导因素，文档贡献相对有限，框架开源且适用于任何有完善文档的Python仓库。

Abstract: We present Code-QA-Bench, a fully automated framework for synthesizing repository-level code understanding benchmarks that separates genuine code comprehension from documentation recall and pretraining memorization. The framework makes two methodological contributions: (1) an answer-first generation pipeline where a tool-equipped agent explores source code to produce verified gold answers before deriving questions, ensuring every task is grounded in real code structure; and (2) a three-condition experimental design evaluating agents under closed-book (no repository), code-only (documentation removed), and documented (full repository) conditions, with deltas directly quantifying documentation utility and memorization.
  We generate 528 code-derivable and 100 doc-dependent tasks across 10 Python repositories from SWE-Bench, scored by an LLM judge on accuracy, completeness, and specificity. Experiments on four frontier models reveal that code access is the dominant factor (+0.23 mean gain over closed-book), documentation provides modest additional benefit (+0.071 on doc-dependent tasks), and code-only $\approx$ documented on code-derivable tasks, validating the design. The framework is open-source and applicable to any well-documented Python repository.

</details>


### [209] [On the Road to Personalized Code Intelligence: Portraiting and Assisting Developers Based on Their In-IDE Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29372)
*Yuhong Liu,Yunhe Su,Zhipeng Peng,Zhiwen Luo,Lin Shi,Zhi Jin,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VirtualME是一个嵌入IDE的数据基础设施，通过持续捕获和解释开发者的动态编程行为来建模开发者个性特征，并将其集成到代码智能系统中，在个性化仓库级知识问答上相比通用基线平均提升33.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化软件工程研究过分关注大语言模型对代码的语义理解或智能体流程构建，忽视了开发者个体差异（如工具链偏好、领域专长和问题解决策略），导致"一刀切"的代码智能系统难以满足个性化需求。

Method: VirtualME包含三个组件：(1) 日志级行为提取：从IDE捕获开发者日志级行为；(2) 任务级行为识别：通过多智能体流水线将日志级行为聚合为任务级行为；(3) 开发者个性测量：构建规则引擎，从技术栈、能力、行为习惯和学习风格四个维度提炼开发者画像。在此基础上，将开发者画像集成到问答智能体中实现个性化仓库级知识问答。

Result: 在包含真实开发者轨迹的多仓库基准上，VirtualME增强的答案在五个维度上全面优于通用基线，平均提升33.80%。

Conclusion: 丰富且持续的开发者行为数据能够为自适应和个性化代码智能开辟新途径。

Abstract: With the advent of large language models, research in automated software engineering has increasingly focused on leveraging these models to achieve a deeper semantic understanding of code or to engineer sophisticated agent-based processes. However, this research trajectory has largely overlooked a critical factor: the developers themselves. Programming is a deeply individualized activity; developers exhibit significant variation in their tool-chain preferences, domain-specific expertise, and problem-solving strategies. Consequently, the current paradigm of one-size-fits-all code intelligence systems struggles to accommodate the needs of individual developers. To address this gap, we introduce VirtualME, a novel IDE-embedded data infrastructure designed to model the developer by continuously capturing and interpreting their dynamic programming behaviors and preferences. VirtualME contains three components. (1) Log-level Behavior Extraction: it captures and extracts developers' log-level behaviors from IDE. (2) Task-level Behavior Recognition: it aggregates log-level behaviors into task-level behaviors via a multi-agent pipeline. (3) Developer-personality Measurement: it builds a rule engine to distill a four-dimensional developer persona: technology stack, ability, behavioral habits, and learning style. On top of VirtualME, we propose a solution for personalized repository-level knowledge Q&A by integrating the developer persona into the Q&A agent. We evaluated VirtualME by building a multi-repository benchmark with real-world developer trajectories, balancing correctness and personalization. Experimental results show that VirtualME-enhanced answers outperform generic baselines on five dimensions, yielding an average 33.80% improvement. Our results demonstrate that abundant, continuous developer-behavior data can pave the new way for adaptive and personalized code intelligence.

</details>


### [210] [Offloading Score: Measuring AI Reliance Through Counterfactual Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29392)
*Vishakh Padmakumar,Lujain Ibrahim,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Jennifer Wang,Q. Vera Liao,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"卸载分数"(Offloading Score)这一新指标，通过模拟反事实工作流来量化用户在与AI工具协作时卸载的认知努力比例，相比传统使用频率或自我报告指标，能更灵敏地检测到时间压力下依赖程度的显著增加（+43%, p=0.018），并可用于识别适当与不适当的依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI依赖度量指标仅关注输出采纳率或自我报告指标，无法反映任务中用户与工具之间的认知努力分配，需要更精细的定量指标来捕捉真实的依赖程度。

Method: 通过构建反事实工作流（估计用户无工具情况下完成任务所需的步骤数），计算使用工具后节省的步骤比例作为卸载分数。通过内在效度评估和40名开发者的编程任务受控用户研究进行验证，实验中通过时间压力操纵来检验指标敏感性。

Result: 卸载分数在时间受限条件下检测到显著更高的依赖程度（+43%, p=0.018），而基于使用频率和自我报告的基线指标无法区分不同条件。高依赖表现为更多子任务委派给工具和更直接重用AI输出。

Conclusion: 卸载分数框架提供两个贡献：用户可用于衡量和反思自身依赖程度的工具，以及智能体设计者可用于缓解过度依赖的定量信号。

Abstract: AI tools are increasingly integrated into real-world workflows. However, existing measures of reliance on these tools focus on AI output adoption or on self-reported indicators, rather than how task effort is distributed between users and tools. Here, we introduce offloading score, a measure of reliance that quantifies the fraction of cognitive effort offloaded to an AI tool. Offloading Score is simulation-based -- we construct a counterfactual workflow by estimating how the user would have completed the task without the tool, and then computing the fraction of steps saved by using the tool. We validate offloading score through intrinsic evaluations of metric validity, and a controlled user study ($n=40$) with developers performing programming tasks using AI tools. We vary time pressure to test whether reliance measures capture the known increase in reliance under time pressure. We show that offloading score detects significantly higher reliance in time-constrained settings ($+43\%$, $p=0.018$), while usage-based and self-reported baseline measures of reliance do not distinguish the conditions. We complement this with descriptive insights showing that higher reliance manifests as greater delegation of subtasks to the tool and more direct reuse of AI outputs. Finally, we demonstrate an approach of using offloading score in combination with target outcomes of a task (e.g., code understanding) to identify when reliance may be (in)appropriate. Our framework offers two contributions: an instrument users can apply to measure and reflect on their own reliance, and a quantitative signal that agent designers can utilize to mitigate overreliance.

</details>


### [211] [How Coding Agents Fail Their Users: A Large-Scale Analysis of Developer-Agent Misalignment in 20,574 Real-World Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29442)
*Ningzhi Tang,Chaoran Chen,Gelei Xu,Yiyu Shi,Yu Huang,Collin McMillan,Tao Dong,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对20,574次编码智能体会话的观察研究，本文揭示了开发者与AI编码智能体之间的对齐失败（misalignment）现象，识别出七种常见形式，发现90.5%的失败造成信任与努力成本而非不可逆损害，且91.49%的解决仍需用户明确纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖基准测试轨迹分析编码智能体的失败，忽略了开发者在实际工作流中体验对齐问题的真实方式，缺乏对实际使用中misalignment的实证观察。

Method: 对来自1,639个仓库的20,574次编码智能体会话进行观察性研究，将misalignment定义为通过开发者抵触行为可观察到的"断裂"，沿形式、原因、成本、解决四个维度进行标注。

Result: 识别出七种反复出现的形式：项目阅读、意图理解、规则遵循、行为边界、实现与执行、进展报告等。90.50%的失败造成努力与信任成本，91.49%的解决仍需用户显式纠正。IDE与CLI场景下模式不同，跨会话持续存在，随时间推移整体比率下降但约束违规与不准确自我报告占比上升。

Conclusion: 研究结果为保持编码智能体与真实开发者工作流对齐的训练、评估和界面设计提供参考依据。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly act directly within software environments, yet existing analyses of their failures rely on benchmark trajectories that miss how developers actually experience misalignment. We present an observational study of 20,574 coding-agent sessions from 1,639 repositories across IDE and CLI workflows. We operationalize misalignment as a breakdown made visible through developer pushback, and annotate each episode along four axes: form, cause, cost, and resolution. We identify seven recurring forms, spanning how agents read projects, interpret developer intent, follow rules, bound their actions, implement and execute code, and report progress. 90.50\% of episodes impose effort and trust costs rather than irreversible system damage, yet 91.49\% of visible resolutions still require explicit user correction. Misalignment patterns also differ across IDE and CLI settings, persist across adjacent sessions, and shift over time: while overall rates decline, constraint violations and inaccurate self-reporting grow in share. Our findings inform the design of training, evaluation, and interfaces for keeping coding agents aligned with real developer workflows.

</details>


### [212] [Usability Analysis of Configurator User Interfaces with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29456)
*Sebastian Lubos,Alexander Felfernig,Damian Garber,Adnan Kraljić,Tarik Kraljić,Viet-Man Le,Thi Ngoc Trang Tran,Gerhard Leitner,Julian Schwazer,Doris Suppan,Reinhard Willfort,Ivan Dukic,Jeremias Fuchs,Manuel Henrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对配置器用户界面进行可扩展的半自动化可用性分析，综合18个配置器特定可用性准则，对16个真实配置器进行评估，结果表明MLLMs能可靠识别可用性问题并提供改进建议，尽管仍需人工验证，但可显著降低分析工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置器（用于定制复杂软件系统、服务和产品的技术）的成功应用不仅依赖于技术正确性、性能和领域建模，还依赖于其可用性。然而，虽然通用可用性启发式方法被广泛使用，但配置器特定的可用性准则和系统化的用户界面（UI）分析工具支持仍然有限。

Method: 从文献中综合出18个配置器特定可用性准则，将这些准则应用于基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的分析中，对16个真实世界配置器进行评估。每个准则单独评估，生成可用性问题的严重性评级和可操作的改进建议。

Result: 验证结果表明，MLLMs能够可靠地识别配置器特定的可用性问题，并提供领域感知的改进建议。尽管人工验证仍然必不可少，但该方法具有显著减少配置器可用性分析所需工作量的潜力。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型可作为配置器UI可用性分析的可扩展半自动化工具，在减少人工投入的同时提供领域特定的可用性评估与改进建议，但人工验证环节仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Configuration is a key technology for tailoring complex software systems, services, and products. A successful application of configurators not only depends on technical correctness, performance, and domain modeling but also on their usability. While general usability heuristics are widely used, configurator-specific criteria and tool support for systematic user interface (UI) analysis are limited. This paper explores the use of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for scalable and semi-automated usability analysis of configurator UIs. We synthesize 18 configurator-specific usability criteria from the literature and apply these criteria in an MLLM-based analysis of 16 real-world configurators. Each criterion is assessed individually to generate severity ratings for usability issues and actionable improvement suggestions. A review of the results confirms that MLLMs can reliably identify configurator-specific usability issues and provide domain-aware improvement recommendations. Although human validation remains necessary, this approach has the potential to significantly reduce the required effort to analyze configurator usability.

</details>


### [213] [CODEFUSE-DEBENCH: An Empirical Study on Readability, Recompilability, and Functionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29490)
*Puzhuo Liu,Yuhan Huang,Jianlei Chi,Peng Di,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了DEBENCH——首个自动化多维度反编译评估框架，从可读性、可重编译性和功能性三个正交维度衡量反编译器质量。基于240个原子测试函数和640个二进制文件，评估了5款主流反编译器和3个修复LLM。核心发现：反编译结果存在严重的"可复用性悬崖"，最佳组合的程序级行为重叠率仅22.3%，精确stdout匹配仅1.2%；可读性与功能性的最优设置不一致；反编译器引擎间的功能差异（20倍）远大于修复模型间的差异（1.6倍）；失败模式包括语法噪声、类型系统崩溃（约19%）和不可逆的上游信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反编译评估主要依赖语法相似性或单一可读性指标，无法反映实际可复用性。需要一种能捕获实用性的多维度评估范式。

Method: 构建DEBENCH框架，包含240个原子测试函数（8个源文件，编译为640个二进制文件）；采用LLM-as-judge可读性评分+URAF（18个子维度）、固定50轮迭代的编译修复循环、基于Frida的程序/函数/指令三级差分动态追踪。

Result: 最佳反编译器-LLM组合程序级行为重叠率22.3%，精确stdout匹配仅1.2%；-O3优化可读性最低但功能性最高；Clang可读性低于GCC但功能性高2.6倍；反编译器间功能差异20倍，修复模型间仅1.6倍；19%的修复错误归因于类型系统崩溃。

Conclusion: 反编译进展更依赖引擎本身而非更大规模的修复模型；可读性与功能性之间存在根本性权衡；类型系统和上游信息损失是限制可复用性的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Binary decompilation aims to recover binaries into high-level source code, but existing evaluations mainly rely on syntactic similarity or single-axis readability metrics, which fail to capture practical reusability. We propose a reusability-driven evaluation paradigm that measures decompiler quality along three orthogonal dimensions: readability, recompilability, and functionality. We present DEBENCH, the first automated framework for multidimensional decompilation evaluation. DEBENCH contains 240 atomic test functions, organized into 8 source files and compiled into 640 binaries. It combines LLM-as-judge readability scoring with URAF (18 sub-dimensions), iterative compile-and-repair under a fixed 50-iteration budget, and Frida-based differential dynamic tracing at the program, function, and instruction levels. We evaluate five mainstream decompilers and three repair LLMs. Our study reveals four findings. First, the reusability cliff is steep: the best decompiler-LLM pair reaches 22.3% Exact+Partial program-level behavioral overlap but only 1.2% exact stdout match, nearly 50 points below recompilability. Second, settings that maximize readability do not maximize functionality: -O3 yields the lowest readability but the highest functionality, and Clang gives lower readability than GCC but 2.6x higher functionality. Third, cross-decompiler variation at the functional level is 20x, far larger than the 1.6x cross-LLM variation, showing that progress depends more on decompiler engines than larger repair models. Fourth, failures fall into three categories: syntactic noise, type-system collapse (about 19% of repair errors), and irreversible upstream losses such as ARM64 relocation idioms and C++ ABI features.

</details>


### [214] [Inferring Code Correctness from Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29822)
*Tambon Florian,Papadakis Mike*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TRAILS提出了一种通过规范生成测试输入、执行代码并让LLM评估输入-输出对是否符合规范（而非直接推理代码）的方法，显著提升了LLM生成代码正确性验证的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码正确性验证是亟待解决的关键挑战。现有方法要么依赖动态共识（多个代码候选）导致成本高、难以扩展，要么依赖静态推理容易受动态错误和顺序偏差影响。

Method: TRAILS首先基于规范进行类别划分生成多样化的测试输入，然后在候选代码上执行这些输入，最后让LLM评估产生的输入-输出对是否符合规范（不直接推理代码），跨输入聚合分数以判定程序正确性。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CoCoClaNeL两个数据集上，使用Qwen3Coder-30B、Devstral-Small-24B和Olmo3.1-Instruct三个LLM评估，TRAILS相比Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought基线MCC提升高达39%，且一致优于HoarePrompt。

Conclusion: TRAILS不仅准确率更高，而且在多次运行中表现出更强的稳定性，降低了对LLM非确定性的敏感度，并为更多独特的代码样本分配了正确的标签。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become integral to modern software development, enabling automated code generation at scale. However, validating the correctness of LLM-generated code remains a critical and largely unsolved challenge. Existing approaches either rely on dynamic consensus across multiple code candidates - making them costly and difficult to scale - or on static reasoning that is susceptible to dynamic bugs and order bias. In this paper, we propose TRAILS~ (Targeted Reasoning Agreement via Inputs and Specifications), an approach that grounds LLM reasoning with concrete (input, output) pairs. TRAILS~ first generates diverse test inputs via category partitioning based on the specification, then executes them against the candidate code and prompts LLMs to assess whether the resulting input-output pairs conform to the specification - without ever reasoning over the code itself. Scores are aggregated across inputs, to determines whether the program is likely correct. We evaluate TRAILS~ on two datasets, LiveCodeBench and CoCoClaNeL, across three LLMs (Qwen3Coder-30B, Devstral-Small-24B, and Olmo3.1-Instruct), comparing against HoarePrompt and a Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought baseline. TRAILS~ improves Matthew Correlation Coefficient by up to 39\% relative to Zero-Shot COT and consistently outperforms HoarePrompt. Beyond accuracy, TRAILS~ demonstrates greater stability across seeded runs, reducing sensitivity to LLM non-determinism, and assigns correct labels to a larger set of unique code samples than competing approaches.

</details>


### [215] [TagDebt: A Bot to Support Technical Debt Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29869)
*João Paulo Biazotto,Daniel Feitosa,Paris Avgeriou,Elisa Yumi Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了 TagDebt，一个可集成到 GitHub 仓库中自动为 issue 打标签（SATD 或非 SATD）的机器人，帮助识别自我承认的技术债务（SATD），提高技术债务管理效率。通过设计科学研究构建该机器人，并基于技术接受模型（TAM）对 16 名从业者进行访谈评估，结果显示从业者认为其有用且易用，但团队和代码库规模等情境因素会影响采纳决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管技术债务的跟踪和管理至关重要，但目前仍缺乏能够轻松集成到现有开发工作流中的专用技术债务管理工具。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法设计并实现 TagDebt 机器人；通过技术接受模型（TAM）对 16 名从业者进行访谈，评估其有用性、易用性及影响使用的情境因素。

Result: 从业者普遍认为 TagDebt 有用（尤其体现在组织 issue 和减少手动工作方面）、易于使用且文档清晰；团队规模和代码库规模等情境因素会影响采纳决策；用户还提出了包括检查更新源代码在内的改进建议。

Conclusion: TagDebt 是面向技术债务管理专用工具的概念验证，能够在不干扰现有工作流的前提下使技术债务可见，帮助从业者规避未管理技术债务的风险。

Abstract: Context: Technical debt (TD) is a widely studied metaphor that helps to explain how sub-optimal decisions that can harm software maintainability over time. Although incurring TD is not intrinsically bad, tracking and managing TD are crucial to avoid its negative effects. Hence, researchers and practitioners have proposed and developed diverse approaches and tools for managing TD. However, we are still lacking specialized tools for technical debt management (TDM), specifically ones that can be easily integrated into existing development workflows. Objective: We present and evaluate TagDebt, a bot that can be integrated within GitHub repositories and automatically assign labels to issues (i.e., SATD or non-SATD). TagDebt helps in the identification of TD (i.e., by looking for self-admitted technical debt (SATD)), leading to more efficient TDM. Methods: We carried out a Design Science Research study to design and implement TagDebt. For its evaluation, we executed a Technology Acceptance Model (TAM) study through interviews with 16 practitioners, to check the bot's usefulness, ease of use, and contextual factors that might impact the bot's usage (such as team size and practitioners' roles). Results: Overall, practitioners found that TagDebt is useful, especially for organizing issues and reducing manual work. Furthermore, they pointed out that the bot is overall easy to use, and its documentation is clear. The analysis also revealed that contextual factors, such as team and codebase size, impact the decision to adopt TagDebt. Finally, several improvements were suggested, such as including features to check and update the source code. Conclusion: TagDebt is a proof-of-concept for the development and usage of more specialized tools for TDM. It helps to make TD visible without disrupting existing workflows and help practitioners avoid the risks of unmanaged TD.

</details>


### [216] [Agora: Toward Autonomous Bug Detection in Production-Level Consensus Protocols with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29910)
*Xiang Liu,Sa Song,Zhaowei Zhang,Huiying Lan,Jason Zeng,Ming Wu,Michael Heinrich,Yong Sun,Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agora，一个领域感知的多智能体框架，通过假设驱动测试与LLM能力相结合，在四个共识协议实现中发现15个未知的协议级逻辑漏洞，而现有LLM方法无法检测到任何此类漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共识协议实现中的漏洞可能导致数据损坏和财务损失，现有基于LLM的代码分析方法难以处理涉及复杂状态依赖行为的深层协议级逻辑漏洞。

Method: 提出Agora框架，采用专业分工的多智能体协作机制，包括假设生成、状态空间探索、基于领域约束的攻击场景合成以及迭代精化验证，实现全局协议不变量的推理。

Result: 在Raft、EPaxos、HotStuff、BullShark四个共识实现上，使用四种先进LLM，发现15个此前未知的违反安全属性的协议级逻辑漏洞，而现有基于LLM的智能体方法未能检测到任何此类漏洞。

Conclusion: 领域感知的多智能体协作对于检测复杂协议中的深层逻辑漏洞至关重要，超越了单函数代码分析的能力边界。

Abstract: Consensus protocols form the backbone of distributed systems and blockchains, where implementation bugs can cause data corruption and financial losses. While LLM-based approaches show promise in code analysis, they struggle with deep protocol-level logic bugs involving complex state-dependent behaviors across multiple execution stages. We present Agora, a domain-aware multi-agent framework that integrates hypothesis-driven testing with LLM capabilities for systematic protocol verification. Agora employs specialized agents that collaboratively explore protocol state spaces, synthesize attack scenarios using domain-specific constraints, and validate findings through iterative refinement. This explicit role separation enables reasoning about global protocol invariants beyond single-function code analysis. We evaluate Agora on four consensus implementations (Raft, EPaxos, HotStuff, BullShark) using four state-of-the-art LLMs. Agora discovers 15 previously unknown protocol-level logic bugs that violate safety properties, while existing LLM-based agents fail to detect any such protocol-level logic bugs. Our results demonstrate that domain-aware multi-agent collaboration is essential for detecting deep logic bugs in complex protocols.

</details>


### [217] [REPOT: Recoverable Program-of-Thought via Checkpoint Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30052)
*Parsa Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RePoT提出了一种确定性验证重放机制，当程序化思维（PoT）生成的计划在环境中执行到第一个无效动作时，仅需一次额外LLM调用即可从已验证前缀恢复执行，在多个模型和基准上显著优于PoT和PoT-retry基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoT方法生成的Python程序一旦包含单个无效动作，整个轨迹就会作废，缺乏容错机制。作者希望在不显著增加计算成本的前提下，为PoT引入恢复能力。

Method: RePoT包含两个阶段：(1) 确定性验证重放——将PoT生成的计划在环境中逐步执行直到遇到第一个无效转换；(2) 从已验证前缀处恢复——通过一次LLM调用，基于已验证的前缀生成后续有效动作。此外，Adaptive RePoT根据已验证前缀长度在后缀修复和全新PoT重试之间进行规则路由。

Result: 在PuzzleZoo-775上，RePoT在四种闭源模型配置下比PoT提升+3到+11个百分点，gpt-5.4-mini-medium最高达96.9%（vs 86.3%）；在与匹配预算的PoT-retry基线对比中，Gemini上显著胜出（+3.8pp），GPT-medium和Claude上无显著差异，GPT-mini上落后；在PlanBench Blocksworld上提升+1.1到+11.4pp，在四个开源模型中的三个上提升+3.3到+20.0pp。Derail-550受控恢复基准实验表明，检查点信息是关键恢复信号。

Conclusion: RePoT以极少额外成本（仅约14%问题需要一次额外LLM调用）显著提升了PoT的鲁棒性，验证了检查点信息而非特定前缀尾部是恢复的关键信号。Adaptive RePoT初步展示了根据能力规模调度的潜力。

Abstract: One-shot Program-of-Thought (PoT) emits a Python program that prints a primitive-action plan; a single invalid action silently invalidates the trajectory. We introduce RePoT (Recoverable PoT): a deterministic verified replay that walks the plan through the environment to its first invalid transition, then one LLM call that resumes from the verified prefix. RePoT costs at most one extra LLM call on the ~14% of problems where PoT fails. RePoT beats PoT by +3 to +11pp across four closed-model configurations on PuzzleZoo-775 and peaks at 96.9% vs 86.3% on gpt-5.4-mini-medium; against the matched-budget PoT-retry baseline, RePoT wins decisively on Gemini (+3.8pp, 95% CI [+2.2,+5.4]), is within sampling noise on GPT-medium and Claude, and loses on GPT-mini -- a capability-scaling pattern we begin to address with Adaptive RePoT, a rule-based dispatcher that routes between suffix repair and a fresh PoT retry based on verified-prefix length (preliminary). We replicate on PlanBench Blocksworld (+1.1 to +11.4pp) and on four open-weights models (+3.3 to +20.0pp on three of four). On Derail-550, our controlled recovery benchmark, every condition with access to checkpoint information clears >=30% on GPT-medium and >=70% on Gemini, vs <=3.1% for error-only feedback -- showing that checkpoint information, not the specific verified-prefix tail, is the load-bearing recovery signal.

</details>


### [218] [Projectional Decoding: Towards Semantic-Aware LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30054)
*Boqi Chen,José Antonio Hernández López,Aren A. Babikian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"投影解码"（projectional decoding）框架，通过在文本生成过程中维护部分图模型作为主要工件表示，将领域语义直接集成到LLM生成过程中，实现增量语义验证并保证生成输出的语义有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束解码技术虽能保证语法正确性和特定语义规则，但缺乏一种通用表示来桥接LLM生成的文本与软件工程中语义验证所需的推理能力。

Method: 提出投影解码框架，在生成过程中同时维护文本和部分图模型作为主要工件表示，通过显式捕获不确定性并进行增量语义验证，引导生成朝向语义有效的输出，并提供可证明的保证。

Result: 在程序生成任务上的初步结果显示该方法有潜力提高LLM生成工件的语义有效性。

Conclusion: 投影解码框架能够在各种软件工程活动中实现LLM的可验证自动化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts across many software engineering (SE) tasks, yet ensuring the semantic validity of these artifacts remains a fundamental challenge. Existing constrained decoding techniques can enforce syntactic correctness and, in some cases, specific semantic rules, but lack a general representation that bridges LLM-generated text with the reasoning required for semantic validation in SE. In this paper, we propose projectional decoding, a novel conceptual framework that integrates domain semantics directly into the generation process by maintaining, alongside text, a partial graph model as the primary artifact representation throughout generation. This abstract representation enables incremental semantic validation by explicitly capturing uncertainty and natively supporting error detection, while guiding generation toward semantically valid outputs with provable guarantees. We present preliminary results on a program generation task which demonstrate the potential of this approach to improve the semantic validity of LLM-generated artifacts. We also discuss how projectional decoding can enable verifiable automation with LLMs across various SE activities.

</details>


### [219] [EvoRepair: Enhancing Vulnerability Repair Agents Through Experience-Based Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30105)
*Haichuan Hu,Guoqing Xie,Quanjun Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Shengcheng Yu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoRepair，首个基于经验的自演化自动漏洞修复智能体框架，通过循环学习-修复过程积累、精炼和复用漏洞修复经验，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在自动漏洞修复中面临两个关键局限：缺乏漏洞内经验积累和缺乏跨漏洞经验复用，导致LLM在迭代修复中反复犯类似错误，且未能充分利用历史漏洞中的修复知识。

Method: EvoRepair采用循环的"学习-修复"过程：(1)检索相关历史经验指导修复；(2)从修复轨迹中提取新经验；(3)使用质量感知评分更新经验库，实现领域特定知识的持续积累、精炼与复用。

Result: 在PATCHEVAL和SEC-bench上使用GPT-5-mini评估，EvoRepair达到93.47%和87.00%的修复率，总体90.46%。相比LoopRepair提升39.56%和33.50%，相比IntentFix提升70.86%和50.50%，相比Live-SWE-Agent提升6.98%。在VUL4J上的迁移实验进一步验证了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于经验的自演化机制能够显著增强智能体驱动的自动漏洞修复，且超越了现有的自演化技术。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated vulnerability repair (AVR), but they still face several limitations, including the lack of intra-vulnerability experience accumulation and the lack of cross-vulnerability experience reuse. As a result, LLMs may repeatedly make similar mistakes during iterative repair and underutilize valuable repair knowledge from historical vulnerabilities. To address these challenges, we propose EvoRepair, the first experience-based self-evolving AVR agent framework that enables LLMs to accumulate, refine, and leverage domain-specific knowledge across long-horizon vulnerability repairs. EvoRepair follows a cyclic learn-and-repair process that retrieves relevant past experiences to guide repair, extracts new experiences from repair trajectories, and updates an experience bank using quality-aware scoring. We evaluate EvoRepair against 12 representative vulnerability repair baselines on PATCHEVAL and SEC-bench using GPT-5-mini. Results show that EvoRepair achieves the best overall performance, reaching 93.47% on PATCHEVAL, 87.00% on SEC-bench, and 90.46% overall. In particular, EvoRepair outperforms latest LLM-based baseline LoopRepair by 39.56% and 33.50% on PATCHEVAL and SEC-bench, respectively, and surpasses IntentFix by 70.86% and 50.50%. Across both benchmarks, EvoRepair also exceeds the recent self-evolving agent Live-SWE-Agent by 6.98% overall. Additional transfer experiments on VUL4J further demonstrate the robustness of EvoRepair across models, programming languages, and datasets. These findings demonstrate that experience-based self-evolution substantially strengthens agentic AVR and goes beyond existing self-evolving techniques.

</details>


### [220] [Automating Low-Risk Code Review at Meta: RADAR, Risk Calibration, and Review Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30208)
*Chris Adams,Arjun Singh Banga,Parveen Bansal,Souvik Bhattacharya,Rujin Cao,Pedro Canahuati,Nate Cook,Brian Ellis,Prabhakar Goyal,Gurinder Grewal,Tianyu He,Matt Labunka,Alex Manners,David Molnar,Ging Cee Ng,Vishal Parekh,Jiefu Pei,Frederic Sagnes,James Saindon,Will Shackleton,Sid Sidhu,Gursharan Singh,Karthik Chengayan Sridhar,Matt Steiner,Pratibha Udmalpet,Sean Xia,Stacey Yan,Audris Mockus,Peter Rigby,Nachiappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Meta 提出 RADAR（风险感知差异自动审查系统），通过多阶段漏斗流水线对 AI 生成的代码变更进行自动化审查与落地。系统审查了 53.5 万+差异，落地 33.1 万+，将回滚率降至非 RADAR 差异的 1/3，生产事故率降至 1/50，中位关闭时间缩短 330%，审查耗时降低 35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助编码工具大幅提升了代码产出（人均行数增长 105.9%，差异量增长 51%），但及时审查比例下降，代码供给与审查带宽之间的差距持续扩大，迫切需要可扩展的自动化审查方案来缓解这一瓶颈。

Method: RADAR 采用多阶段漏斗架构：按作者身份和来源类型分类→资格门控→静态启发式规则→机器学习 Diff 风险评分→LLM 自动代码审查→确定性验证，仅允许通过所有阶段的变更自动落地。评估方式包括遥测数据、策略变更的前后对比以及双重差分分析。

Result: 已审查 53.5 万+差异，落地 33.1 万+。风险评分阈值从 25% 放宽至 50% 时批准率升至 60.31%。RADAR 审查差异的回滚率为非 RADAR 的 1/3，生产事故率为 1/50。中位关闭时间提升 330% 以上，审查墙钟时间降低 35%。

Conclusion: 风险感知的分层自动化可以在不牺牲生产安全的前提下，有效缓解 AI 驱动的代码增长所带来的审查瓶颈。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools have altered software production. At Meta, significant lines of code per human-landed diff grew by 105.9% year over year and per-developer diff volume rose 51%, with agentic AI responsible for over 80% of that growth. Meanwhile, the share of diffs receiving timely review has declined, exposing a widening gap between code supply and reviewer bandwidth. We ask three questions that progress from feasibility through calibration to impact: (1) can risk-stratified automation operate at scale across diverse organizations, (2) how does tuning the risk threshold affect the trade-off between automation yield and safety, and (3) to what extent does automated review reduce end-to-end latency for AI-generated changes? We deployed RADAR (Risk Aware Diff Auto Review), a multi-stage funnel that classifies each diff by authorship and source type, applies eligibility gates, static heuristics, a machine-learned Diff Risk Score, LLM-based Automated Code Review, and deterministic validation before landing qualifying changes. We evaluate RADAR through telemetry covering 535K+ RADAR-reviewed diffs, observational before-after comparisons for policy changes, and difference-in-differences analysis of efficiency outcomes. RADAR has reviewed 535K+ diffs and landed 331K+. Relaxing the Diff Risk Score threshold from the 25th to the 50th percentile increased the approve rate to 60.31%. The revert rate for RADAR-reviewed diffs is 1/3 that of non-RADAR diffs, and the Production Incident rate is 1/50 that of non-RADAR diffs. RADAR reduces median time to close by over 330% and median diff review wall time by 35%. Risk-aware layered automation can materially reduce review bottlenecks created by AI-driven code growth without compromising production safety.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [IORM: Hierarchical I/O Governance for Thousands of Consolidated Databases on Oracle Exadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29006)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Zakaria Alrmaih,Chenhao Guo,Anubhav Singh,Sue Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oracle Exadata存储端的I/O资源管理器（IORM），通过I/O标签、分层资源配置文件和统一存储治理三个机制，实现了对多租户数据库环境下跨容器数据库、租户数据库和工作负载三层的精细I/O资源调度，有效消除了混合负载下的噪声邻居干扰和尾延迟异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oracle Exadata将数千个租户数据库整合到共享存储基础设施上，现有的块层调度器缺乏对数据库语义和租户边界的感知能力，无法管理容器数据库、租户数据库及内部工作负载构成的多级资源层次结构，导致混合负载场景下出现噪声邻居干扰和尾延迟恶化问题。

Method: IORM基于三个核心机制：(1) I/O标签——从数据库内核向存储调度器传递语义上下文；(2) 分层资源配置文件——使用份额和上限表达跨整合层次的可组合分配策略；(3) 统一存储治理——将策略一致应用于持久内存、闪存和硬盘所有存储层级，包括缓存放置决策。

Result: 在生产级Exadata系统上的评估表明：IORM显著提升了延迟一致性，几乎消除了尾延迟异常，混合负载下平均读延迟提升数倍；分层限制在所有三个层级上正确组合；比例份额分配在高倾斜需求下仍能紧密跟踪配置比例。

Conclusion: IORM使得数千租户共存于共享存储的云部署成为可能，生产环境中的OLTP工作负载能与来自同一或不同数据库的分析型负载并行运行而不产生噪声邻居干扰，实现了极端密度下的多租户资源隔离。

Abstract: Oracle Exadata consolidates thousands of tenant databases onto shared storage infrastructure deployed at hundreds of customer sites worldwide. Oracle Multitenant architecture enables this extreme density, with thousands of tenant databases sharing a single Exadata storage system -- but this creates a multi-level resource hierarchy (container databases, tenant databases, and workloads within tenants) that commodity block-layer schedulers cannot govern, as they lack visibility into database semantics and tenant boundaries. This paper presents the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a storage-side scheduler built on three mechanisms: I/O Tagging, which propagates semantic context from the database kernel to the storage scheduler; Hierarchical Resource Profiles, which express compositional allocation policies across consolidation tiers using shares and limits; and Unified Storage Governance, which applies these policies consistently across all tiers of the storage hierarchy -- persistent memory, flash, and hard disk -- including cache placement decisions. IORM enables successful cloud deployments where thousands of tenants coexist on shared storage: production OLTP workloads run alongside concurrent analytical workloads from the same or different databases without noisy-neighbor interference. Evaluation on production Exadata systems demonstrates that IORM dramatically improves latency consistency, virtually eliminating tail latency outliers and delivering several-fold improvements in average read latency under mixed workloads. Hierarchical limits compose correctly across all three levels, and proportional share allocation tracks configured ratios closely even under highly skewed demand.

</details>


### [222] [ScanTwin: Simulating Performance Regressions Without Access to Tenant Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29093)
*Donghyun Sohn,Jennie Rogers*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScanTwin是一个轻量级框架，通过从Parquet页脚提取每行组草图（包括边界值和压缩大小），并在ε-差分隐私保护下使用边界参数化释放这些数据，从而在不访问原始数据的情况下，使开发者能重现云数据平台中特定租户数据集上的性能回归问题。实验表明，该框架在TPC-H和SSB数据集上实现了零剪枝误差和极低的字节误差，并能准确追踪实际扫描时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据平台中，开发者常遇到仅在特定租户数据集上出现的性能回归问题，但由于保密限制无法访问原始数据，导致难以本地复现。现有合成数据方法仅关注统计属性（如匹配数据分布或提高查询准确性），忽略了控制引擎扫描行为的物理属性（如行组剪枝）。

Method: ScanTwin提出从Parquet页脚提取每行组的草图信息，包括边界值和压缩大小，并通过边界参数化方法在ε-差分隐私保护下释放这些信息，从而在保护数据隐私的同时保留关键的物理属性。

Result: 在TPC-H和SSB（600万行）数据集上，ScanTwin在ε=∞时实现了0%的剪枝误差和低于1%的字节误差。在ε=5时，高选择性查询（>30%）在两种数据集上的剪枝误差低于8.5%，且DuckDB上每查询的扫描时间与原始数据紧密吻合。

Conclusion: ScanTwin通过提取和差分隐私保护下的行组级物理属性，有效解决了云数据平台中因数据保密限制而无法复现性能回归的问题，同时在隐私保护下保持了高精度的查询性能模拟。

Abstract: In cloud data platforms, developers often encounter performance regressions that occur in specific tenant datasets. However, due to confidentiality constraints, they cannot access the original data, which makes it difficult to reproduce these regressions locally. Current methods for synthetic data usually focus on statistical properties, such as matching data distributions or improving query accuracy. However, they overlook the physical properties that control how the engine behaves during scans, including row-group pruning.
  We propose ScanTwin, a lightweight framework that extracts a per-row-group sketch from the Parquet footer, including boundary values and compressed sizes, and releases them under $\varepsilon$-differential privacy using a boundary parameterization. On TPC-H and SSB (6M rows), ScanTwin achieves 0% pruning error and less than 1% byte error at $\varepsilon{=}\infty$. Under $\varepsilon{=}5$, high-selectivity queries ($>$30%) incur below 8.5% pruning error on both datasets, and per-query scan timing on DuckDB closely tracks the original.

</details>


### [223] [One Ring to Shuffle Them All: Scalable Intra-Process Data Redistribution with Ring-Buffer Shuffle in Redpanda Oxla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29099)
*Adam Szymański,Tyler Akidau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对多核CPU环境下并行查询引擎的数据重分布问题，提出了一种基于无锁环形缓冲区的流式shuffle设计，通过原子槽位分配和固定大小批处理实现O(1)均摊同步开销和O(M)内存独立性。在72核至192核系统上，相比通道流式和批处理分区方法分别提升最高100%+和300%+，但在多芯片架构下共享原子计数器成为瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内部进程shuffle设计在多核扩展性上存在根本缺陷：批处理分区避免了热路径同步但需物化中间数据并引入全局生产者/消费者屏障，缓存局部性差；基于通道的流式处理避免物化但每通道同步开销随核数增长而扩展性差。随着服务器CPU核数增至数十乃至上百，这些架构权衡日益阻碍引擎充分利用现代硬件。

Method: 提出环形缓冲区流式shuffle设计，采用无锁原子槽位分配到固定大小的批处理组中，实现均摊O(1)同步开销和O(M)内存独立性（与输入规模无关）。该设计已在Redpanda的Oxla查询引擎中生产运行两年。

Result: 在72核单插槽系统上，环形缓冲区相比通道流式提升44%，相比批处理分区提升79%；在192核系统上，相比通道流式优势超100%，相比批处理分区超300%。但在多芯片架构（多个分立L3缓存）下，共享原子计数器成为跨芯片瓶颈，通道流式仍具竞争力。端到端TPC-H和ClickBench评估显示优势与工作负载形状相关。

Conclusion: 环形缓冲流式shuffle在多核场景下显著优于传统方法，但并非通用最优：在芯片架构下需考虑跨芯片原子操作开销，实际优势取决于工作负载特征。

Abstract: As server CPUs scale to dozens and now hundreds of cores per socket, parallel query engines must rethink how they redistribute data between threads. Partitioned operators such as hash joins and aggregations require frequent data redistribution across threads, yet existing intra-process shuffle designs fundamentally fail to scale with core count: batch partitioning avoids cross-thread synchronization in the hot path but materializes all intermediate data, introduces a global producer/consumer barrier, and requires a consumption approach with low cache locality, while channel-based streaming avoids materialization but incurs per-channel synchronization that scales poorly with core count. As core counts rise, these architectural tradeoffs increasingly prevent engines from fully utilizing modern hardware.
  We present a ring-buffer streaming shuffle design that addresses these shortcomings through lock-free atomic slot acquisition into fixed-size batch groups, achieving amortized O(1) synchronization cost per batch and O(M) memory independent of input size. Ring-buffer shuffle has been implemented in Redpanda's Oxla query engine for two years, where it currently powers production queries for Redpanda SQL users.
  We evaluate all three approaches on a 72-core NVIDIA GraceHopper, a 192-core dual-socket AWS Graviton4, and a 96-core (192-thread) AMD EPYC. On a 72-core single-socket system the ring buffer outperforms channel streaming by up to 44% and batch partitioning by up to 79%; at 192 cores the advantage over channel grows to over 100% and over 300% versus batch partitioning. Even so, on chiplet architectures with many partitioned L3 caches, the shared atomic counter becomes a cross-die bottleneck and channel-based streaming remains competitive. End-to-end Graviton4 evaluation on TPC-H (21 queries) and ClickBench (43 queries) shows the advantage is workload-shape-dependent.

</details>


### [224] [Towards Reliable Agentic Progressive Text-to-Visualization with Verification Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29692)
*Wenxin Xu,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Haoyang Li,Hwanhee Kim,Yuanfeng Song,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PMVis 提出了一种渐进式多轮交互范式用于文本到可视化任务，并构建了首个支持渐进式迭代查询的数据集 PMVisBench，以及基于多智能体框架的 PMVisAgent，通过 ReAct 风格的工具纠错机制在多表场景下实现高达 23.21% 的执行准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到可视化方法采用一次性范式，要求用户在一轮中指定所有可视化细节，导致认知负担过重且容易生成错误可视化结果。

Method: 提出 PMVis 渐进式多轮交互范式；构建 PMVisBench 数据集（通过 VQL 简化和 NLQ 重构，并设置显式规则约束保证中间 VQL 有效）；设计 PMVisAgent 多智能体框架（包含 User、System 和 Validation Agent，通过 ReAct 风格工具循环进行验证与修复，防止错误累积）。

Result: 在 PMVisBench 上，PMVisAgent 在单表和多表设置中分别比现有最优方法提升 17.57% 和 23.21% 的执行准确率；消融实验验证了渐进式交互与澄清机制的重要性。

Conclusion: 渐进式多轮交互范式能有效降低用户认知负荷、提升可视化生成准确性，多智能体的验证-修复机制能显著缓解多轮交互中的错误累积问题。

Abstract: Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) translates natural language queries into visualization query languages, enabling non-expert users to perform data analysis. However, most existing methods follow a one-shot paradigm that requires users to specify all visualization details in a single round, often leading to cognitive overload and incorrect visualizations. In this paper, we propose PMVis, a progressive multi-turn paradigm for text-to-vis, where users' intents are refined through multi-turn interactions. To support research in this paradigm, we construct PMVisBench, the first dataset designed to capture the progressive and iterative nature of real-world user queries. It is built through VQL simplification and NLQ reconstruction, with explicit rule constraints to ensure each intermediate VQL remains valid and meaningful. Building upon PMVis, we further introduce PMVisAgent, an agent-based framework that simulates realistic user-system dialogues. PMVisAgent consists of a User, a System, and a Validation Agent that performs verification and repair via a ReAct-style tool-use loop to mitigate error accumulation across rounds, with explicit interaction and verification rules to ensure reliability of the multi-agent system. Extensive experiments on PMVisBench demonstrate that PMVisAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-vis baselines. It achieves up to 17.57\% and 23.21\% improvements in execution accuracy in single-table and multi-table settings, respectively, while ablation studies confirm the importance of combining progressive interaction with clarification. The code is available at https://github.com/wxxv/PMVis.

</details>


### [225] [Zero-Scan Data Quality: Leveraging Table Format Metadata for Continuous Observability at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30308)
*Mohit Verma,Shantanu Rawat,Christian Bush,Sumedh Sakdeo,Lokesh Amarnath Ravindranathan,Dwarak Bakshi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元数据优先的方法，通过复用Apache Iceberg表格式在写入时计算的统计信息（如提交时间戳、记录计数、空值计数、值边界等）来实现持续的数据质量可观测性，无需扫描任何数据。在LinkedIn超过20万张Iceberg表（800+PB）上的部署表明，该方法能以零边际计算成本满足约60%的用户定义DQ规则，并将分析资源消耗降低约50%。通过扩展清单统计信息加入轻量级计数器（求和、零值计数、布尔计数）和可增量合并的草图（Theta草图用于去重计数、KLL草图用于分位数），可将元数据可满足的覆盖率提升至接近90%的生产DQ规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表格式（如Apache Iceberg）在写入时计算并存储元数据提交时间戳、记录计数以及列级统计信息（如空值计数和值边界），这些统计信息主要用于查询规划，但与数据质量监控需求高度重叠。现有方法需要扫描数据来进行质量监控，成本高昂，因此希望复用已有写入时统计信息来实现零额外计算成本的数据质量可观测性。

Method: 1) 元数据优先方法：直接复用Iceberg清单（manifest）中已有的写入时统计信息（时间戳、记录数、空值计数、值边界等）进行异常检测、漂移监控、空值率跟踪。2) 扩展清单统计：加入轻量级计数器（求和、零值计数、布尔计数）。3) 引入可增量合并的草图结构：Theta草图（用于不同值计数）和KLL草图（用于分位数估计）。4) 建议将每文件草图存储在Puffin侧车文件中，沿用现有清单统计的存储-聚合模式。

Result: 在LinkedIn的200,000+张Iceberg表（800+PB数据）上部署，该方法以零边际计算成本满足约60%的用户定义DQ规则，并将分析资源消耗降低约50%。通过扩展统计信息和草图，可将元数据可满足的覆盖率提升至接近90%的生产DQ规则。验证了草图的准确性、可合并性及存储开销。

Conclusion: 表格式应该存储每文件的草图（在Puffin侧车文件中），遵循与现有清单统计相同的先存储后聚合模式，从而在不扫描数据的情况下实现高效的数据质量监控，大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: Modern table formats such as Apache Iceberg compute and store metadata-commit timestamps, record counts, and column-level statistics such as null counts and value bounds at write time as part of file writing. These statistics serve query planning, yet they overlap substantially with data quality (DQ) monitoring needs. We describe a metadata-first approach that repurposes write-time statistics for continuous DQ observability: anomaly detection, drift monitoring, null-rate tracking; without scanning any data. Deployed at LinkedIn across 200,000+ Iceberg tables (800+ PB), this approach satisfies approximately 60% of user-defined DQ rules at zero marginal compute cost and reduces profiling resource consumption by around 50%. Extending manifest statistics with lightweight counters (sum, zero-value counts, boolean counts) and incrementally mergeable sketches; Theta sketches for distinct counts, KLL sketches for quantiles; can further raise metadata-satisfiable coverage to close to 90% of production DQ rules. We validate sketch accuracy, mergeability, and storage overhead on production data and propose that table formats should store per-file sketches in Puffin sidecar files, following the same store-then-aggregate pattern used for existing manifest statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Designing for the Moment: How One-Minute Interventions Fit or Falter Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29051)
*Zahra Hassanzadeh,Anne Hsu,Rachel Kornfield,David Haag,Ananya Bhattacharjee,Jay Olson,Jan David Smeddinck,Norman Farb,Alex Mariakakis,Lydia Chilton,Joseph Jay Williams*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索了无需入职培训或传感的一分钟数字干预设计空间，基于Fogg行为模型和四项设计原则，通过14天22人研究，发现共同创作式的有意个性化可在相关性与低摩擦间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在无需传感和入职培训的情况下，设计出能促使低动机用户立即行动的一分钟数字干预措施。

Method: 基于Fogg行为模型和文献中的四项设计原则设计干预提示，对22名参与者进行14天研究，涵盖身体活动、健康饮食和心理健康三个领域，并分析参与者对提示的重写内容。

Result: 提示的有效性得到验证；通过参与者重写提示获得的见解表明，共同创作式的有意个性化是一种轻量级个性化机制。

Conclusion: 共同创作式的有意个性化能够在干预的相关性与使用低摩擦之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper explores the design space for one-minute digital interventions that prompt immediate action without onboarding or sensing. By embracing Fogg's Behavior Model and four design principles informed by literature, the goal of these interventions was to provide triggers that encourage actions so simple that even people with low motivation would be willing to complete them. We examined the utility of these prompts by conducting a 14-day study with 22 participants interested in making small lifestyle improvements in at least one of three domains: physical activity, healthy eating, and mental well-being. When combined with insights drawn from participants' rewrites of our prompts, our findings suggest that intentional personalization through co-authorship could be a lightweight personalization mechanism that balances relevance with low friction.

</details>


### [227] ["It's OK Because...": The Wild West of Student Rationalization of AI Use in Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29090)
*Jiyoon Kim,Kentaro Toyama,Sangmi Kim,John M. Carroll*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于20份半结构化访谈、AI聊天记录和课程文档，研究发现学生对AI使用的道德合理化过程涉及至少5个概念化环节、超过20种合理化策略，且这些合理化在逻辑上不一致，导致学生沿道德滑梯滑出教学预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI不仅使学生能够将大量学术工作外包，还模糊了学术诚信的道德边界，亟需理解学生如何从道德层面合理化AI使用行为。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈（n=20）、AI聊天日志、课程文档（教学大纲、提交作业）的多源质性研究方法，分析学生的道德推理过程。

Result: 发现了5个AI使用概念化环节；归纳出超过20种合理化策略并形成分类体系；发现合理化发生在事前（ad hoc）和事后（post hoc）两种时间模式，且缺乏自洽性。

Conclusion: 现代AI构成了陡峭的道德滑梯，学生在概念上逐步下滑至远离教学目标的位置，研究对教育设计和AI政策制定具有重要启示。

Abstract: Generative AI challenges academic integrity not only by enabling students to delegate substantial portions of their academic work, but also by blurring the ethical boundaries by which students distinguish acceptable assistance from misconduct. Drawing on semi-structured interviews (n=20), AI chat logs, and course documents (syllabi, submitted assignments), we investigated how students themselves make moral sense of AI use in academic writing. Our analysis results in a range of novel findings: First, there are at least five distinct sites of AI-use conceptualization, ranging from faculty's intended AI policy, to students' actual AI use. Second, students use over 20 distinct rationalizations to justify AI use, such as that copying AI-generated text is victimless; that any AI text reflecting their own beliefs or their own style is their own writing; or that they are learning more by using AI -- even extensively -- than otherwise. We present a taxonomy of these rationalizations, and show how some of them are employed to justify conscious violations of course policies. Third, student rationalizations occur in both an ad hoc and post hoc manner, and they are not necessarily self-consistent. These and other findings suggest that modern AI presents a steep, ethical, slippery slope which students conceptually slide down, landing far outside the pedagogical goals and expectations of instructors. We discuss implications for educational design and AI policy.

</details>


### [228] [A Domain-Informed Multi-Objective Framework for EEG Channel Selection in Motor Imagery BCIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29943)
*Dekka Muni Kumar,Dhruba Jyoti Kalita,Yogesh Kumar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多目标优化框架（结合NSGA-II、MOPSO和MOEA/D）的EEG通道选择方法，用于提升运动想象脑机接口性能。该方法平衡了空间相关性和功能可辨别性，在四个数据集上分别达到87%、71%、75%和65%的分类准确率，优于传统方法，并能选出更紧凑、计算复杂度更低的通道子集，适用于可穿戴和实时BCI应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG通道选择方法存在依赖单目标准则和易陷入局部最优的局限性，亟需一种能同时平衡多个目标（如空间相关性与功能可辨别性）的优化方法，以提升MI-BCI的分类性能和计算效率。

Method: 提出多目标优化框架，集成三种算法：非支配排序遗传算法（NSGA-II）、多目标粒子群优化（MOPSO）和基于分解的多目标进化算法（MOEA/D）。框架通过高斯核评估空间相关性，通过任务相关去同步化（ERD）评估通道的功能可辨别性，最终平衡二者进行通道选择。

Result: 在Physionet、OpenBMI、HighGamma和BCIIV-2A四个数据集上的分类准确率分别为87%、71%、75%和65%；选出的通道子集高度集中在感觉运动皮层区域，且通道数量更少、计算复杂度更低，优于传统的单目标和基于固定子集的方法。

Conclusion: 该多目标优化框架能有效提升MI-BCI的性能，同时实现紧凑的通道配置和更低的计算成本，更适合可穿戴、便携和实时BCI应用。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) classification using electroencephalography (EEG) signals is essential for advancing brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Traditional EEG channel selection methods often face limitations, such as dependency on single-objective criteria and susceptibility to local optima. To address these challenges, this work proposes a multi-objective optimisation framework that employs non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm, multiple-objective particle swarm optimisation, and a multi-objective evolutionary algorithm based on decomposition. Our approach effectively balances spatial relevance, using a Gaussian kernel, and functional discriminability, which assesses intratrial task-related desynchronisation, thereby improving performance. We evaluated this framework on four EEG datasets: Physionet, OpenBMI, HighGamma, and BCIIV-2A. The proposed approach successfully identifies compact, relevant channel subsets concentrated around sensorimotor cortex regions linked to MI activity, addressing the prevalent challenges of dimensionality and complexity inherent to traditional techniques. Furthermore, the framework achieved classification performance of 87%, 71%, 75%, and 65% on the Physionet, OpenBMI, HighGamma, and BCIIV-2A datasets, respectively. By outperforming existing single-objective and accuracy-based methods, and those relying on fixed subsets, these findings demonstrate that this new multi-objective optimisation framework can enhance MI-based BCI performance while facilitating compact channel configurations with reduced computational complexity, making them better suited for wearable, portable, and real-time BCI applications.

</details>


### [229] [Improving outdoor navigation for people with blindness using an AI-driven smartphone application and personalized audio guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29120)
*Raymond Liu,Patrick Slade*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍Mobilio，一款结合机器学习、传感器融合和个性化音频反馈的智能手机应用，为视力障碍者提供包含逐向导航、路径引导、障碍物检测与规避的完整户外导航功能。实验表明，与白杖+Google Maps相比，Mobilio+白杖使社区路径导航时间减少13±3%，环境接触减少41±5%，导航可靠性接近人类向导。


<details>
